# Dharmasamuccaya

## 1 jitavargaḥ

bhikṣuṇā avalokitasiṃhena samudbhāvitaḥdharmasamuccayaḥprathamam udānam(jita-dharma-kāyavargāḥ
parivarto hyanityatā |apramādaḥ kāma-tṛṣṇe strī ca madyena te daśa ||)(1) jitavargaḥmaṅgalācaraṇam||om
namo buddhāya ||prahīṇasarvāsrava nirmalaśrīryaḥ kleśajambālanimagnalokam
|kṛpāguṇenodaharatsamena praṇamyate'smai tribhavottamāya

granthakārapratijñāsaddharma smṛtyupasthānasūtra-vaipulyasāgarāt |gāthāḥ samuddhariṣyāmi lokalocanatatparāḥ

mokṣasyāyatanāni ṣaṭapramādastathā śraddhā vīryārambhastathā dhṛtiḥ |jñānābhyāsaḥ saṃtāśleṣo
mokṣasyāyatanāni ṣaṭ

nava śāntisamprāptihetavaḥdānaṃ śīlaṃ damaḥ kṣāntirmaitrībhūteṣvahiṃsatā |karuṇāmuditopekṣā
śāntisamprāptihetavaḥ

narakasyāgrahetavaḥcāpalyaṃ pāpasaṃśleṣaḥ krūratā vitathaṃ vacaḥ |mithyādṛṣṭiḥ pramādaśca narakasyāgrahetavaḥ

preteṣu ṣaṭ udbhavakāraṇānimātsaryamīrṣyā kaṭuvākyatā hi pramādasevā viratiḥ śubhācca |tattveṣvabhidroharataṃ
manaśca preteṣu panthāna ihodbhavanti

tiryagyonau udbhavasādhanāniajñānasevā jaḍa (tā) ca buddheḥ saddharmanāśaḥ priyamaithunatvam
|āhāraśaktiḥ prabalā ca nidrā tiryaggatau ca pravadanti hetum

karmaphalamaśubhaṃ trividhaṃ kṛtvā kāyavāṅmanasāṃ mahat |uttarottarasambaddhaṃ karmaṇā sampradṛśyate

bālānāmapi sambaddhamuttarottarameva tat |śubhañca trividhaṃ kṛtvā sādhavo yānti nirvṛtim

tasmāt karmaphalaṃ matvā pramādasya ca varjanam |śreyase kriyate buddheretat sukhamanuttamam

dānaśīlavato nityaṃ sarvasattvānukampinaḥ |siddhayanti sarvaśastulyāstasmācchīlaparo bhavet

kasya kāryasiddhiḥ bhavati?maitrīkaruṇāyuktasya gatākāṃkṣasya dehinaḥ |parānugrahayuktasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ
sthitā

santuṣṭasyāpramattasya sarvasattvahitaiṣiṇaḥ |rāgadveṣavimuktasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

mitrāmitraprahīṇasya samacittasya dehinaḥ |apāyagatibhinnasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

evaṃ ca tasya dhīrasya dharmadānaratasya ca |mātsaryerṣyāpramuktasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

saṃvarasthasya śāntasya gurupūjāratasya ca |kāryākāryavidhijñasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

aśaṭhasyātidakṣasya priyavākyasya nityaśaḥ |dhyānādhyayanaśaktasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

deśakālavidhijñasya sādhyāsādhyaṃ vijānataḥ |śakyopāyavidhijñasya kāryasiddhirdhruvaṃ sthitā

krodhādivijayasādhanamukhena dharmatāsvarūpanirūpaṇamakrodhena hataḥ krodhaḥ kṣamayā krūratā
jitā |dharmeṇa nirjito'dharmaḥ prabhayā vijitaṃ tamaḥ

satyādeḥ praśaṃsāmṛṣāvākyaṃ satyahataṃ paiśunyaṃ ca samādhinā |pārūṣyaṃ mārdava jitaṃ abaddhaṃ
bandhanā jitam

prāṇātipāto maitryā ca steyaṃ dānaiḥ sadā jitam |ayoniśomanaskāro yonijena sadā jitaḥ

avidyā vidyayā jitā divasena tathā kṣapā |śuklapakṣeṇa ca kṛṣṇapakṣo (hi) sarvaśo jitaḥ

mithyākāmeṣvavijito buddhayā tattvavicārayā |āryāṣṭāṅgena mārgeṇa yonijena sadā jitaḥ

vaiśāradyaiścaturbhiśca kadarya jitameva tat |vismṛtiśca hatā smṛtyā kṣaṇenātmānameva ca

araṇyavāsaniyatairjitā rāgavaśā narāḥ |meruṇā parvatā jitā (vṛkṣeṇa) vanamālikā

samudreṇa jitā sarve tīrthā (hi) jalasambhavāḥ |ādityatejovijitā sarve ca grahatārakāḥ

nityā jitā anityena dāridrayaṃ dānasampadā |śāṭhyaṃ na mārdava jitaṃ satyenānṛtiko hataḥ

bhūtena nirjito'bhūto vahninā tarusampadaḥ |pipāsā vijitā toyairjighitsā bhojanaistathā

vīryapraśaṃsāsiṃhena vijitāḥ sarve mṛgāḥ sattvādhikāśca ye |santāpena mahecchā ca jitā nityaṃ
prakīrtyate

dayā praśasyate nityamadayā naiva śasyate |vīryeṇa nirjitaṃ sarva kausīdyaṃ mohavardhanam

tattvadarśipuruṣapraśaṃsājñānādhikaiḥ sadā dāntaiḥ puruṣaistattvada(rśi)bhiḥ |vijitāḥ krūrakarmāṇo
mithyāvādādhikā narāḥ

buddhena nirjitāḥ sarve tīrthyāḥ kuṭilavādinaḥ |asurā vijitā devairdharmateyaṃ vyavasthitā

||iti jitavargaḥ prathamaḥ ||

## 10 madyajugupsāvargaḥ

10) madyajugupsāvargaḥmadyapānavarṇanasādhanapradarśanammadyapānaṃ na seveta madyaṃ hi viṣamuttamam
|naśyanti kuśalā dharmā madyapānaniṣevaṇāt

yaḥ sevate sadā madyaṃ tasya buddhirasaṃsthitā |balabuddhī na dharmo'sti tasmānmadyaṃ vivarjayet

nāśānāmuttamaṃ nāśaṃ madyamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |tasmānmadyaṃ na seveta (madyaṃ) nāśayate naram

aniṣṭāḥ pāpakā dharmā madyapānaniṣevaṇāt |bhavanti tasmād viṣavanmadyapānaṃ vivarjayet

dhanakṣayaṃ pāpakaraṃ kausīdyakaramuttamam |madyapānasthitā doṣāḥ tasmāt tat parivarjayet

rāgasyoddīpanaṃ madyaṃ krodhasyāpi tathaiva ca |mohasyoddīpakaṃ bhūyastasmānmadyaṃ vivarjayet

madyamūlamanarthasya hāsasya narakasya ca |sarvendriyavināśānāṃ hetubhūtaṃ hyanarthakam

atiharṣābhidhānasya śokasya ca bhayasya ca |vāgdoṣasyā'tidamyasya pāruṣyasyā''spadaṃ hi tat

madyenā''kṣiptamanasaḥ puruṣāḥ paśubhiḥ samāḥ |kāryākārya na vindanti tasmānmadyaṃ vivarjayet

madyakṣipto hi puruṣo jīvannapi mṛto mataḥ |ya icchejjīvitaṃ saukhyaṃ sa madyaṃ varjayet sadā

madyaṃ sarvadoṣāspadamsarvadoṣāspadaṃ madyaṃ sarvānarthakaraṃ sadā |sarvapāpeṣu sopānaṃ tamasāmālayo
mahān

madyena pretaloke narake vā patanammadyena narakaṃ yānti pretalokaṃ tathaiva ca |tiryakṣu yānti
puruṣā madyadoṣeṇa vañcitāḥ

viṣādapi viṣaṃ madyaṃ narakānnarakaṃ tathā |vyādhīnāṃ ca paraṃ vyādhirmadyamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ

madyena hānipradarśanambuddhīndriyavināśāya dharmaratnaṃ kṣayāya ca |yo'tirekaparaṃ madyaṃ brahmacaryabadhāya
ca

madyena laghutāṃ yānti pārthivā śāstracakṣuṣaḥ |kiṃ punaḥ prākṛtā martyā madyapānavilambitāḥ

madyasya vināśakaratvamkuṭhāraḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ hrīvināśakaraṃ param |madyaṃ niṣevitaṃ martyairvināśāyopakalpyate

madyena jñānājñānavivekaśūnyapradarśanamna jñānaṃ nāpi vijñānaṃ na kāryāṇi na ca kriyām |jānīte
puruṣaḥ sarva madyena hṛtacetasā

madyasevanasya paritāpasādhanamakasmāt tapyate janturakasmāt paritapyate |bhavatyakasmāt pāpī
(ca) yo madyamanusevate

madyasya buddhisammohajanakatvambuddhisammohajanakaṃ lokadvayavināśakam |vahniśca mokṣadharmāṇāṃ
madyamekaṃ vyavasthitam

madyasya kimpākatvamabhyāse madhuraṃ madyaṃ vipāke paramaṃ kaṭu |kimpākādapi kimpākaṃ madyamuktaṃ
parīkṣakaiḥ

narakasya sādhanaṃ madyamna madye viśvaseddhīmān naraṃ vakṣyati māmiti |śītasparśa vipākoṣṇaṃ
madyaṃ narakagāmikam

sampattau vyasanaṃ madyaṃ devānāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |yathā yathā sukhā prītistannāśe vyasanaṃ tathā

madyapānamadonmattāḥ sattvā mohavaśānugāḥ |sākṣyamohamayaṃ pānaṃ pibanti rasatṛṣṇayā

madyaṃ mohamayaṃ pāna pītvā kālena coditāḥ |nākāt pratyakṣanarakaṃ tasmāt madyaṃ na saṃspṛśet

darśanātpānācca madyapānaṃ mohajanakamdarśanāt sparśanāt pānāta madyaṃ mohayate naram |tasmāt
sa madyapānaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet

darśanājjāyate lobhaḥ sparśanād gandhasambhavaḥ |gandhād rasābhilāṣaśca rasanādadhamā matiḥ

manīṣiṇaḥ madyasevanātpatantinaikasarvādhamaṃ nyāsaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |nāmarūpaniṣedhāya
yathā madyaṃ niṣevitam

madyapānaphalasūcanamvāgbhrāmayati mastiṣkaṃ cakṣuṣīdhvanireva ca |sambhrāntivimatirmūḍho na
kiñcit pratipadyate

striyo'pi madyapāyinamupahasantistriyo'pyupahasantīmaṃ puruṣaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi |niśceṣṭaṃ kāṣṭhasadṛśaṃ
niścalaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi

madyapānaṃ maraṇādapi nikṛṣṭataramsambhāvitasya maraṇaṃ madyapānaṃ prakīrtyate |hālāhalādabhyadhikaṃ
kārāvāsādhikaṃ ca tat

madyapānavarjanaṃ śreyaskaramādīnavāśca ṣaṭ triṃśanmadyapānādavasthitāḥ |tasmādādīnavo jñeyaḥ
sa hi tad varjayet sadā

madyapānaṃ viduṣo'pi jantoḥ malinīkaraṇāya bhavatiatijātasya viduṣo malinīkaraṇaṃ mahat |kāśapuṣpasamaṃ
jantuṃ kurute laghusattvaram

viṣayānaladagdhaḥ kāryākāryaśūnyo bhavatipramādauhyati magnānāṃ viṣayairapahṛṣyate |madyapānena
bhūyaśca manovyāmohakāriṇā

viṣayānaladagdhasya kāryākāryamajānataḥ |vanopavanalabdhasya madyapānasya kiṃ punaḥ

madyapānaṃ mohāya pāpāya jāyaterasena śobhanaṃ madyaṃ pariṇāmena dāruṇam |pariṇāmaphalaṃ pāpaṃ
narakeṣūpapadyate

pītaṃ janayate mohaṃ mohāt pāpeṣu rakṣyate |saṃraktahṛdayo bālo narakānupadhāvati

madyapānamadhamatvasādhanamprakarṣa janayatyādau vipāke dainyamuttamam |tṛṭ chedaṃ kurute cāsau
paścāddāhaṃ sudāruṇam

tad buddhiṃ nāśayatyādau paścānnāśayate sukham |tasmāt sa puruṣo dhīro yo madyaṃ nānusevate

madyaniṣeviṇo vihagasadṛśā bhavantivihagaiḥ sadṛśaṃ yānti puruṣā madyasevinaḥ |tulyaṃ vyāmohajanakaṃ
madyaṃ mohamahāviṣam

vimohitā durgatimadhigacchantiyairmadyaṃ viṣavad dṛṣṭaṃ tairdṛṣṭaṃ padamuttamam |yaistu tad
virasaṃ pītaṃ pītaṃ tāmra (ka) lohitam

niṣpratīkārakarmāṇi yaḥ karoti vimohitaḥ |madyapānasamāviṣṭaḥ so'nte gacchati durgatim

madyapānasevināṃ nāśo bhavatiekatra sarvapāpāni madyapāna (niṣevaṇam) |yasmānnāśayate (madyaṃ)
cittamūlaśca saṃvaraḥ

madyapāyī naṣṭadharmo bhavatinaikāṅgitā hi cittasya na dharmāṇāṃ vicāraṇā |yaḥ pā(pa)nirato
bhikṣurbhavenmadyaniṣevaṇāt

madyenākṣiptamanaso kuśalasya ca ghātakaḥ |naṣṭadharmasya sattvasya nāyaṃ loko na cāparaḥ

īryyāpathaṃ na jānāti na kālaṃ nāpi deśanām |saddharmato viruddhaśca tucchaṃ kimapi bhāṣate

svayaṃ tāvanna jānāti kimidaṃ kathyate mayā |vākpāruṣyaṃ kathaṃ cānyaṃ parijñāsyatyaśobhanam

lāghavaṃ yāti lokasya dharmācca parihīyate |nidhana puruṣairdṛṣṭaṃ madyajvalanasevanāt

madyapānaṃ kutsāṃ sampādayatināśo bhavatyatīte hi vartamāne suhṛjjane |anāgate kutsitānāṃ madyaṃ
traikālyanāśakam

madyaṃ dharmapradūṣakamevanāmarūpavināśāya cittanāśāya dehinām |utpannadoṣajanakaṃ madyaṃ dharmapradūṣakam

madyavarjanaṃ dharmāya pānañca mṛtyurna bhavatisamāhitā dharmaśīlāḥ puruṣā madyavarjakāḥ |te
yānti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra mṛtyurna vidyate

||iti madyajugupsāvargo daśamaḥ ||

## 11 cittavargaḥ

atha dvitīyam udānam(cittañca vāk tathā karma saṃyojanantu pāpakam |naraka-preta-tiryak-kṣutkausīdyāni
vidurdaśa || )(11) cittavargaḥcittaṃ rājavat pravartateagādhaṃ viṣamaṃ tīvraṃ sarvasattvagataṃ
mahat |cittaṃ sarvasya jagato rājavat sampravartate

cittadhāriṇastajjñāḥ paramāṃ gatiṃ prāpnuvantiadṛśyaṃ samprati bhayaṃ karma nārakacañcalāḥ |ye
cittadhāriṇastajjñāste gatāḥ paramāṃ gatim

etannayati vyutthānametannayati durgatim |tadevādyantamamalaṃ nirvāṇamadhigacchati

manaḥ pūrvamaṅgamā dharmā manaḥ śreṣṭhā manomayāḥ |manasā suprasannena bhāṣate vā karoti vā

cittavaśagasya cittamanudhāvatiyo na cittasya vaśagaścittaṃ tasyāntagaṃ sadā |sa nirṇāśayati
kleśān tamaḥ sūryodayo yathā

cittadagdhāḥ nāśaṃ yānticittaṃ śatruḥ paraṃ śatrurna śatruraparaḥ smṛtaḥ |cittadagdhāḥ sadā
sattvāḥ kāladagdhā yathā jaḍāḥ

yaścittavaśamāpanno bālo mūḍho'jitendriyaḥ |tena duḥkhe samo nāsti nirvāṇaṃ tasya dūrataḥ

cittavaśagaḥ naraḥ narakamadhigacchatiāvarjyaḥ śatruraparo na cittāni (nigūhate) |eṣa badhnāti
puruṣo nṛpateryamaśāsane

viṣayeṣu ratā nityaṃ na sa dharmaḥ kathañcana |saddharmapathasammūḍho narakaṃ nayate mahat

durdamānāṃ paraṃ cittamanīnāmagniruttamaḥ |durdāntaṃ śīghragantṛ ca narakaṃ nayate dhruvam

ye cittasya vaśaṃ yātā yātāste narakaṃ purā |yaistu tad vāryate śīghraṃ na te duḥkhānugāminaḥ

cittānurūpaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhañca bhavatiyathā yathā (nṛṇāṃ) cittaṃ pariṇāmastathā tathā |kalpānāṃ
śubhakarttuśca pāpakarttuśca pāpakam

cittāyattaṃ bhavet karma karmāyattaṃ mano bhavet |cittakarma samutthāya saṃsāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ

duṣṭena cetasā karma yaḥ karoti pumāniha |pacyate-narake tena jālinyā sa viḍambitaḥ

cittakarma evaṃ saṃsāraḥcittādhīnaṃ bhavet pāpaṃ saṃsāraḥ parikīrtitaḥ |cittapratyayajaṃ hyetat
hetupratyayasambhavam

cittena vañcitāḥ sattvā pāpasya vaśamāgatāḥ |gacchanti narakaṃ pāpāt kāraṇāgramahābhayā(t)

na bhavet cittavaśago'pi tu dharmavaśo bhavetna gacchet cittavaśatāṃ gacched dharmavaśe sadā
|dharmacārī sukhī nityaṃ pāpacārī na śarmabhāk

cittāyattaṃ sarva phalamcittāyattā kriyā sarvā cittāyattaṃ phalaṃ smṛtam |vicittaṃ hi phalaṃ
cittaṃ tathā phalamadhismṛtam

cittena cintitaṃ sarva lokaścittabhavānugaḥ |na hi tad vidyate sthānaṃ yanna cittavaśānugam

śubhasya nirvāṇasādhanatvammokṣa bandhanayormūlaṃ hetubhūtaṃ paraṃ matam |śubhena mucyate janturaśubhenāśu
badhyate

cittavaśagāḥ kadāpi nirvāṇaṃ nādhigacchantijālinyā mohitāḥ sattvāścittasya vaśamāgatāḥ |nirvāṇaṃ
nāpi gacchanti jātyandhā iva satpatham

pañcendriyāṇi jitavān pāpaṃ bhuṅkte na karhicit |ekacittaṃ tathā karma kurute vividhe bhave

pañcaraṅgojjvalaṃ cittaṃ yathā dṛśyaṃ praśasyate |pañcendriyavicitraṃ hi tathā karma bhave bhavet

cittakarttā'nekadhā bhavaticittakartā hi puruṣo dṛśyairbhavati naikadhā |cittaṃ cittakaraṃ śūnyaṃ
sarvathā naiva dṛśyate

cittaṃ śobhanamaśobhanaṃ ca karma karotiśobhanā'śobhanaṃ citraṃ yathā bhittiḥ kṛtistathā |śobhanā'śobhanaṃ
karma tathā cittaṃ karoti hi

cittānugaṃ karmadivārātrau yathā cittaṃ sthitaṃ bhavati citragam |divārātrau tathā karma bhave
samanuvartate

akṛtvā sukṛtaṃ karma cittavṛttyanugā narāḥ |patanti vivaśāḥ pāpe cittāriparivañcitāḥ

karmacittakaro hyeṣa yatra yatra niyujyate |caracittena mahatā taccittaṃ ca karotyayam

pariṇāmaviśeṣeṇa yatra yatra niyokṣyate |preṣyante tatra cittena tridhā tu gaticāriṇaḥ

cittasya damanaṃ sukhāvahamcittādhināni vartante sarvakarmāṇi dehinām |tasmāccittaṃ sadā dāntaṃ
nayate padamacyutam

durviṣahyasya laghunā yatra yatra nipātinaḥ |cittasya damanaṃ sādhu cittaṃ dāntaṃ sukhāvaham

yena cittaṃ sadā dāntaṃ tena doṣāḥ sadā jitāḥ |jitadoṣasya dhīrasya duḥkhaṃ naiva prapadyate

cittasya laghuceṣṭitameva duḥkhamyacca svakaṃ bhaved duḥkhaṃ yacca duḥkhaṃ paratra ca |tat sarvamaviṣahyasya
cittasya laghuceṣṭitam

cittameva sarveṣāṃ prabhuḥsasurāsuranāgānāṃ piśācoragarakṣasām |prabhurekaḥ paraṃ cittaṃ rājā(hi)tribhavasya
tat

cittabhrāntaduḥkhamaśnutecittaṃ nayati deveṣu cittañca narabhūmiṣu |cittaṃ nayatyapāye(ṣu) cittaṃ
bhrāmayati prajāḥ

cittād bhrāntasya naṣṭasya viṣayairmohitasya ca |tṛṣṇayā dahyamānasya sthitaṃ duḥkhamanuttamam

cittasya damanāt sukhānubhūtirbhavatiekacāri sadāmūḍhaṃ durviṣahyaṃ mahābalam |sampradāri sadādṛśyaṃ
capalaṃ śīghragāminam

evaṃ vidhaṃ hi ye cittaṃ damayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |te mārabandhanātītāḥ pāraṃ prāptāḥ sukhodayam

capalaṃ cittaṃ narakāyopakalpyatesaṅkalpakuṭilaṃ tīvramagādhaṃ capalaṃ hi tat |cittaṃ tamaḥśritaṃ
śubhraṃ narakāyopakalpyate

doṣanirmukto'cyutaṃ padaṃ prāpnotitadevaṃ doṣanirmuktaṃ nendriyārthavaśānugam |aliptaṃ pāpakairdharmairnayate
padamacyutam

śuddhaṃ cittaṃ śuddhe karmaṇi pravartatehetupratyayajaṃ cittaṃ yogavāhi paraṃ ca tat |pariṇāmavaśācchīghraṃ
tathā tat sampravartate

cittānadhīnatve karma cañcalaṃ bhavatiekaṃ karoti karmāṇi vividhāni calaṃ ca tat |ekānekaṃ caraṃ
sūkṣmaṃ kṣaṇādūrdhva na tiṣṭhati

saṃsārasya māyopamatvapratipādanamdurvijñeyaḥ sadā tasya niḥśarīrasya sarvadā |ko'sau nayati
lokāntamatha kena ca gacchati

gataśca tiṣṭhati kutra śarīrakarmakārakam |dṛśyāni tasya karmāṇi sañcayo na ca dṛśyate

damanaṃ duṣkaraṃ tasya yasya bhūtirna vidyate |sarvasattvagataṃ ghoraṃ laghukāri ca cākṣuṣam

pāpāt pāpataraṃ dṛṣṭaṃ māyopamamidaṃ bhavet |puṇyāt puṇyataraṃ dṛṣṭaṃ samyagmārgasamāśritam

māyopamatvasamarthanamna gatirjñāyate tasya gamanaṃ naiva dṛśyate |nīyate ca kṣataṃ sarva jātyuttarasamāni
ca |

śastreṇa chidyate nedaṃ jvalanena ca dahayate |chidyate dahyate caiva janaḥ sarvamacakṣuṣā

dṛḍhaṃ yat karmarajjvādi naraṃ badhnāti duḥkhitam |jātyantarasahastrāṇi nayate na ca dṛśyate

dāntameva cittaṃ sukhāvahammuhūrtena śubhaṃ bhūtaṃ muhūrtenāśubhaṃ ca tat |śubhāśubhābhyāṃ racitaṃ(cittaṃ)
dāntaṃ sukhāvaham

ṣaḍ bhirdvāraiḥ prasūteṣu viṣayeṣvatilolupam |cittaṃ nayati lokāntaṃ vyasanaṃ na ca budhyate

viprasannacittasya śreyaskaratve'nuttamaṃ sukhaṃ bhavativiprasannaṃ yathā toyaṃ viprasannaṃ
yathā nabhaḥ |viprasannaṃ tathā cittaṃ nīyate sukhamuttamam

janāḥ sātiśayā (dṛṣṭā) vitarkaviṣayodbhavāt |pauravāścittarājñaste bhavanti sahacāriṇaḥ

cañcalaṃ manaḥ saṃsāre gatiṃ kārayatidhāvati yatra ca manastatra dhāvantyajñāninaḥ |parasparabalānnī
(tā) bhramanti tribhavārṇa(va)m

viśeṣacittenottamā gatirbhavaticittavaiśadya bhāvānāṃ viśeṣaḥ karmaṇāstathā |na karmaguṇahīnasya
viśeṣa upalabhyate

susamāhitacittasya praśaṃsāsusamāhitacittaḥ sa nityaṃ dharmānudarśakaḥ |na doṣavaśamāyāti yathā
divyastamonudaḥ

gṛhasthasya samyagdṛṣṭitve nirvāṇaprāptirbhavatisamyagdṛṣṭeścādhimātrā yasya cetasi vartate
|gṛhastho'pi sa me jñeyo muktaḥ saṃsārabandhanāt

vijñā devalokaṃ gacchantikalyāṇanirmalaṃ yacca tato doṣeṇa bādhyate |vijño (na) malamāpnoti
devaloke sa jāyate

vinītacittāḥ sukhagāmino bhavantiviṣayadvāracapalaṃ nadīkuṭilagāminam |yairvinītamidaṃ cittaṃ
te surāḥ śubhabhāginaḥ

adharmamārgaḥ kalyāṇāya na bhavatiadharmapathamāśritya janāḥ pāpavaśānugāḥ |ciraṃ bhramanti
saṃsāre cittena parikheditāḥ

cittaṃ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe parivartatekṣaṇe kṣaṇe cittamidaṃ naikaśaḥ parivartate |laghusvabhāvacapalaṃ
māyāgandharvasannibham

cañcalacitto baddho bhavatitasya bandhanameveṣṭaṃ jñānavijñānasammatam |dūravṛttirmahāvegaḥ
paridhāvatyanekadhā

dhṛtyā cittadhāriṇaḥ sukhamaśnutehriyamāṇaṃ sadā cittamindriyārthe durāsadam |sandhārayati yo
dhṛtyā sa dhīraḥ pāragāmikaḥ

dhṛtimajñānamohaṃ ca viṣayottānamānasam |sandhārayati yo dhīmān sa loke paṇḍito naraḥ

śubhacittaṃ kāmāvarodhakaṃ bhavatiyo yathā kurute cittaṃ tathā kāmān sa paśyati |śubhena kāmaśamanaṃ
nāśubhena pravardhanam

apraśāntamatirduḥkhamāpnotiśāntacittaṃmanāḥ kāmānasyatīha viṣāstravat |apraśāntamatiḥ sarpamaṇivat
tān sa paśyati

cittavaśago na śubhāṃ gatimāpnotiindriyāṇīndriyārthāśca cittādhīnā bhavantyamī |citta saṃśleṣayogena
pariṇāmaḥ pravartate

dhīra-praśaṃsātasyaiva vyavadhānena dhīraśca pratibudhyate |rūpasāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ pariṇāmaḥ kathaṃ
pṛthak

cittasvarūpa-nirūpaṇamyathā rūpaṃ tathā sarva viṣayāścittahetavaḥ |cittaṃ dāntaṃ sadā śāntaṃ
doṣamiśravigarhitam

ekamekaṃ yathā śāli gandharūpe pṛthagvidhe |saṃśleṣo yāti nānātvaṃ tathācittaṃ pravartate

kriyāṃ hi tatra kurute yatra (cittaṃ) pradhāvati |cittasaṃvāhanaṃ tadvākyaṃ loko'yaṃ sampravartate

viṣayād bhrāntamanasaś(cā)tivego mahābalaḥ |cittaṃ(śānta)karaṃ dāntaṃ śivaṃ bhavati dehinām

dāntena cetasā tattvadarśanaṃ bhavatitenādāntena śīghreṇa sarvapāpavihāriṇā |bhrāmyate tribhavaḥ
sarvaḥ na ca tattvaṃ sa paśyati

samīraṇaraṇodbhrānto lokaḥ saṃsāre bhramatisamīraṇaraṇodbhrānto yathā bhramati sāgaraḥ |citteśvarasamāyukto
loko'yaṃ bhrāmyate sadā

cittaṃ śubhaṃ sukhamedhītaśubhopanītaṃ cittaṃ(hi) śubheṣvevāpanīyate |tathā śubhānucaritamaśnute
teṣu dhāvati

yogavāhicittaṃ pratyayaṃ labhateyogavāhi nṛṇāṃ cittaṃ vāyvambusamameva ca |pratyayaśca yathā
labhyastathā tat paridhāvati

cittaṃ pradhāvati parivartate caśīghraṃ pradhāvate cittaṃ śīghrañca parivartate |śīghraṃ nayati
deveśa (bhuvane) dāyabhūmiṣu

cittasya karttṛtvamkarttṛtvaṃ sarvadharmāṇāṃ bharttṛtvaṃ sarvakarmaṇām |netṛtvaṃ sarvakarmāṇāṃ
cittatvamiti lakṣyate

cittaṃ na viśvasetna viśvaseddhi cittasya nityaṃ chidraprahāriṇaḥ |durlabhasyāpi vaśyasya capalasya
viśeṣataḥ

cittagativarṇanammuhūrtena śubhaṃ yāti muhūrtena tathā'śubham |bhavatyavyākṛtaṃ śīghraṃ gatirasya
na vidyate

nāyatirjñāyate tasya gamanaṃ naiva vidyate |abhūtaḥ sambhavastasya bhūtvā ca pratigacchati

rūparahitaṃ cittamnākāro vidyate tasya sañcayo'pi na vidyate |grahaṇaṃ tasya naivāsti niḥśarīrasya
sarvataḥ

pratyayasādhanavarṇanamhetupratyayasāmagrayā prāptaḥ sañjāyate punaḥ |maṇi-somapratyayo (hi)
pratyayo jñāyate'nalaḥ

cittamindriyarūpādīn prati dhāvatitathaivendriyarūpādīn prativijñānasambhavaḥ |naikasya jñāyate
cittaṃ samavāyyanulakṣyate

cittaṃ durnivāryamtadevaṃ viṣayaṃ matvā durnivārya ca sarvataḥ |saddharmamatirāstheyā na kāmeṣu
kathañcana

cittamati capalamcaṇḍāticapalaṃ tīvramaviṣahyaṃ mahāvalam |cittaṃ karoti karmāṇi yanna paśyanti
bāliśāḥ

duḥkhapravartakaṃ cittamsarvasya karmaṇaścittaṃ hetubhūtaṃ bhave bhave |bhavet pravartakaṃ duḥkhaṃ
yena dhāvati bāliśaḥ

cittadoṣo mahādoṣa evavātādayo na doṣāḥ syurdoṣā rāgādayo matāḥ |vātādibhirapāyeṣu mānavo nopapadyate

cittadoṣo mahādoṣo nityaṃ(pāpa) vidarśakaḥ |tasmātteṣāṃ samāśreyo na vātādigamādiha

rāgānugaṃ cittaṃ duṣṭaṃ bhavativātādayaḥ praṇaśyanti dehanāśāccharīriṇām |(rāgādayo) na tasya
syurjanmāntaraśatairapi

tasmād rāgasya vaśagaṃ na vidheyaṃ kadācana |ābhyantikaṃ paraṃ śreyaḥ prāpyate rāgasaṅkramāt

mana eva cikitsako bhavatiyaścittavaidyaḥ sa bhiṣag na śarīrabhiṣag bhṛśam |manaścikitsako jñeyo
na tathā laukiko mataḥ

svacittakarmaṇā dagdho narake patatikarmacintyaṃ samaṃ cintyaṃ na cittena prapadyate |svacittakarmaṇā
dagdho narakānupadhāvati

dhyānādeva cittaṃ śuddhaṃ bhavatiyacchreyaḥ samacittasya na tat (kvāpyupa)padyate |saṃrakṣyaṃ
hṛdayaṃ mūḍhaḥ prayātamanudhāvati

cittavaśago duḥkhamāpnotidhyānād dhyeyena kūṭena yaḥ pumān vanamāśritaḥ |taṃ muktvā cittakaḥ
so'yaṃ saṃprahṛṣyati bāliśaḥ

ye bālāścittapāśena cittakarmavicāriṇaḥ |nīyante hyavaśā ghoraṃ narakaṃ karmamohitā

cittavaividhyanirūpaṇamsucittamapi yaccitaṃ na taccittaṃ satāṃ matam |karmacittaṃ mahācittaṃ
tridhātugatacitritam

sattvā citrairupāyairhi bhramanti gatipañcake |tat sarva karmasācivyaṃ citrakartṛ vicitritam

cittavaśago baddho bhavaticittacitrakareṇedaṃ karmajātaṃ vicitritam |yena sarvamidaṃ baddhaṃ
jagad bhramati mohitam

varṣātaparajodhūmaiścittaṃ naśyati bhūbhujaiḥ |kalpakoṭisahastreṇa citraṃ cittaṃ vinaśyati

nāśaṃ prayāti basudhā sāgaraścāpi śuṣyati |cittenāpi kṛtaṃ citraṃ tasyānubhavane sthite

pramatte citte nānāgatirbhavatinānāgatisamāvṛttā nānākarmasamānugāḥ |nānācittavaśāḥ sattvā bhramanti
gatipañcake

bāliśā (nāṃ)nṛṇāṃ cittaṃ viṣayaṃ(prati)dhāvati |na cinotyaśubhaṃ karma yena dhāvati durgatim

cañcalaṃ cittaṃ sadā viṣamaṃ bhavatitasmāccittaṃ sadā rakṣyaṃ cañcalaṃ viṣamaṃ kharam |nityaṃ
viṣayasaṃsaktaṃ tṛṣṇāviṣasamāvṛttam

viṣayānuraktaṃ cittaṃ vyasane pātayatidhāvate viṣayaṃ caitad vyasanaṃ nāvabudhyate |paścāttu
vyasane prāpte phalaṃ vindati karmaṇaḥ

samāhitacittaḥ sukhamaśnuteapāyabhīrutā tasya tasya cittaṃ samāhitam |samāhitena cittena sukhāt
sukhamavāpnute

nirjitacittaḥ sukhamāpnotiviṣamaṃ capalaṃ tīvramaviṣahyaṃ mahābalam |yaiścittaṃ nirjitaṃ dhīraiste
loke sukhino matāḥ

||iti cittavarga ekādaśaḥ ||

## 12 vāgvargaḥ

(12) vāgvargaḥnirvāṇābhilāṣukaḥ pāruṣyaṃ varjayetpāruṣyaṃ varjayet dhīmān samyagvāg bhīrato
bhavet |mādhuryābhirato jarntunirvāṇasyāntike sthitaḥ

vācaṃ paśyan sadābhāṣenmalināṃ ca vivarjayet |vāṅmanenā'bhibhūtā ye te yānti narakaṃ narāḥ

ekadharmavyatītasya mṛṣāvādasya dehinaḥ |pratīrṇapratilokasya nākārya pāpamastiha

mṛṣāvāg na prayoktavyāmṛṣāvācaṃ na bhāṣeta sarvā pratyayakārikām |yathā badhyati saṃsāre sugatiṃ
naiva paśyati

sādhupratyayatenīhā sarvavidveṣakārikā |kāntāraḥ sarvādharmāṇāṃ mṛṣāvāca pravartate

jātamātrasya martyasya kaṭhārī jāyate mukhe |paścāt chinattyātmānaṃ vācā durbhāṣitaṃ vadan

sarvā kāryapatākā sā sarvapāpaprasūtikā |tamasāṃ yonirekā sā yāṃ vācaṃ bhāṣate mṛṣā

satyena hīnāḥ puruṣāḥ sarvasādhuvivarjitāḥ |tṛṇavad yānti loke'smin pratyapāyeṣu duḥkhitāḥ

na vadedanṛtaṃ dhīraḥ kaṣṭā hyanṛtavāditā |pūtigandhyasukhī cāpi paścāttāpena tapyate

satyamahimāsatyaṃ ca na vivarjeta tasya dharmo na vidyate |vinivartitadharmasya sthitaṃ duḥkhamanekajam

satyaṃ sarvadharmāṇāṃ pradīpabhūtampradīpaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ sādhūnāṃ ratnavat priyam |svargasya
ca paraṃ vartma satyamuktaṃ gatajvaraiḥ

satyaṃ na hi divaṃ yāti mokṣasyā'satyavādinaḥ |satyahīnā hi puruṣāḥ paśutulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

jaghanyānāṃ jaghānyāste yeṣāṃ na vidyate |satyaṃ dharmasya sopānaṃ jyotiṣāmākaro mahān

panthāśca mokṣadharmāṇāṃ dhanānāṃ dhanamuttamam |pāpakaiśca (pari)trāṇāṃ satyamuktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ

satyabhūṣitaṃ vāg bhūṣaṇasyāpi bhūṣaṇamjyotiṣāṃ paramaṃ jyotiścakṣuścakṣuṣmatāmapi |draviṇena
vinā satyaṃ bhūṣaṇasyāpi bhūṣaṇam

nidhānamatulaṃ satyamahārya (sarvasādhakam) |gacchanti (satyā yiṇaḥ) puruṣāḥ paramāṃ gatim

na tathā bhāsate rājā nānālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ |satyena bhūṣito dhīmān śobhate devavad yathā

na mātā na pitā cātha mitrāṇi na ca bāndhavāḥ |trāṇamevaṃ yathā śrutvā tasmāt satyaparo bhavet

anṛtanindāvahnīnāṃ paramo vahnirviṣāṇāṃ paramaṃ viṣam |durgatīnāṃ ca sopānamanṛtaṃ parikīrtitam

viṣāgnitulyasaṃsparśa vartayedanṛtaṃ pumān |anṛtena hi yo deva dagdhaityabhidhīyate

satyāsatyayoḥ parasparavirodhitvamtasmāt sarvābhisāreṇa (na vadeda) nṛtaṃ pumān |sarva bhayādikaṃ
kaṣṭamanṛtaṃ kīrtitaṃ budhaiḥ

vahnīnāṃ paramo vahnirnirdahedapi sāgaram |kiṃ punaryo mṛṣāvādī kāṣṭhaloṣṭhasamākṛtiḥ

satyaṃ tyaktvā mṛṣāvādaṃ yo naraḥ pratipadyate |ratnaṃ tyaktvā sa pāpātmā pāṣāṇaṃ pratipadyate

yasyātmā na bhavet dviṣṭo yasya vā narakaṃ priyam |mṛṣāvādaṃ svadehāgnim abuddhiḥ sa niṣevate

sadedaṃ satyavacanaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ sarvadehinām |satyaṃ tyaktvā mṛṣāvāde kasmād yānti kubuddhayaḥ

satyaṃ guṇānāmagryaṃ vai doṣāṇāmanṛtaṃ smṛtam |guṇāṃstyaktvā kathaṃ mūḍho doṣeṣu paridhāvati

bījaṃ sarvasya duḥkhasya mṛṣāvādaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |tathā satyaṃ sukhasyaiva tasmānnānṛtako bhavet

satyavādī hi puruṣaḥ priyaḥ sarvasya dehinaḥ |cakṣurdoṣaimṛṣāvādī tasmānnānṛtako bhavet

devakalpāḥ sadā kṛṣṭāḥ puruṣāḥ satyavādinaḥ |nārakeyāstathā mūḍhā janā hyanṛtacetasaḥ

kalyāṇānāṃ paraṃ satyaṃ doṣāṇāmanṛtaṃ tathā |doṣavarjī guṇadveṣī puruṣaḥ puruṣottamaḥ

sukhodayaṃ sukhodarkaṃ sukhena paripacyate |sukhena labhate satyaṃ(satyaṃ) nirvāṇagāmikam

duḥkhodayaṃ kaṭuphalaṃ duḥkhena saha pacyate |anṛtaṃ sarvaduḥkhāntaṃ kaḥ pumān na vivarjayet

nānyadeśāgataṃ satyaṃ nānyasmāt prārthyate hi tat |sarvatīrthottamaṃ satyaṃ na tīrthasalilāvṛtam

dīpānāṃ ca paro dīpaḥ satyaṃ buddhena deśitam |auṣadhānāṃ paraṃ tacca sadā duḥkhaniṣūdanam

amṛtañca viṣañcaiva jihvāpāśe sthitaṃ nṛṇām |amṛtaṃ satyamityuktaṃ viṣaṃ tūktaṃ mṛṣāvacaḥ

yasyānṛtamabhipretaṃ tasya satyaṃ dhruvaṃ sthitam |viṣantu yasyābhimataṃ tasyeṣṭaṃ syānmṛṣāvacaḥ

viṣeṇa niścitaṃ nāśo mṛṣāvādena niścitaḥ |mṛtavat sa pumānasti yo mithyātvabhibhāṣate

nātmano hi hitaṃ pathyaṃ ( para)sya na kathañcana |paścāttu paraduḥkhāya tat kathaṃ sevyate
nṛbhiḥ

deśe deśe mayā dṛṣṭaṃ janmamṛtyusahasrakam |paraḥ sahastraṃ janayennāśayedanṛtaṃ vacaḥ

abhijātasa niṣkarṣo dvijātīnāṃ ca bhūṣaṇam |darśanaṃ mokṣamārgasya satyamityabhidhīyate

tṛṣṇānadī hayamārāṇāṃ satāṃ satyena karmaṇām |pūrvavat satyamityuktaṃ paraṃ sugatadeśikam

anādinidhane loke tṛṣṇāpāraṅgatā (narāḥ) |nāstyatrāṇaṃ yathā satyamiti dharmavido viduḥ

abhidhyāpāśo na sevanīyaḥsatyavadhyā sadā kleśā vajravadhyā yathā nagāḥ |hatā(ste) puruṣā (jñeyā
ye ') bhidhyā parivañcitāḥ

paracittasamenedaṃ rūpādyaiḥ parivañcyate |abhidhyāmānasaṃ pāśaṃ sevitaṃ na vicakṣaṇaiḥ

dahyate'vikṛta ātmā tailadīptirivānalaḥ |abhidhyādagdhamanasaḥ kāraṇaṃ nopalabhyate

vyāpādaḥ sattvān mohayatyevayasmāt tat sarvadā varjyaṃ kaṭukāśāviṣodayam |vyāpādamohitāḥ sattvā
nityaṃ tadgatamānasāḥ

na śāntimadhigacchanti sarpā iva bileśayāḥ |atha kūrasvabhāvā ye vyāpādaparamā narāḥ

na teṣāṃ vidyate saukhyamādityena yathā tamaḥ |na dharme nārthanikaro na dharmā na ca bāndhavāḥ

rakṣanti puruṣān sarvān vyāpādāhatacetasaḥ |tamasāmākaro hyeṣa vyāpāda iha kathyate

vyāpādayati janakaṃ vyāpādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |avyāpādaḥ paraṃ śreyo na vyāpādaḥ kathañcana

avyāpādaparā ye tu te yānti padamacyutam |niyatātathavādī yo'dharmavādī na dhārmikaḥ

sa cauraḥ sarvalokasya na cauraḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ |dharmavādī naro yastu carate dharmameva yaḥ

sa yāti śāśvataṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocyate |mithyāvacohata (pumān) laukikaḥ samprakīrtyate

lokottaraiḥ kalpaśataiḥ kadācit kathyate na vā |tasmāt lokottaraṃ vākyaṃ laukikaṃ na kathañcana

saṃsārabandhanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ laukikaṃ viṣavad vacaḥ |tṛṣṇārataḥ sadā puṇyānmucyateti suniścitam

lokottaro (naro) dhanyo vidvadbhiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |hitaṃ tattvaṃ ca yo mūḍho na gṛhṇāti gurorvacaḥ

satyavādī sadā devatābhiḥ puraskṛtaḥsa paścād vyasane prāpte tapyate svena cetasā |satyavādī
sadā dānto devatābhiḥ puraskṛtaḥ

priyo bhavati lokasya paścād deveṣu modati |priyo bhavati lokasya paścād svāntena(vardhate )
|darśanīyaṃ mukhaṃ cāsya devalokeṣu jāyate

abhūtavādī puruṣaḥ sarvasattvopapātakaḥ |tamonicayasaṅkāśo jīvannapi mṛtaḥ samaḥ

kathaṃ na jihvā patitā vākyasaṃstutikattṛkā |mṛṣā vadati yo vācaṃ sadbhūtaguṇanāśikām

mukhe sannihito byālo mukhe sannihito'phalaḥ |(mukhe)sañjvalito vahniryo vācā bhāṣate mṛṣā

jihvā saṅgrathitaiḥ pāśaiḥ narakasyāgrahetukaiḥ |chettā ca dharmahetūnāṃ mṛṣāvādaḥ pravartate

na tasya mātā na pitā buddho nāpi samvaraḥ |yaḥ pāpabuddhiḥ patito mṛṣāvādaṃ prabhāṣateḥ

akasmāllaghutāṃ yāti ṣaḍbhiśca parivartate |mucyate devatābhiśca yo vācaṃ bhāṣate mṛṣā

asaṅgṛhītavākyasyā'vicārya krodhanasya ca |capalasyā'lpabhāgyasya sthitaṃ duḥkhamanantakam

parasya duḥkhaṃ dṛṣṭvāpi sattvaḥ sattvopapātakaḥ |paravyasanavat tajjñaḥ pumān narakagāmikaḥ

ye yathāvādino marttyā na ca tatkāriṇaḥ sadā

satyameva svargasya nirvāṇasya ca sopānamsatyaṃ svargasya sopānaṃ nirvāṇadvārameva tat |tasmāt
satyaparo nityaṃ nityaṃ dharmagatiḥ smṛtiḥ

anṛtaṃ na vadetaśokamajaraṃ sthānaṃ prayāti puruṣottamaḥ |varjayedanṛtaṃ dhīmān ninditaṃ tattvadarśibhiḥ

||iti vāgvargo dvādaśaḥ ||

## 13 karmavargaḥ

(13) karmavargaḥśubhāśubhakarmaṇāṃ phalabhogaḥśubhānāmaśubhānāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ phalaniścayaḥ |bhujyate
sukṛtaṃ sarva karmabaddhā hi dehinaḥ

(yadaṅgī)kriyate karma tatkṛdbhiranubhūyate |cittamānena mūḍhena tṛṣṇānagaravāsinā

svayameva phalaṃ bhuṅktesahāyairbahubhiḥ sārdha kurute karma duṣkṛtam |ekākī karmaṇastasya phalaṃ
bhuṅkte bhave bhave

karmaṇāmavisaṃyogaḥ sarvaiḥ svajanabāndhavaiḥ |śubhāśubhaṃ paraṃ loke gacchantamanugacchati

yatra prayānti puṇyāni gandhastatrānudhāvati |tathā śubhāśubhaṃ karma gacchantamanugacchati
|svakarmaphaladāyādā prāṇena karmayojinaḥ

sukṛtaiḥ suralokaṃ gacchantisukṛtaiḥ suralokeṣu duṣkṛtaiśca tathāpyadhaḥ |yadduḥkhaṃ karmaphalajaṃ
jāyate kaṭukodayam

tasyopamānamasuraṃ triṣu dhātuṣu jāyate |tridoṣajaṃ tricittotthaṃ triṣu dhātuṣu pacyate

tasya karmavipākasya triṣu dhātuṣu lakṣyate |hetupratyayasāmagrīsamutthaṃ ca prameva tat

anyakṛtakarmaṇaḥ phalaṃ nānyo bhuṅktena hyanyena kṛtaṃ pāpamanyena paripacyate |sukarmaphaladāyādaḥ
prāṇināṃ sarva eva hi

pūrvoktānāṃ trividhānāṃ svasya kṛta eva vipāko bhavatikarmaṇastrividhasyāsya na cāpyanyasya
pacyate |catvāriṃśadvipākasya ghoraṃ bhavati ceṣṭitam

karmaphalavarṇanamekaḥ karoti karmāṇi ekaśca phalamaśnute |ekastarati durgāṇi sahāyo jāyate'paraḥ

jalāpekṣī jano yastu kurute karma duṣkṛtam |na jano janaśatānāṃ bhuṅkte hi vyañjanaṃ (kvacit)

na hyanyena kṛtaṃ karma saṅkrāmatyaparasya tat |na manye'nādinidhanamasmiṃlloke na cāparaiḥ

duḥkhāsvādaṃ sukhodbhūtaṃ yena duścaritaṃ kṛtam |tena carati saṃsāre prerite karmavāyunā

kasya dharmo vardhate?anapekṣitatattvasya vyākulīkṛtacetasaḥ |vardhate sakalo (dharmaḥ) dharmāvṛtamanoharaḥ

ke narakaṃ gacchanti?manasā vañcitāḥ sattvā manasā vipramohitāḥ |gacchanti narakaṃ pāpāstamastamaparāyaṇāḥ

tamovṛte hi saṃsāre durlabhaṃ buddhaśāsanam |duḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ yānti yebhyaḥ dharmo na rocate

anādimati saṃsāre karmajālāvṛtā prajā |jāyate bhriyate cai(va) svakarmaphalahetunā

jāyante narake devā nārakeyāstathāvidhāḥ |manuṣyāḥ pretaviṣaye narakaṃ vā prayānti hi

sukarmaṇaiva sukhamanyonyaprabhavaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā yadi vā sukham |kleśadharmodbhave janmanyaparādikṛtaṃ
ca tat

asaṅkhyeyakṛtaṃ karma saṃsāre prāṇibhiḥ sadā |tatra śakyaṃ budhairgantuṃ varjayitvā tathāgataiḥ

nādharmasya phalaṃ sādhu viparītaṃ na pacyate |hetoḥ sadṛśatā dṛṣṭā jalasya vividhasya vai

sādṛśyasya hetuphalaṃ viparītaṃ na jātu hi |saṃskṛtānāmarūpāṇāṃ hetuḥ pratyayasambhavaḥ

sahetukaṃ sarva karmaphalamnāhetukaṃ phalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ narake tu viśeṣataḥ |hetusaṅghātasaṃsaktaṃ
narakeṣu vipacyate

kṛtoparatagāḍhānāṃ niyataṃ pāpagāminām |karmaṇā phalasambandho narakeṣu vipacyate

deśānāṃ prati kālaṃ tu yat karma (vi)nivartate |tasya neṣṭaṃ phalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tattvamārgavidarśakaiḥ

udāharaṇapradarśanapūrvakaṃ phalasya karmādhīnatvamevetidīpādhīnā prabhā yadvat karmādhīnaṃ
phalaṃ tathā |anyo'nyaphalasambhūtaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ sarva eva hi

pratītyasamutpādasamarthanamanyonyahetukā dṛṣṭā hyanyonyavaśavartinaḥ |sādṛśyasyānubandhena
dṛśyante tattvadarśakaiḥ

nāhetuphalasandhānamīśvarādibhirāsthitam |bhavanti saṃskṛtā dharmā deśitāstattvadarśibhiḥ

anādimatisaṃsāre hetupratyayasambhave |sādṛśyaṃ karmaṇā dṛṣṭaṃ viparītaṃ na kalpyate

buddhasya svarūpamtatstheṣu karmavaśagāḥ prāṇinaḥ karmahetujāḥ |sa karmaphalatattvajño (buddha)
ityabhidhīyate

keṣāṃ śāntiḥ na vidyate?mārgāmārgaviruddhā ye mūḍhā buddhasya śāsane |(na) teṣāṃ vidyate śāntirādityasya
tamo yathā

karmāyattaṃ sukhamkarmāyattaṃ sukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sukhāyattaṃ manastathā |mano'vaboddhayā(ste)dharmā
ye vyutpattivicāriṇaḥ

sucaritasya karmaṇaḥ phalamanityāḥ sarvasaṃskārā jalabud budasannibhāḥ |tasmāt sucaritaṃ karma
loke martya paratra ca

dṛṣṭaṃ karmaphalaṃ loke dṛṣṭā eva vicitratā |yaḥ pramādaparaḥ puṃsāstasyātmā dhruvamapriyaḥ

karmarajjvātidṛḍhayā duṣpramokṣasugāḍhayā |baddhā bālā na gacchanti nirvāṇapuramuttamam

||iti karmavargastrayodaśaḥ ||

## 14 saṃyojanavargaḥ

(14) saṃyojanavargaḥjñānena nirvāṇādhigamaḥjñānaśastre(ṇa)tāṃ chitvā nirmuktā vigatajvarā |prayānti
nirvṛttiṃ dhanyā yatra jñānyeva vidyate

sadṛśaṃ hi phalaṃ hetoḥ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |hetorasadṛśaṃ naiva phalaṃ pacati dehinām

hetupratyayasambaddhajanmapratyayasaṅgatāḥ |yathā baddhā hi saṃsāre pramucyante (na)dehinaḥ

paramaśānterūpāyaḥtāṃ viśliṣya susambaddhāṃ gāḍhāṃ durviṣehāṃ parām |gacchanti paramāṃ śānti
yatra duḥkhaṃ na vidyate

karmaṇā niyataṃ jantuḥ karmaṇā paripacyate |sūpagaṃ (nanu) saṃsāre sukhaṃ naivopalabhyate

duḥkhe duḥkhābhiṣaktānāṃ jantūnāṃ mūḍhacetasām |sanmārgadeśiko nāsti yo'smād duḥkhāt pramocayet

ye na dharmaparā nityaṃ ye na satyaparāḥ sadā |ye ca yoniratā nityaṃ teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na hīyate

dharma iha paratra ca sukhāvahaḥmātṛvat pitṛvaccaiva (mitravad) bandhuvat sadā |dharmo vai deśito
buddhairiha loke paratra ca

trigatyavasthitāḥ sattvāstridoṣavaśamāgatāḥ |tridhātugatikā nityaṃ trilokyāṃ patitāḥ (janāḥ)

trikarmopāntavaśagāḥ strīpānādiparāśca ye |na bhavānmuktigatikāḥ syurbhūmau vicaranti te

keṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na vidyate?triratne (na) pramādyanti tribodhivaśagāśca ye |tridṛṣṭivarjakā ye
tu teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na vidyate

trikālasthitisaṃlagnā vijvarāstattvadarśinaḥ |tribhāgakṛtisantuṣṭā vītaśokā nivṛttigāḥ

trirāśisamavetā ye trikarmapravicāriṇaḥ |na te vatsyanti saṃsāre vītadoṣā gatajvarāḥ

mārgāmārgavidhijñā ye bhāvābhāvavicintakāḥ |maitrībhāvaviviktā ye te yānti paramāṃ gatim

naraḥ kān bhajet?anāvilena(manasā) viprasannena (cetasā) |sarvadā dharmavaśagān dhīrān viprān
bhajennaraḥ

satyameva prapannā ye smṛtyā (hitvā) manomalam |bhāvābhilāṣād viratā muktā niḥsaṃśayaṃ hi te

nityaṃ duḥkhasukhairbaddhā vipralabdhā hyanekaśaḥ |narā nidhanatāṃ yānti prāṇino mohavañcitāḥ

kairdurguṇaiḥ svargo na labhyate?nāhrīkyamanapatrāpyaṃ kausīdyaṃ pāpamitratā |naitāni nākabījāni
tebhyo rajyenna paṇḍitaḥ

āhrīkyamanapatrāpyaṃ nityaṃ pāpānucāriṇaḥ |naraḥ prapātāt patati sa paścāt pratibudhyate

krodherṣyādibhayagrastāḥ svarga na gacchantikrodherṣyāstyānamiddhaṃ hi tvaśraddhaṃ yanmanastathā
|mohaśokabhayagrastā na svarga prabhavanti te

amṛtaṃ madyapānaṃ ca mithyādṛṣṭiśca lubdhatā |kāraṇāni karaṇasya vyāpādakrūrakarmatā

kudṛṣṭeḥ kuphalamadarśanaṃ paraṃ śreyo na kudṛṣṭiḥ kathañcana |kudarśanena saṃmūḍhāḥ prayānti
narakaṃ narāḥ

ahetuṃ hetumāpaśyantya (nityaṃ) nityavannarāḥ |prayānti narakaṃ tīvraṃ mithyāvādena vañcitāḥ

kaṣṭena tejasā teṣāṃ mithyādarśanatatparāḥ |prajñābhimānino bhūtā mohayantyaparān janān

mohāndhakāragahane patantyeva mahārṇave |loke prakṛtiduḥkhe'smin hetubhūtā hi te janāḥ

śarāṇāṃ tāpanaṃ kṣepaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |cittatā yā tudatyante sarvataḥ kleśaparvataḥ

kleśanāgād vimokṣo yo yasya jñānapuraḥ saraḥ |prāpyate vītakai rūpaiḥ puruṣaistattvadarśibhiḥ

anyo'nyamatibhiḥ sarvā loko'yaṃ vipralobhitaḥ |na yāti paramāṃ śāntiṃ yatra mithyā na kathyate

narakahetavaḥauddhatyapāpasaṃsargaḥ kausīdyaṃ lubdhatā tathā |hetavo narakasyaite śīlasya hi
vivarjanam

madyapānaṃ sadā hiṃsā paradārābhidarśanam |lobhaḥ kūrā matiścaiva hetavo narakasya te

nāśahetavaḥpaiśunyaṃ pāpasaṃsargo mithyādṛṣṭirasaṃyamaḥ |cāpalyaṃ manasaścaiva nāśayanti naraṃ
sadā

avidyayā saha pāruṣyaiḥ mithyāvāgabhimānitā |na sukhāya bhavantyete asmiṃlloke na cāpare

ke parivarjanīyāḥ?yadyasau bhramati prāyo lokesmin kinna cāpare |yadyasambhrāmite loke ye ca
duḥkhe nimajjati

tacceṣṭitamavidyāyāḥ kathayanti tathāgatāḥ |mithyāmāno'timānī ca sarvathā (parivarjyatām) |parivarjyāḥ
sadā hyete duḥkhavṛkṣasya hetavaḥ

jñāninaḥ paramodārā nityaṃ kleśavadhe ratāḥ |chitvā (tad) bandhanaṃ sarva gacchanti padamacyutam
|ādimadhyāntakalyāṇamasmiṃlloke na cāpare

doṣasevanaiḥ nāśo bhavatidoṣāṇāṃ sevanaṃ dṛṣṭamavidyāsampravartakam |varjanīyāḥ sadā kleśāḥ
sevyaṃ jñānamanuttamam

jñānena muktirbhavatijñānena muktirbhavati na ca kleśairihocyate |jñānāgninā hi dahayante kleśakāṣṭhā
na śeṣataḥ

kāmamayajñānaṃ kleśajanakamkleśaiḥ kimartha saṃruddhā jālinī viṣa(yai) ratā |punaḥ kāmamayaṃ
jñānaṃ cakṣuḥpaṭalabādhakam

rāganindānirvāṇayati sambuddhavākyabhaiṣajyabhojanaiḥ |saddharmasenāpatinā rāgasenā vigarhitāḥ

śubhā vāṇī prayoktavyāudīritā śubhā vāṇī niścetavyā prayatnataḥ |catvāro hi viparyāsā budbudavaccakāsate
|lokadharmāstathā cāṣṭau nāśayantyakhilaṃ jagat

jñānaplavaḥ santaraṇasamarthaḥjālinīprabhavā nadyo vitarkaśatadustarāḥ |jñānaplavaṃ samāruhya
taranti munayaḥ śivāḥ

keṣāṃ muktirna vidyate?mūḍhā ye bhūtamanaso narāḥ kāmasya sevinaḥ |sadā ca pañcabhiḥ sthānairmuktisteṣāṃ
na vidyate

ayoniśomanaskārairyo vahniḥ samudīryate |sa yoniśomanaskāravarṣaṇaiḥ pratikalpate

avidyāsaṃbhavam andhatvaṃ kathaṃ dūrībhavet?andhatvaṃ cirakālotthamavidyāsambhavaṃ nṛṇām |tadalpakāle
jātena pradīpena na paśyati

jñānalokena trayo doṣāḥ vinaśyantirāgamūḍhā vipacyante narake mandamedhasaḥ |jñāninastu na naśyanti
nirvāṇagamanā yathā |trayo do(ṣā) vinaśyanti jñānalokena dehinām

tasmādaśeṣavijñānaśīlakṣāntirato bhavet |amūḍhānāṃ praṇaśyanti trayo doṣāḥ śarīriṇām

jñānānalena mandaśca kleśendhana mahāttviṣaḥ |vyādhinirmagnatanavaḥ śayyāveṣṭanagocarāḥ

yatra yāti paraṃ vedaṃ manomohamayaṃ phalam |yāni duḥkhānyanekānyanekā bhujyate gatiḥ

pañcakaiḥ prāṇibhinityametanmohasya ceṣṭitam |trayo vā nirjitā doṣā yairidaṃ dahyate jagat

jñānayogena sukhaṃ bhavatitamagniṃ jñānayogena nirvāpya sukhito bhavet |tasmājjñānāgninā nityaṃ
nirdahet kleśaparvatam |kleśaparvatadagdhasya sukhaṃ padamavasthitam

||iti saṃyojanavargaścaturdaśaḥ ||

## 15 pāpavargaḥ

(15) pāpavargaḥpāpaphalasya ajñātā paritāpaṃ labhatekriyamāṇasya pāpasya vijñeyaṃ kaṭukaṃ phalam
|yo na jānāti mūḍhātmā paścāt sa paritapyate

(phalaṃ ca) labhate puruṣaḥ sarvapāpasya karmaṇaḥ |tasmāt pāpaṃ na seveta yadīcchet sukhamātmanaḥ

alpādapi mahāghorāt narakāt pāpacetasaḥ |muktyartha (tāni) pāpāni bhūyo mohavaśaṃ gatāḥ

na viśvaseddhi pāpasyā(lpatāṃ) narakagāmikām |alpena vahninā śaile dahayante sarvato drumāḥ

nāsti pāpasamo śatruḥpāpena narakaṃ yāti pāpena paridahyate |pāpena naiti nirvāṇaṃ nāsti pāpasamo
ripuḥ

pāpācāro hi puruṣo na kvacit sukhamedhate |(pāpaṃ) tasmānna seveta yadīcchet sukhamātmanaḥ

sādhukāraṃ ca sādhūnāṃ pāpamārgasya duṣkaram |kurvantaḥ pāpakān sattvā modante(laghucetasaḥ)

vipākakaṭukaḥ paścāt paritāpo'lpacetasaḥ |kuto'pacitagātrasya karmaṇo dṛśyate phalam

tasmāt pāpaṃ na kurvīta kaṣṭā pāpasya vedanā |pāpaṃ pāpavipākaṃ ca pāpināṃ pacyate dhruvam

na hi pāpaṃ na pāpasya tasmāt pāpaṃ vivarjayet |parivarjitapāpasya na pāpād bhayamasti hi

svakṛtaṃ bādhate pāpaṃ parīkṣyaṃ naiva bhujyate |kalyāṇasya phalaṃ sādhu kaṣṭaṃ pāpasya pacyate

kṛtvā tu puruṣaḥ pāpaṃ kalyāṇaṃ nānusevate |na śastrāgniprapātebhyo duḥkhaṃ syāt tādṛśaṃ nṛṇām

yathā niṣevitaṃ mithyāpāpaṃ pāpeṣu kalpyate |vinivarjitapāpasya nityaṃ ca śubhacetasaḥ

śāntasya bhikṣornirvāṇaṃ nātidūram(dehino) śāntavaktrasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ |tīvrāt tīvrataraṃ
yānti narāḥ kukṛtakāriṇaḥ

sukhāt sukhataraṃ yānti narāḥ sukṛtakāriṇaḥ |na hi tīvrasya pāpasya sukhaṃ phalamavāpyate

sukhasya vā phala tīvraṃ viparītaṃ na pacyate |anādimati saṃsāre sukṛtānāṃ phalaṃ sukham

karmaṇāṃ sukṛtānāṃ ca tathā duḥkhaphalaṃ smṛtam |pāpaṃ pāpānugaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ śubhasya ca śubhaṃ tathā

pāpī narakeṣupapadyatepāpācāraḥ śubhadveṣī narakeṣūpapadyate |parivarjitapāpasya nityaṃ ca śubhacetasaḥ

hastāvalambi nirvāṇamadṛṣṭaṃ tadanāvilam |ādimadhyāntakalyāṇā dharmā nityaṃ sukhodayāḥ

tasmāt pāpaṃ vivarjayetādimadhyāntakaṭukaṃ phalaṃ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ |tasmāt pāpaṃ sadā varjyamālambyañca
śubhaṃ sadā

parivarjitapāpasya nityaṃ sukhamavasthitam |anādimati saṃsāre dagdhāḥ pāpaiḥ punarnarāḥ |khedaṃ
kasmānna gacchanti bālā mohavaśānugāḥ

āpātamadhuraṃ pāpaṃ pariṇāme'gnisannibham |pāpakārī tu puruṣaḥ sarvaloke vigarhitaḥ |śubhakārī
sadā śāntastasmāt pāpaṃ vivarjayet

dhīraistattvanidarśakaiḥ pāpavivarjanamaniṣṭamahitaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ vipāke kaṭuvedanam |pāpaṃ pāpavipākaṃ
ca tasmād dhīmān vivarjayet

asti pāpaṃ dhruvaṃ pāpe pāpamasti na pātakam |pāpāceṣṭā kṣayāpekṣā kṛpā cittasugāmitā

pāpaṃ pāpāśritaṃ nityaṃ dharmo dharmāśritastathā |unnayatyuktametaddhi dhīraistattvanidarśakaiḥ

pāpavirahitāḥ paramaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnuvantiacetanā dhruvaṃ bālā yeṣāṃ duścaritaṃ priyam |vinivarjitapāpāstu
gacchanti paramāṃ gatim

atīva vañcanāghorā niyatā pāpagāminī |mānuṣyaṃ durlabhaṃ prāpya yasya pāpaṃ priyaṃ bahu

śubhāśubhaphalayoḥ pariṇāmabhedaḥpāpād virajyate yastu śubhe vā'tīva rajyate |sa sukhāt sukhamāpnoti
nivṛttiṃ vā prayāti hi

naro bhavati dhanyo'yaṃ yaḥ śubhānyupasevate |sa tvadhanyatamo loke yaḥ pāpamupasevate

ādimadhyāntanidhanaṃ sadbhireva vigarhitam |pāpaṃ pāpānugaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ narakāyopakalpyate

śubhakarmā sukhamāpnoti devalokaṃ ca gacchatiatīva śobhate loke śubhakarmā jitendriyaḥ |kāyasya
bhedāt satataṃ devaloke sa jāyate

pāpasya pariṇāmaḥ sudāruṇaḥduṣkṛtasyānubaddhaṃ hi duṣkṛtaṃ phalamucyatesukṛtasya tathā dṛṣṭaṃ
phalaṃ sukhavipākajam |bījasyāśuvibhītasya pariṇāmaḥ sudāruṇaḥ

nimittaṃ sadṛśaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ |pāpenākṛṣyate jantuḥ durgatau baḍiśairiva

baḍiśairmucyate mīnaḥ pāpaṃ pāpānna mucyate |dina pīḍāpi pāpasya sarvathā duḥkhakārikā

amedhyasya yathā gandhaḥ pratikūlo hi dehinām |śubhasya vāsanā ramyā vipraṇaṣṭasya durgatau

vileṣu vāsanaṃ yadvat puṣpe naṣṭe pradṛśyate |calatyayaṃ pāpakārī nityaṃ kāmavaśānugaḥ

ke śubhacāriṇaḥ?ālasyānṛtiko nityaṃ nāsau kalyāṇamarhati |anantā rajanī teṣāṃ (yeṣāṃ) pāpe sthitaṃ
manaḥ

prabhātaṃ rajanī teṣāṃ yeṣāṃ pāpe sthirā matiḥ |anīrṣyakāḥ sakalyāṇāḥ puruṣāḥ śubhacāriṇaḥ

alpapāpī sukhamāpnuteye na pāpātmano bhūtāste nityaṃ koṭacāriṇaḥ |alpabhārā yathā nāvā plavate
na nimajjati

pāpīmitreṇa duḥkhaṃ bhavatitathālpapāpapuruṣaḥ plavate na nimajjati |na pāpamitrasaṃsargāt puruṣaḥ
sukhavān bhavet

pāpamitraṃ samāsādya sarvānalaparo bhavet |karmārambha vidhijño yo nityaṃ sūkṣmārtha (darśakaḥ)

nāsau limpati pāpena paṅkena gaganaṃ yathā |aśrutaṃ puruṣaḥ śṛṇvan śrutañcāpi bhaved dṛḍham

paṇḍitalakṣaṇampāpaṃ ca varjayed dhīmān pretya deveṣu jāyate |guṇadoṣaparijñānammetat paṇḍitalakṣaṇam

mūḍhalakṣaṇamguṇadoṣāparijñānametanmūḍhasya lakṣaṇam |guṇeṣu guṇasañcāro doṣeṣu ca tathaiva
ca

mūḍhāmūḍhayorlakṣaṇamsadoṣaguṇatattvajño nityaṃ bhavati śokabhāk |pāpakarttā (tu) puruṣaḥ śatruvaccātmanaḥ
sthitaḥ

śubhakarttā tathā puṃso mitravat pratipadyate |tamonirayabhūmiṣvamṛto'yaṃ pratipadyate

yasya pāparatā buddhirnityaṃ viṣayatatparā |viṣayārāmacapalā nityaṃ rāgānucāriṇī

yasya buddhirna dharmeṣu narasya duḥkhabhāginaḥ |hetutaśca mahāduḥkhāntahetorvahnisaṃyutāt

kṣīṇapāpā vayaṃ sarve bhūyo lokamihāgatāḥ |sukṛtasya phalaṃ sādhu hyanekaguṇamaṇḍitam

duṣkṛtasya phalaṃ duḥkhaṃ visaṃvādakamiṣyate |te vayaṃ sukhaduḥkhābhyāṃ bhūyāma bhavasaṅkaṭe

karmavāyusamudbhrāntāḥ samudrasya yathormayaḥ |kṛtaṃ pāpeṣvamanasaḥ pāpaiḥ rakṣanti ye janāḥ

te tasya hetornarakaṃ prayāntyaśubhacāriṇaḥ |tasmāttu narakānmuktāḥ te gacchanti triviṣṭapam

te hi duḥkhaṃ (na)saṃsmṛtya punaḥ kāmavaśānugāḥ |kharībhūtamidaṃ cittaṃ dolādolaiḥ sukhāsukhaiḥ

viyogo'yaṃ parīghāto na ca duḥkhairna vidyate |apāradārasaṃyukta indriyaiḥ parivañcitaḥ

tṛṣṇāpāśavaśagaḥ saṃsāre bhramatijano bhramati saṃsāre tṛṣṇāpāśavaśānugaḥ |narakāt pretabhuvanaṃ
tiryagyoniṣu pretataḥ

tiryagbhyo nākabhuvanaṃ nākād bhūyo nṛjātiṣu |ekakarmaparibhrānto jagad bhramati cakravat

sarva duḥkhamna ca khedavaśaṃ yānti(hyabhyāsa) vaśamāgatāḥ |rakṣanti nākabhuvanaṃ virajyante
tathā sukhaiḥ

sukhāt sukhaparibhrāntā bhramanti bhramacāriṇaḥ |duḥkhaṃ padmasahasrāṇi padmakoṭiśatāni ca

(tāni) duḥkhāni bhuṅktāni na ca kliśyanti bāliśāḥ |na sukhaṃ vidyate loke sarvaduḥkhaiḥ pariplutam

duḥkhasya hetuḥ bhavatiudvegaṃ naiva gacchanti prāṇino mohapīḍitāḥ |duḥkhena khidyate bālo duḥkhahetau
ca vartate

hetau śataphalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ bījaṃ prati yathā phalaiḥ |sukhaduḥkhakaśābaddhāḥ prāṇinastribhavānugāḥ

na yānti paramaṃ kṣemaṃ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitam |smaryate (hi) yadā duḥkhaṃ tadā vidhyati dehinam

vismṛte ca punarduḥkhe caranti vigatajvarāḥ |nārakaṃ tu sadā duḥkhaṃ smṛtaṃ bhavati dehibhiḥ

tadā kṛṣṇamidaṃ saukhyamanumānena tiṣṭhati |tadeva viditaṃ matvā jagat sarva samanvitam

śreyasi kriyatāṃ buddhirdoṣebhyo vinivāryate |kṣaṇādhikāracapalaṃ jīvitaṃ veti vartate

kṣīyante cāpi karmāṇi tasmācchreyaḥparo bhavet |cittavegaṃ manaḥ sarva jīvitaṃ veti vartate

anāgatād duḥkhāt jñānī na bibhetitasmānna jīvikāhetau pāpaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |anāgatasya duḥkhasya
na vibheti mahājanaḥ

yenā(sya) pacyate pāpaṃ mohapāpavaśaṅgatam |mohitaḥ pāpakairdhamairaśubhaiḥ śuklavarjitaiḥ

mūḍhaḥ pāpavaśād narakaṃ gacchatinīyate narakaṃ mūḍho yatra pāpaṃ vipacyate |yasya pāpād bhayaṃ
nāsti sa pāpaṃ kurute bahu

sa pāpānantasandagdhaḥ paścānnarakamāpnute |(api) sūkṣmāgnikaṇikā yathā dāhāya kalpate

pāpavirahitāḥ sukhamicchantianyairapi tathā pāpairnarakaṃ yāntyabuddhayaḥ |apāpabhīrutā tasmāt
kartavyā sukhamicchatā

hiṃsā mithyāvaco'brahmacarya bhūyaḥ sukhodayam |āhrīkyamanapatrāpyamauddhatyaṃ pāpamitratā

viṣāgnisadṛśaṃ pāpamviṣāgnisadṛśā hayete tebhyo rakṣet prayatnataḥ |duḥkhaṃ hi māradharmo'yaṃ
sukhaṃ dharmasamantataḥ

sukhaduḥkhayorlakṣaṇamlakṣaṇaṃ(sukha) duḥkhānāṃ vidustattvavido janāḥ |satyaṃ dānaṃ tathā kṣāntiḥ
sadā cāpāpamitratā |maitrī sadābhibhūteṣu prasthānaṃ tridivasya (hi)

||iti pāpavargaḥ pañcadaśaḥ ||

## 16 narakavargaḥ

(16)narakavargaḥaṣṭau narakāgnayaḥkṛtvā duṣkṛtakarmāṇi sattvā viṣamajīvinaḥ |yā gatiḥ pretya
gacchantī tānapāyān (hi me) śṛṇu

sañjīvaṃ kāmasūtraṃ ca sampātaṃ dvau ca rauravau |tamoparaṃ mahācitrapatanaṃ ca pratāpanam

ityete narakā aṣṭāvākhyātā duratikramāḥ |raudrakarmābhisaṅkīrṇāḥ pratyekaṃ kṣobhadā (matāḥ)

narakāgnīnāṃ bhayānakatvamcatuḥskandhāścaturdvārā vibhaktā bhāgaśo mitāḥ |ayaḥ prākāraparyantā
ayasā pratiduḥkhitāḥ

taptāyomayasaṃ bhūmirjvalantī tejasodgatā |anekayojanaśataṃ dahati svaciṃṣā(bhṛśam)

kandarpadamanā ghorā arciṣmanto durāsadāḥ |romaharṣaṇarūpāste'pyāhurduḥkhā bhayānakāḥ

narake patitā bhṛśaṃ tapyanteete patanti narake ūrdhvapādā avāṅmukhāḥ |ṛṣīṇāmativaktāraḥ saṃyatānāṃ
tapasvinām

te punastatra pacyante taptāmbhasi kṛtā iva |krandamānā divārātrau, nagā vāteritā iva

praṇadanti mahānādaṃ tīvraduḥkhaiḥ pratāpitāḥ |sarvā diśaśca dhāvanti nārakaiḥ puruṣairdrutāḥ

kudṛṣṭyā mohayitvārthe mohapāśāvṛto janaḥ |prāpnoti narakaṃ ghoraṃ kāraṇodadhisaṃvṛtaḥ

kudṛṣṭyā dagdhasarvasvo manuṣyaḥ puruṣādhamaḥ |tathā baddho'sti narake mānināṃ ghorakāraṇam

ye mithyāvaśamāpannāḥ puruṣā mandamedhasaḥ |te sarve narakaṃ yānti cittairhi parivañcitāḥ

kukṛtaphalamyastvayā duṣkṛtaṃ pūrva kṛtaṃ cittānuvartinā |tasya bhuṅktaphalaṃ mūḍha vipākottama
(māpsyasi)

hāryāṇi paricittāni, saṃsevyāḥ parayoṣitaḥ |hrasvā (hi) jantavo nityaṃ svacittaparivañcitāḥ

tasya karmavipākasya vaśa eva tvamāgataḥ |kiṃ krandasi svayaṃ kṛtvā kukarma puruṣādhama!

ye narāḥ kukṛtaṃ kṛtvā pañcatvaṃ yāntyamedhasaḥ |yeṣāṃ tu niṣphalaṃ janma bījamuptaṃ yathoṣare

alpamādhuryasaṃyuktān kāmān kaṭuvipākinaḥ |sevate yastvasau mūḍhaḥ sa yāti tamaso tamaḥ

kiṃ kṛtaṃ mūḍha bhavatā putradārasukhārthinā |dahasye narake caikaḥ svakarmaphalavañcitaḥ

keṣāṃ duṣkaraṃ jīvanam?yaḥ putradāravaśagaḥ sevate duṣkaraṃ naraḥ |sa yāti narakatvaṃ (ca) pāpaṃ
tadanubhūyate

na dārā na sutā nārtha na mitrāṇi kathañcana |mṛtyukāle samutpanne rakṣanti samupasthitāḥ

yeṣāṃ sāṃkleśikaṃ cittaṃ tṛṣṇayā parivañcitam |sahāyatā kutasteṣāṃ kasmāt (tvaṃ) paritapyase?

hṛto'si pūrvaviṣayaistṛṣṇayā parivañcitaḥ |kiṃ krandasi svayaṃ kṛtvā kukṛtaṃ mūḍhacetanaḥ

na tathāgnirna śastrāṇi na viṣaṃ nārayaḥ sthitāḥ |bādhate puruṣaṃ loke yathā duṣkṛtyamātmajam

pakṣiṇāṃ nīḍavāsavat bhavacakramdigbhāge hi yathā gatvā saṃśrayante vanaspatīn |vihagāḥ kālamutthāya
tathā prāṇisamāgamaḥ

nāśayitvā paraṃ dravyaṃ kṛtvā lokavyatikramaṃ |kiṃ karotyaśubhaṃ karma mohena parivañcitaḥ

yena yāti parāṃ śāntiṃ yena yātyasurālayam |tatra hetuḥ paraṃ mohastamasāmapi yat tamaḥ

kuṭumbāya kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ narake pātayatiputradāramayaiḥ pāśaiścānītā narakālayam |yadartha kriyate
pāpaṃ yuṣmābhiścittavañcitaiḥ

mitrāṇi putrāḥ (pitarau kiṃ ) bandhujana-bāndhavāḥ |yeṣāmarthe kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bhavadbhirmandabuddhibhiḥ

nūnamātmā na te kānto babhūva narabhūmiṣu |yena tvayā kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ putrārthena narādhamaḥ

yena pūrvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ paścānna paritapyate |sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ putrakāraṇavañcitaḥ

pāpakarma-phalamkiṃ kariṣyanti putrāste kiṃ dārāḥ kiṃ ca bāndhavāḥ |dahasyanādinarake vahniḥ
karma dunotyasau

svayaṃ kṛtvā'śubhaṃ karmāsukhodayamaninditaḥ |tapyase'pi vṛthādhānaḥ paścānmohena vañcikaḥ

rāgadveṣānugāḥ pāpaṃ mohena parivañcitāḥ |aghaṃ prayānti te sarve putradārasukhārthinaḥ

antargatena ca punarbahiḥsthena ca vahninā |dahyamāno(hi)duḥkhena narake pāpakarmaṇā

vaśaṃ prāptāḥ svacittasya sarva eva viḍambitāḥ |kiṃ krandasi vṛthā nāde vahninā paritāpitaḥ

pāpānugaphalaṃ dṛṣṭamatha pāpāni karmāṇi kṛtavānasi manyase |durmate tatra mā kranda kiṃ vṛthā
paridevase

pāpeṣu rakṣyate mūḍhaḥ kāraṇāśuci rakṣyate |pāpānugaphalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ hetupratyayasambhavam

kasmānna sevito dharmaḥ pāpañca nahi varjitam |pāpād bahiśca puruṣo narakaṃ naiva paśyati

yo na vindanti mūḍhātmā phalaṃ pāpasya karttṛkam |so'vaśyaṃ labhate doṣān kathaṃ bhokṣyasi durmate

pāpena vañcita pūrvaṃ pāpena paridahyate |na kariṣyasi pāpāni tvaduḥkhenaiva yāsyasi

kṛtavānasi pāpāni śubhāni na kathañcana |pāpena dahyate tasmādaśubhe na manaḥ kṛthāḥ

kiṃ duḥkhādapi duḥkham?nāsti duḥkhādato duḥkhaṃ yaḥ pāpamanusevate |tasmāt pāpaṃ na seveta yadi
duḥkhaṃ na vāñchasi

kuṭumbijanānāṃ spaṣṭoktiḥtasyāntarhṛdaye bimbaṃ kintu śāntirna vidyate |kāruṇyāspadabhūtāḥ smo
nāsmākaṃ karuṇāhṛdi

yathā kṛtaṃ bhavadbhiśca pāpaṃ mohāvṛtairbahu |tad vaḥ pradhāvate gāḍhaṃ na ca ye tatra hetavaḥ

śīlaṃ na rakṣitaṃ mūḍha pāpaṃ ca bahu sañcitam |sambhūtasya ca pāpasya phalametadupasthitam

na vayaṃ kāraṇaṃ tatra yūyameva hi kāraṇam |yaḥ pāpaṃ kurute karma sa hetustasya karmaṇaḥ

bhavadbhiryat kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tṛṣṇāśāpaviḍambitaiḥ |tad vo prapadyate ghoraṃ kimasmān paribhāṣata

nākṛtaṃ pacyate pāpaṃ na pāpaṃ syādahetukam |yena yaddhi kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tasya tat paripacyate

pāpī narakaṃ yātitad bhavantaḥ kriyāhīnā madyapānena vañcitāḥ |patitā narake tīvre kiṃ vṛthā
paritapyatha

te yūyaṃ kāmaparamāḥ ghoraṃ narakamāgatāḥ |kāraṇeṣvapi tīvreṣu kiṃ tathā paritapyatha

yadā kṛtāni pāpāni bhavadbhiḥ kāmamohitaiḥ |tadā kasmānna nikṛṣṭaṃ kimadya paritapyatha

hetubhūto hi narako mṛṣāvādasya deśitaḥ |heturvai sevitaṃ pūrva niṣphalaṃ paridevatha

nārthena labhate satyaṃ na kṛcchreṇopapadyate |tasmāt satyaṃ parityajya mṛṣāvādeṣu rajyase

pūrveṣu pāpamahitaṃ bhavatā mūḍhacetasā |kṛtaṃ bhavadbhiḥ kukṛtaṃ (duṣkṛtyaṃ ) paripacyate

pāpakṣayād vinirmukto narakaṃ nopalabhyate |nākrandamānāḥ puruṣāḥ vimucyante kathañcana

pāpakartṛ phalaṃ svayameva bhuṅktekiṃ krandatha vṛthā mūḍhā vañcitāḥ svena karmaṇā |muñcitā
vañcitā bālā vṛthā krandantyabuddhayaḥ

ahitairhitarūpaistvaṃ mitrarūpaiśca śatrubhiḥ |vipralabdho'si bho martya! gacchannasi mahat
tamaḥ

nānyaḥ śatruryathā karma duṣkṛtaṃ tava pāpakam |karmaṇā trividhenā'tha nīyase yamasādhanam

kasmādasi tato mūḍha, vañcitaḥ putrasaṃjñakaiḥ |na dānādiṣu buddhistaiḥ kṛtā (te) mohavañcitā

asmāṃllokātparaṃ lokaṃ śatrupṛṣṭhorago yathā |śatrūṇāṃ prathamaḥ śatruḥ sarvapāpanidarśakaḥ

viṣāgniśastrapratima! svayaṃ hi duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |tvayā kṛtāni karmāṇi tvamevamanubhokṣyase

yasya śāntaṃ sadā cittaṃ viṣayairyo na hanyate |na hyavasthāmimāṃ yānti tathātvamanupaśyasi

narakasya dhvaniṃ śrutvā kathaṃ krandasi durmate! |kiṃ punaryatra vahnistvāṃ dhakṣyati śuṣkakāṣṭhavat

na dagdhā vahninā ye ca dagdhāste kukṛtaiḥ svakaiḥ |vahnistu śāmyate kvāpi duṣkṛtāgnirna śāmyati

nāgnirniryāti lokāntaṃ nāgnispṛṣṭo naro (mṛtaḥ) |kukṛtāgnimayaṃ pāpaṃ yad dhakṣyati hi tattvataḥ

kukṛtāgnividagdhā ye te dagdhā narake narāḥ |pāpāgnivarjitā ye tu na teṣāṃ narakodayaḥ |yadyātmanaḥ
priyo nityaṃ vibheṣi narakād yadi

vivarjaya svapāpāni tato duḥkhaṃ na yāsyasi |yānti pāpamaye nityaṃ(narā) mohavaśānugāḥ

prāpnoṣi narakaṃ ghoraṃ kimaśrūṇi vimuñcasi ? |duḥkhaṃ duḥkhavipākaṃ ca duḥkhāntagamanaṃ tathā

sukṛtaṃ nādimadhyāntaśobhanaṃ paripacyate |nātra loke tvayā'pāpaṃ yatkṛtaṃ sukṛtaṃ (muhuḥ)

tasya tīvravipākasya phalamadyopabhokṣyase |hetupratyayasādṛśyaṃ viparītaṃ na kalpyate

hetuḥ kṛto yathā pūrva tathā phalamavāpsyasi |yathā tava tathā'nyeṣāṃ prāṇarakṣā prayatnataḥ

kasmāt prāṇātipātāste kṛtāḥ pāpānuvartinā |prāṇatyāgena puruṣairyad dhanaṃ samupārjitam

karmodayakṛtaṃ tatte yattvayā samupārjitam |sarveṣāṃ dayitā dārāḥ prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasaḥ

tattvayā rāgaraktena kasmādapakṛtā'vanāt |buddhervyāmohajanakaṃ dharmāṇāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ param

dharmapānaṃ tvayā kārya karmānuparivarjitam |jihvāviṣasamutthaṃ yat sarvāpratyayakārakam

mṛṣāvādaṃ tvayā pāpaṃ karmā'nuparivarjitam |evaṃ pañca-vidheyeṣu yasmāt tvamanuraktavān

tasmāt saṃmukṣya mā rodi kiṃ vṛthā paridevase |viṣayaṃ pāpakā dharmā yasmānnu parivañcitaḥ

tasmāt prāpnoṣi narakaṃ jvālāmālākulaṃ mahat |etat tṛṣṇāgninā sarva pradīptaṃ bhuvanatrayam

dharmācaraṇe sāvahitena bhavitavyamdṛṣṭvā nācarito dharmaḥ kimadya paritapyase |(śrutvā) te
madhuraṃ cittaṃ kāmamandirakaṃ vacaḥ

udīrite mahāpāpe tasmai tatphalamāgatam |sa kṛtvā pāpakaṃ karma niyatā pāpavedanā

karuṇaṃ vikalaṃ dīnaṃ kimadya paritapyase |karmakṣayeṇa narakāt mucyante pāpakāriṇaḥ

vikrośamagnā bahuśo na mucyante kathañcana |aniṣṭaṃ pāpakaṃ karma kṛtvā rogavaśe sthitāḥ

ajñānino'śubhamācarantiparidevati yo mūḍho vṛthā sa paridevati |anāgataphalajño yaḥ pratyutpannaśubhe
rataḥ

krośate narake nāsau yathā tvamanutapyase |mayā kṛtāni karmāṇi phaladāni mamaiva hi

rogeṇa śatruṇā dagdhaḥ paścānnarakamāgataḥ |pramādabhūmiraśubhā rāgadagdhasya dehinaḥ

tena pāśena baddho'haṃ gato'vasthāmimāṃ bhṛśam |durācāraratānāṃ tu nṛṇāmasya phalaṃ kaṭu

ajñānabādhito'smīti svakṛtaṃ bhujyate mayā |nirdayānāṃ sughorāṇāṃ pāpānāṃ vaśamāgataḥ

muktiḥ kathaṃ syāt?muktirasmāt kathaṃ syādvai duḥkhasaṃsārasāgarāt |duḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ karma
mayedaṃ paripacyate

sukhāvāptiṃ na paśyāmi kṣemaṃ vā narakasya me |jīvalokādahaṃ bhraṣṭo abuddha iva mārgataḥ

jīvo'yaṃ vivaśo bhūtvā vikṛtaiḥ parivāritaḥ |jvālāmālākulaṃ sarvamantarikṣaṃ nirantaram

diśaśca tridiśaścaiva pṛthi(vī) ca nirantarā |kṛpaṇo'jñānago duḥkhī nivāsaṃ nopalakṣaye

kṣuradhārāvitaptasya nṛpasyāsyātibhīṣaṇam |kāntāre bāhyamānasya niḥ sahāyasya sarvataḥ |trātāraṃ
nāvagacchāmi yo'smād duḥkhāt pramocayet

niḥśaktiravaśo duḥkhī vahninā paritāpitaḥ |nīye'haṃ vivaśaḥ kvāpi bāhubandhanayantritaḥ

na vā'rthā nāpi mitrāṇi na putrā na ca yoṣitaḥ |trāyante vyasanādasmāt kṛtaghnā bata te mama

adharmacāriṇo na kvāpi śaraṇamnirānandasya na sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ (gṛhṇāmi sarvaśaḥ) |mṛtyupāśena
baddhasya śaraṇaṃ nopalakṣyate

saṃkruddhā iva te, krūrā bhaviṣyanti samantataḥ |nirānandā diśaḥ sarvā vyālaiśca parivāritā

narakaṃ pātakasyaiva pateyaṃ dhṛtibhāvataḥ |yacca yaccehaṃ paśyāmi jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ tathā
|tat sarva vyākulaṃ caiva vahninā paridīpitam

nādhigacchāmi śaraṇaṃ niḥsahāyo'smi sarvataḥ |ghore tamasi majjāsi sāgare ca hṛtaplavaḥ

gaganaṃ naiva paśyāmi nakṣatragrahatārakam |viparītamidaṃ sarva tamasā parivāritam

pañcendriyāṇi sarvāṇi viratāni hi sarvataḥ |krakacaistu tadā sarva śarīraṃ paripāṭayate

nādhigacchāmi śaraṇaṃ kāmaṃ bandho bhaviṣyati |vardhante duḥkhanikarā sarvataḥ parivārataḥ

muhurmuhuśca vardhante vedanāñcitadehajāḥ |duḥkhaiśca (parivardhante) nissahāyaṃ ca sarvataḥ

samīkṣya karmajaṃ nityaṃ dhyeyo hetuḥ punaḥ punaḥ |kathayanti sukhaṃ vṛddhāḥ svakarmaphalajairdṛḍham

yat pūrva karaṇīyaṃ te tatpaścāt paricintyase |mohena vañcitaḥ pūrvaṃ kimadya paritapyase |kiṃ
punaḥ pāpakartturyaḥ kaṭukaṃ paripacyate

anekaduḥkhahetūtthaṃ tava duḥkhaṃ bhaviṣyati |trāṇaṃ naiva (ca) yatrāsti vinā karmaparikṣayāt

evaṃ satyasya nāśena yamadūtairanekaśaḥ |nīyate narakaṃ ghoraṃ karmapāśavaśaṅgataḥ

catuṣkoṇaścaturdvāro vibhakto bhāgaśo mitaḥ |pacyate yatra vivaśā nārakeyā hyanekaśaḥ

bhedaprāyeṇa bahuśo yattvayā kathitaṃ bahu |kalmaṣaṃ bhūtadharmāṇāṃ tasyaitat phalamāgatam

paiśunyasevino narakaṃ yāntibhasmībhavanti te nityaṃ mitrasvajanabāndhavaiḥ |eṣāmabhimataṃ duṣṭaṃ
paiśunyaṃ sadvigarhitam

tasmānna carito dharmaḥ paiśunyaṃ ca na varjitam |paiśunyaṃ saphalaṃ bhuktvā kimadya paritapyase

jihvādhanavinirmuktaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ viṣamamucchritam |pāruṣyamiti sandṛṣṭamaho! tatphalamīdṛśam

sattvaṃ pāruṣyaparamaṃ ghoraṃ narakamāgatam |jihvāyātaṃ tavākṛtyai kimadya paridevase

sahastraguṇaparyantaḥ svato hṛdayasambhavaḥ |kṣutpipāsāmayo vahniḥ samādahati mārutam

paruṣavāco narakasya cāntaramyadantaraṃ himāgnyorhi merusarṣapayośca yat |tadantaraṃ jihvayoktyā
narake jvalanasya ca

narakaprabhavo vahnirna kvā'pyanyatra prāpyate |kṣutpipāsāmayo vahnirdeveṣvapyupalabhyate

yo hyataḥ kāraṇāttīkṣṇo vahnirbhavati nārakaḥ |na tathā sa prabhavati yathā vahnistadudbhavaḥ

pūrvottarā baddhapadaṃ nirarthakamasaṅgatam |vistrabdha yattvayā proktaṃ tasyaitatphalamāgatam

pareṣāṃ sampadaṃ dṛṣṭvā mama syāditi cintitam |tamo'bhikhyāsamutthasya viṣasya phalamāgatam

vyāpārānaladagdho'si mānuṣyaṃ puruṣādhama! |dahanād dahanaṃ prāpto tapyase ca vikatthyase

vyāpāraparamohe tu narakāyopapattaye |rajjvaitayaiva baddho'si puruṣaḥ parikrandase

vyāpādenākṛṣṭo'si ghoraṃ narakamāgataḥ |karmakṣayād bhavenmokṣo narakācca vimokṣyase

adharmakathanaṃ dahatyevaadharmo dharmarūpeṇa sādhurūpeṇa pāpakam |yat tvayā kathitaṃ pūrva
tat tvāṃ dahati nānalaḥ

kathaṃ tattvavinaṣṭānāṃ dharmādharmanicchatām |deśitaṃ vivaraṃ pāpaṃ niyataṃ pāpagāmikam

(yas)tvayā varjito dharmaḥ sādhavaścāpi ninditāḥ |yattvayopārjitaṃ kṛṣṭaṃ tattvayā'dya vipacyate

tṛṣṇāviṣayayuktena mohavegena sarvadā |yataḥ sadharmavinayaiḥ kiṃ tathā paridevase

sa bhavet pāpakṛnnityaṃ mohapāśavaśaṅgataḥ |ki tapyase rodiṣi ca karma kṛtvā sudāruṇam

hetupratyayamūḍhasya dharmādharmeṣu sarvadā |prāpto'si narakaṃ ghoraṃ nārakāgraṃ sudāruṇam

kāmavaśānugā narakaṃ yāntikāmaiḥ kramati saṃyuktairyanna bhavati ceṣṭitam |sukṛtaṃ karmavirasaṃ
phalametadupasthitam

kṣapayitvā pramādena sukhāsaktena cetasā |sukṛta narakaṃ yānti devāḥ kāmavaśānugāḥ

āryāpavādakā narakaṃ yāntiāryāpavādakā ye ca ye ca karmaphaladviṣaḥ |te mṛtvā narakaṃ yānti
ye ca mithyāvinītakāḥ

dharmavañcakā eva narakagāminaḥjananīgṛhapāpīyā ye cānye dharmavañcakāḥ |teṣāmiṣṭakarā loke
narakasya ca gāminaḥ

pāparatā duḥkhamanubhavantiye cānye sukhasaṃsaktā nityaṃ pāparatā narāḥ |duḥkhād duḥkhaṃ tu
te yānti cittena parivañcitāḥ

avadyakāryāṇi narake pātayantitasmādavadyakārye na matiṃ (kuryāt kathañcana) |hīnānyavadyakāryāṇi
narake pātayanti ca

ataḥ kuśalakarmāṇi karttavyāni(karmāṇi) kuryāt kuśalāni nityaṃparārthabaddhena manorathena |(hitaḥ
sadaivaṃ narakasya rodhī) |sevyo'pavargo daśadharma eṣaḥ

||iti narakavargaḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ ||

## 17 pretavargaḥ

17) pretavargaḥadānasya kutaḥ sukham?adāntasya kutaḥ śāntiranuptasya kutaḥ phalam?adīpikā prabhā
nāsti adānasya kutaḥ sukham

hṛcchāntirhi tathā nāsti nayanārthasya dehinaḥ |dānena virahāt tadvat sukhaṃ pretairna labhyate

kukarmī pretalokaṃ gacchatiyaṃ pretya pretalokasya triloke ca yathā'dhamāḥ |bhrameṇa paramodvignāstanmātsaryakṛtaṃ
phalam

na dātrā labhyate kiñcit kṛtanāśo na vidyate |svakarmaphalabhoktāraḥ prāṇinaḥ karmabhoginaḥ

tava yatkukṛtaṃ dagdhāḥ pretyalokeṣvavasthitāḥ |kṣutpipāsāmayeneha vahninā paridīpitāḥ

kadā tu viṣayo'smākaṃ bhaviṣyati sukhodayaḥ |paridāhāt kadā cāsmāt parimokṣo bhaviṣyati

mārgāmārgavihīno duḥkhita eva jīvatimārgāmārgavivikto'haṃ na jñātaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam |kṣutpipāsāmayo
vahnirajñātaḥ prakaṭodayaḥ

kleśāndhakāravadanā nirāśāstyaktajīvikāḥ |tvaksnāyujālabaddhāḥ sma jīvāmo bata duḥkhitāḥ

na trātā sarvato'smākaṃ hanta kṛcchragatā vayam |utpanneṣu manuṣyeṣu khaṇḍitāḥ svena karmaṇā

kasmānna carito dharmo ratnadīpeṣu sañcitaḥ |yaḥ karoti śubhaṃ nityamaśubhaṃ na ca sarvadā

samadṛṣṭipathenaiti svargasopānamāśritaḥ |yeṣāmarthe kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kleśayan mānasaṃ bahu

anena te gatāḥ sarve bhavānāṃ trāsane sthitaḥ |baddho'si bandhanaistīvrairyamadattairadhiṣṭhitaḥ

pāparajjvā''kṛṣyamāṇaḥ svakṛtaṃ bhunaktiprāpto'si tamaso ghoraṃ yamalokaṃ durāsadam |ihopabhokṣyase
karma yattvayā kukṛtaṃ kṛtam

svakṛtaṃ bhujyate bāla! pareṣāṃ naiva bhujyate |ākṛṣyamāṇa eṣo'tra pāparajjvā sughorayā

analaḥ pretalokasya kṣutpipāsāmayo mahānnāgniśastraviṣāṇāṃ hi nipātastādṛśaḥ kaṭuḥ

kṣutpipāsāmayāgneśca nipāto yādṛśaḥ (kaṭuḥ) |na kṣaṇo nāpi hi lavo na muhūrto na śarvarī

duḥkhānvitā vayamyatra saukhyaṃ bhavenmṛtyurnityaṃ duḥkhāvṛtā vayam |duḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ prāpto
duḥkhaheturniṣevitaḥ

duḥkhāt kadācinmokṣaḥ syād bhaviṣyati sukhodayaḥ |notsave dṛśyate toyaṃ taḍāgeṣu ca śuṣyati

karmaphalasvarūpanirūpaṇamsarito nāśamāyāsuḥ kathaṃ dhāvāmahe cayam |te vayaṃ śuṣkasalilāḥ saśailavanakānanāḥ

paridhāvāmahe bhūmau nityaṃ salilakāṃkṣiṇaḥ |te vayaṃ dagdhatanavaḥ kṣutpipāsāhatā narāḥ

śaraṇaṃ nādhigacchāmaḥ kaṣṭaṃ vyasanamāgatāḥ |vajradaṃṣṭrairmahātīkṣṇaiḥ kākolūkaiśca sammataḥ

abhidruto na paśyāmi śaraṇaṃ saukhyadāyakam |pratibimbamidaṃ tasya karmaṇaḥ samupasthitam

kṛtaṃ karma yathā'smābhistathedaṃ phalamāgatam |karmavāyuragā baddhāḥ karmasūtreṇa pācitāḥ

karmakṣayādṛte na muktiḥpalāyanaṃ na paśyāma ṛte karmakṣayāditi |yasya pāpānyaniṣṭāni vahnivat
tāni paśyati

kīdṛśaṃ pretabhavanam?sa naiti pretabhavanaṃ kṣutpipāsānalāvṛtam |muhurmuhuḥ pravardhante vedanā
narakodbhavāḥ

jvālāmālākulasyaiva śailasya sadṛśā vayam |jvalitaḥ śāmyate śailaḥ salilenaiva sarvathā

sa samudropamo vahnirammākaṃ naiva śāmyati |kasmin kṣaṇe samudbhūtastṛṣṇāvāyusahāyavān

karmāgnirnirdahatyasmān parivāryasamantataḥ |te vayaṃ pāpakarmāṇaḥ śukladharmavivarjitāḥ

pretalokamimaṃ prāptāḥ svargasya phalamohitāḥ |kṣutpipāsāmayo vahnirdvitīyaścāgnisambhavaḥ

kukarmaṇaiva mudgarāśimayā janāḥ pretabhavanaṃ gacchantimudgarāśimayā(dārāḥ) na putrāḥ na ca
bāndhavāḥ |vañcito'smi svacittena karmaṇā parivañcitā

prāpto'smi pretabhavanaṃ mitrajñātinirākṛtaḥ |na dārāḥ nāpi mitrāṇi na putrāḥ nāpi bāndhavāḥ

kukṛtaphalanirūpaṇamtrāyate karmapāśena nīyamānaṃ balīyasā |nānyastrātā yathā karma trividhaṃ
bhujyate mayā

dānaṃ śīlaṃ śrutaṃ cāpi trividhaṃ parikīrtitam |mohajālavṛteneha yanmayā kukṛtaṃ kṛtam

śubhakarmaṇaiva pretalokāt muktirbhavatikarmaṇo hetubhūtasya yatkṛtaṃ phalamāgatam |yadi mucyāmahe
pāpāt pretalokād durāsadāt

aśubhāni kukarmāṇi vivarjayetna bhūyaḥ pāpakaṃ karma kariṣyāmi kathañcana |uṣṇānyuṣṇavipākāni
mahāpīḍākarāṇi ca |kukarmāṇyaśubhānīha tasmāttāni vivarjayet

||iti pretavargaḥ saptadaśaḥ ||

## 18 tiryagvargaḥ

(18) tiryagvargaḥparadrohaṃ kadāpi na kuryātbhakṣaṇaṃ bhavadanyonyavadhabandhāvarodhanam |tiryagyoniṃ
samāsādya tasmād drohaṃ vivarjayet

mohopahatacittāśca śīladānavivarjitāḥ |tiryagyonau va jāyante bālāstṛṣṇāviḍambitāḥ

gamyāgamyaṃ na vindanti bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ tathaiva ca |kāryākāryabahirbhūtā dharmādharmatiraskṛtāḥ

pañcendriyajarāmūḍhāstṛṣṇāpāśavaśānugāḥ |krodherṣyāmatisaṃgrastāstiryagyonyupagā narāḥ

paryeṣṭyupahatā martyāḥ pramādopahatāḥ surāḥ |kṣuttarṣavyasanāḥ pretāḥ kāraṇātte ca nārakāḥ

ke tiryagyoniparāyaṇāḥ?parasparabadhātyuktāstiryagyoniparāyaṇāḥevaṃ bahuvidhaistaistairvyasanairākulaṃ
jagat

mātsaryopahato duṣkṛta na kuryātna kuryāda duṣkṛtaṃ karma mātsaryopahataḥ param |mātsaryopahatā
yānti pretāstiryakṣu jantavaḥ

keṣāṃ saphalaṃ janma?teṣāṃ hi saphalaṃ janma teṣāṃ buddhiravañcitā |te ca pūjyāḥ sadā sadbhiryeṣāṃ
dharme sadā matiḥ

jñānarathāvarūḍhairmuktirbhavatievaṃ tridoṣākṛtakarmasāraṃjagad bhramatyeva durāvagādham |karoti
yastasya ca śuddhasattvo'-vamānanāṃ jñānarathāvarūḍhaḥ

||iti tiryagvargo'ṣṭādaśaḥ ||

## 19 kṣadhāvargaḥ

(19) kṣadhāvargaḥkṣudhāpīḍitā narā aśubhaṃ kurvantisaṃsāre prāyaśo duḥkhaṃ traidhātukakṣudhāmayam
|yeneme pīḍitāḥ sattvāḥ kurvantyaśubhamātmanaḥ

svadehe yuktito vahnirbubhukṣetyadhīyate |śeṣo dahati lokān vai kalpānala iva drumān

lokottaramimaṃ vahniṃ gacchantamanugacchati |nātmanirvāṇasandṛṣṭaṃ kalpāntaraśatairapi

nā'nalasya hi tadvīrya kṣudhāyā yādṛśaṃ balam |traidhātukamidaṃ kṛtsnamāhāraṃ prati vartate

mohayed vividhā cintā loke manujasambhavāḥ |tāḥ sarvā bhojanārthāya bhavanti tribhavārṇave

duḥkhamanirvacanīyamantarābhavasaṃsthasya kṛṣyamāṇasya karmabhiḥ |dīrghāgamasya yad duḥkhaṃ
na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

avidyendhanamagnasya dahyamānasya coṣmaṇā |garbhasthasya hi yad duḥkha na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

āhārarasagṛddhasya nityaṃ tadgatacetasaḥ |yad duḥkhaṃ paragṛddhasya na ca varṇayituṃ kṣamam

dṛḍhā vidagdhamanasaḥ kāme vā taptacetasaḥ |yad bhavatyadhikaṃ duḥkhaṃ na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

apriyaiḥ saha saṃsargo viṣayāstasya nityaśaḥ |praṇaṣṭe hṛdi yad duḥkhaṃ na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

tṛṣṇāviṣavidagdhasya nityaṃ paryeṣaṇātmakam |āmṛtyu yad bhave duḥkhaṃ na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

avyucchinnaṃ bahuvidhaṃ yad duḥkhaṃ pāpamitrajam |apāyajanakaṃ yasya na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

vyasanaṃ putradārāṇāṃ yad dṛṣṭ(vā) hṛdi jāyate |narakāṇāṃ mahāmārgo na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

kṣutpipāsāvidagdhasya dīpyamānasya vahninā |yad duḥkhaṃ naṣṭamanaso na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

ajastraṃ paribhūtasya mitrajñātisuhṛjjanaiḥ |yad bhavecchokajaṃ duḥkhaṃ na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

dārapralambanagataṃ duścalaṃ priyacetasaḥ |yad duḥkhaṃ jīrṇakāyasya na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

mṛtyunā hriyamāṇasya tasmāṃllokātmano rasāt |yad duḥkhaṃ jāyate vṛttau na tad varṇayituṃ kṣamam

dānairaśubhanāśaḥyat kurvantyaśubhaṃ bālā yacca gacchati durgatim |tadāharati dānena kathayanti
manīṣiṇaḥ

ataḥ sukṛteṣu manaḥ kāryampratiduṣkṛtakarmāṇi varjanīyāni sarvadā |sukṛteṣu manaḥ kārya dānaśīlavibhūṣitam

||iti kṣudhāvarga ekonaviṃśaḥ ||

## 2 dharmopadeśavargaḥ

(2) dharmopadeśavargaḥguruḥ kīdṛgbhavet?yo hi deśayate dharma kṣemaṃ nirvāṇagāminam |sa sarvabandhanacchettā
gururbhavati dehinām

chittvā mohamayaṃ pāśaṃ yo hi deśayate śivam |sanmārgadeśakaḥ prokto durmārgavinivārakaḥ

dharmasya pravaratvamyena dharmeṇa manujāstaranti bhavasāgaram |saddharmaprabaraḥ prokto na
dharmo laukiko hi saḥ

kṣetrāṇīmāni catvāri vidyante yasya dehinaḥ |tasyedaṃ saphalaṃ janma kathitaṃ mārgadeśakaiḥ

sakalendriyatāṃ prāpya labdhvā buddhasya śāsanam |yo na dharmarato martyaḥ sa paścādanutapyate

yamasāyujyamvyāsaktamanasāṃ nityaṃ nityaṃ kāmagaveṣiṇām |putradāraprasaktānāmantako'bhyeti dehinām

cittasya vaśīkaraṇamsaṅkalpadoṣamanasaṃ taistairdoṣaiḥ samākulam |cittaṃ nayatyupāyena cittabaddhā
hi dehinaḥ

durdamasyāticaṇḍasya siddhiprepsorviśeṣataḥ |na cittasya vaśaṃ gacchet śatrubhūtaṃ hi tannṛṇām

saśrutena sudiṣṭena bhāvitenāpyanekaśaḥ |dharmeṇa śāmyate cittaṃ jālineva yathā hayaḥ

saddharmaśravaṇaphalamsaddharmaśravaṇaṃ kṛtvā pāpād viramate pumān |śreyase pratipattiṃ ca nityamevopapadyate

saddharmaśravaṇaṃ kṛtvā manaḥ prahalādamṛcchati |kuśalaṃ cāsya sumahat sandhāne sampratīkṣate

śrutvā bhavati dharmātmā śrutvā pāpaṃ na kurvate |śrutvā karmaphalaṃ jñātvā nirvāṇamadhigacchati

śrutvā vedayate dharma śrutvā buddhaḥ prasīdati |śrutvā dharmaṃ vimokṣāya yatate paṇḍito naraḥ

dharmalakṣaṇatattvajñaḥ śrutvā bhavati mānavaḥ |tasmācchrutvā prayatnena buddhiḥ kāryā prayatnataḥ

śrutvā saṃsāravimukhāṃ kathāṃ sugatadeśitām |prahāya tṛṣṇāṃ vividhāṃ prayāti padamavyayam

caturvidhāṃ pratyayitāṃ dharmāṇāṃ codamavyayau |śrutvā tāṃ jñāyate sarvā pumān śraddhāvibhāvitaḥ

skandhāyatanadhātūnāṃ yadetallakṣaṇadvayam |śrutvā tajjñāyate sarva tasmāt dharmaparo bhavet

prabalā ye trayo doṣāḥ sarvasaṃsārabandhanāḥ |te praṇaśyantyaśeṣeṇa saddharmaśravaṇena vai

pravṛtterlakṣaṇaṃ kṛtsnaṃ nivṛtteścāpyaśeṣataḥ |śrutimāṃstat prajānāti tasmācchrutamanuttamam

mṛtyukālopapannasya vedanārtasya dehinaḥ |na vyathā śrutamālambya svalpāpi hṛdi jāyate

samyagjñānena ye dagdhāḥ kleśavṛkṣāḥ samantataḥ |na teṣāmudbhavo bhūyaḥ kadācidupalabhyate |21||apramādavidagdhaṃ
hi śrutaṃ sarva sukhodayam |śreyasaśva śrutaṃ mūlaṃ tasmācchrutaparo
bhavet

saddharmaśravaṇaṃ śrutvā vṛddhāṃścopāsya paṇḍitaḥ |prayātyanuttamaṃ sthānaṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitam

dharmeṇa vartate śrutvā duḥkhānmucyeta vai yathā |śrutvā bhavati maitryātmā tasmācchreyaḥ paraṃ
śrutam

kāyavāṅmanasāṃ buddhiḥ śrutvā bhavati dehinām |tasmāt sadbuddhikāmo yastena śrotavyamādarāt

śrutvā bhāvaṃ samāśritya dṛḍhavīryaparākramam |taranti puruṣāstūrṇa tribhava vipulaṃ mahat

śrutvā yaḥ puruṣaḥ sarvairdhanavānabhijāyate |aśrutārthadhanairyuktaṃ daridraṃ prāhustaṃ budhāḥ
|27||saddharmanāśaphalamsaddharmadhananaṣṭasya guruṇā varjitasya ca |viphalaṃ jīvitaṃ
ceṣṭaṃ pāpairupahatasya ca

pramādādīnāmaniṣṭaphalasādhanatvampramādinaḥ kusīdastha pāpamitrasya dehinaḥ |jīvitaṃ niṣphalaṃ
dṛṣṭaṃ bījamuptaṃ yathosare

śāstraprāmāṇyamśrutadṛṣṭivinirmuktamarthamāhurvicakṣaṇāḥ |na cakṣurbhyāṃ vinirmuktamarthamityabhidhīyate

dharmasevanāgrahaḥyo hi dharma parityajya adharmamanutiṣṭhati |sa bhaiṣajyaṃ parityajya vyādhimevopasevate

sevatāṃ sevatāṃ puṃsāṃ dharmo bhavatyanekaśaḥ |varṣāṇāṃ samavāyena yathodyānaṃ pravardhate

iha vajrāsane bhūmirna saṃsāre'nyathā bhavet |bodhicittasamutthānaṃ bodhiprāptistathottamā

dharmacārī praśāntātmā kāyotthāyī samāhitaḥ |avaśyaṃ śubhabhāgīsyāt sa pramādena vañcitaḥ

jñānasevanāgrahaḥtasmājjñānaguṇānmatvā jñānaṃ seveta paṇḍitaḥ |nahyajñānena saṃyuktaṃ pumān
kaścit pratibhavet

dharmānusāriṇī śraddhādharmānusāriṇī śraddhā yathā yāti sukhāvahā |sātidurgatisanyaktā vyasaneṣu
mahad balam

pradīpakalpā tamasi vyādhitānāmivauṣadham |arthānāṃ netra bhūtā sā daridrāṇāṃ mahadbalam

bhavādyairhriyamāṇānāṃ pūrvabhūtā sukhāvahā |pramādamadamattānāṃ sā pramādavighātikā

śāntapadaprāpteḥ phalamsa hi yattatpadaṃ śāntaṃ nirvāṇamunibhirvṛtam |tat prāpayatyakhedena
samyagjñānapuraḥsaram

||iti dharmopadeśavargaḥ dvitīyaḥ ||

## 20 kausīdyavargaḥ

(20) kausīdyavargaḥkausīdyena jñānahāniḥkausīdyamatimāyā ca dambhaḥ pāruṣyameva ca |niyātabhūmayo
dṛṣṭā jñānasya ca vivarjanam

saṃśleṣaścāpyasādhūnāṃ sādhūnāṃ varjanaṃ tathā |nāśasya hetavaḥ śaktā mithyādarśanameva ca

adeśakālasaṃraṃbho vācyāvācyamajānataḥ |anarthabhūmayo hyetā visrambhaścāpi tanmayaḥ

anarthabhūmayaḥyena tena ca samprītiḥ yatra tatra ca bhojanam |lāghavaṃ janayatyante'praśaṃsā
cātmanastathā

dhairyanāśaḥ smṛtibhraṃśo virodhaḥ pārthivena ca |akrāntamṛtyavo hyete krūratā manasi sthitā

akarmāphalatattvajño dharmādharmavahiṣkṛtaḥ |puruṣaḥ sādhunirmuktaḥ prapātagamanāśayaḥ

kausīdyamatinidrā ca rasanā gṛhyate tathā |puṃdveṣayonayaḥ proktāḥ pāruṣyavacanaṃ tathā

lobho'pamānasya kāraṇamatilobho'pamānaśca atimānaśca cāpalam |dharmavarjyā kāmasevā mohasya
paridīpikāḥ

doṣāṇāṃ mūlaṃ kausīdyamtrayāṇāmiha doṣāṇāṃ kausīdyaṃ mūlamucyate |vīryārambheṇa duṣyante doṣā
manasi sambhavāḥ

vīryārambheṇa hi phalaṃ hyavaśyamupabhujyate |nyāyenārabdhatattvasya karmaṇo dṛśyate phalam

karmaṇastrividhasyāsya phalaṃ trividhamucyate |trirāśiniyataṃ tacca triśūlaṃ tribhavānugam

kausīdyasevino durgatiḥpāpasevī pracaṇḍo yaḥ kausīdyamapi sevate |dharmavidveṣakaḥ krūro'nutpathānupadhāvati

yasya tasya ca santuṣṭo yasya tasya prakupyati |yatra tatra ca saṃsakto sa mūḍha iti kathyate

kausīdyaṃ (yat) svamanasaḥ pramādaviṣamūrcchitam |prapātaṃ taṃ ca saṃrabdhamavisaṃvādakaṃ param

vīryārambhe mahāpāpakausīdyamalavarjitāḥ |vimukterupabhoktāraste janāḥ sukhabhāginaḥ

kausīdyaṃ sarvadharmāṇāmajarāmarakārakam |tena doṣeṇa mahatā narā duḥkhasya bhāginaḥ

sahāyaśca sukhāveśī tasmāt tat parivarjayet |tena viddho hi puruṣaḥ svadhistutyaḥ samantataḥ

kusīdasyālpabhāgasya mohāpahṛtacetasaḥ |kutsitaḥ svajanaiḥ sarvairna gatirvidyate śivā

kausīdyarataḥ pāpībhavatikausīdyapāpasaṃsargīstyānamiddhaṃ tathaiva ca |mokṣadvāravighātāya
bhavantyete mahābhayāḥ

duḥkhasyaitāni harmyāṇiāhrīkyamanapatrāpyamauddhatyaṃ pāpamitratā |duḥkhasyaitāni harmyāṇi tebhyo
rakṣennu paṇḍitaḥ

kausīdyenābhibhūtā ye nirārambhā gatitviṣaḥ |socchvāsamaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ jīvitaṃ cāpi niṣphalam

jīvamānā na jīvanti kausīdyopahatā narāḥ |mṛtyoratyadhikaṃ hyetat kausīdyamiti manyate

ārabdhavīryā eva bhavasāgaraṃ tarantikausīdyapaṅkamagnā ye magnāste duḥkhasaṃstare |ārabdhavīryā
ye puṃsaste tīrṇā bhavasāgarāt

kausīdyānmandavīryo yaḥ sadā pāparataśca yaḥ |sa jīvamāno'pi mṛto mṛtastu narakāya saḥ

mānavānāṃ nirdhanatve kausīdyaṃ kāraṇamnirdhanāḥ paśubhistulyāste narā duḥkhabhāginaḥ |parapiṇḍāśino
dīnāḥ kausīdyaṃ tatra kāraṇam

prāyaśastu kusīdānāṃ paradāropajīvinām |ratābhilāṣo'tyadhiko maithune ca sadā ratiḥ

te tattvakārikā riktāḥ kevalāhāratatparāḥ |mṛtyukāle samutpanne dahyante svena cetasā

śītoṣṇaṃ ca sahantyete kṣutpipāse tathaiva ca |gātrāntā ca kriyā kāryā yātrā dharmāya sarvadā

ataḥ kausīdye na matiḥ kāryāna kausīdye matiṃ kuryāt kuśīle śīlakāmukaḥ |saṃsāre sīdati nityaṃ
na ca duḥkhāt pramucyate

kudīdānvitaḥ lokavañcito bhavatiparibhūya satāṃ madhye kusīdāllokavañcitaḥ |vañcitaśca bhavatyante
śarmaṇo vā vimucyate

dharmeṇa vimuktirbhavativīryavān smṛtisaṃlabdha ekāntanirataḥ sadā |vimuktapāpakaidharmairmokṣaṃ
prāpnoti yatnataḥ

kukarmeṣu matiḥ na kāryāevaṃvidhā duḥkhaparamparā hi,sattvaḥ kukarmeṣu (matiṃ) na kuryāt |loke
tridoṣānalasampradīpte,kuryāt parāṃ śāntikṛpā mṛte na

||iti kausīdyavargo viṃśaḥ ||cittañca vāk tathā karma saṃyojanantu pāpakam |naraka-preta-tiryak-kṣut-kausīdyāni
vidurdaśa ||||iti dvitīyam udānam ||

## 21 karuṇāvargaḥ

atha tṛtīyam udānam(karuṇā-dāna-śīlāni kṣāntirvīryamathāpi ca |dhyānaṃ prajñā'tha nirvāṇo mano
bhikṣuśca te daśa || )(21) karuṇāvargaḥkaruṇā māteva hitakāriṇī bhavatikṛpā sarveṣu bhūteṣu
māteva hitakāriṇī |yāṃ samāśritya puruṣāḥ prayānti sukhamuttamam

dayānveṣīha puruṣaḥ sarvasattvahite rataḥ |pūjanīyaḥ satāṃ yāti pretya svarge ca modate

dayāvantaḥ sadāvarta sarvabhūtahite ratam |tamevaṃ puruṣaṃ nityaṃ praṇamanti divaukasaḥ

karuṇānvito devalokaṃ gacchatiprayāti devalokaṃ ca śīlavān karuṇānvitaḥ |kṛpānvitaḥ sa puruṣo
candramā iva śobhate

sukhārthinā karuṇā sevitavyāāśrayanti ca bhūtāni gataśokā gatavyathā |tasmād dayāprayatnena
sevitavyā sukhārthinā

kāruṇyena yaśavṛddhirbhavatiyasya vāk kāyacitte (ca) kāruṇyena vibhūṣite |tasya mitramayā lokā
bhavanti yaśasā vṛtāḥ

kāruṇyārdrasya viduṣo nirvāṇaṃ yātikāruṇyārdrasya viduṣo nityaṃ mṛdvindriyasya ca |samyagdṛṣṭiprayatnasya
nirvāṇaṃ nāti dūrataḥ

kāruṇyavibhūṣitā manuṣyalokaṃ devavad bhūṣayantimanuṣyaloke te devā ye kāruṇyena vibhūṣitāḥ
|kāruṇyena daridrā ye te daridrāḥ satāṃ matāḥ

mṛdvāśayā martyāḥ sādhavaḥmṛdvāśayā hi ye martyāḥ sādhuvat kāñcanopamāḥ |kāruṇyamakṣayaṃ yeṣāṃ
sadā manasi vartate

ke dharmaparāyaṇā bhavanti?te ca sattvāḥ sadodyuktā nityaṃ dharmaparāyaṇāḥ |yeṣāṃ kāruṇyadīpena
hṛdayaṃ samprakāśitam

na rātrau na divā teṣāṃ dharmo hi vinivartate |yeṣāṃ sarvāsvavasthāsu karuṇābhirataṃ matam

kāruṇyaṃ śītalaṃ cittamkāruṇyaśītalaṃ cittaṃ sarvasattvahite ratam |bhuktvā saukhyaṃ nirupamaṃ
paścād gacchati nirvṛtim

kāruṇyamavināśi dhanamkāruṇyaṃ munibhiḥ śastaṃ kāruṇyaṃ nirmalaṃ saraḥ |kāruṇyaṃ doṣanirghāti
kāruṇyaṃ dhanamavyayam

guṇānāṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ cāgraṃ sarvadoṣavighātakam |kāruṇyārdrā hi paramaṃ prayānti dhanamacyutam

kāruṇyaṃ (vai) dhanaṃ yasmānmādhuryapayasā yutam |na dāhaḥ krodhajastasya hṛdaye sampravartate

kāruṇyanāvamāruhya janā bhavasāgaraṃ tarantikāruṇyanāvamāruhya prītirdhairyaparāyaṇaḥ |tridoṣormimahāvege
bhrāmyate bhavasāgare

karuṇāyāḥ paribhāṣāguṇānāmadvayaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vinā cittena bhūṣaṇam |sādhūnāṃ dayitaṃ nityaṃ kāruṇyamiti
kathyate

mārdavaṃ yasya hṛdaye vilīnamiva kāñcanam |sa jano hi tu kalpānte duḥkhādāśu vimucyate

dayāloḥ śreyāṃsi rohantiyasya pātrīkṛtaṃ cittaṃ mārdaveṇa samantataḥ |śreyāṃsi tasya rohanti
kedāra iva śālayaḥ

cetogṛhe nidhānaṃ tadavyayaṃ (sarva) dehinaḥ |nirvāsayati dāridrayaṃ nṛṇāmadhyāśayaṃ mahat

tīkṣṇendriyasyāśāntasya nidhyānasya vicāriṇaḥ |viṣayeṣu pramattasya duḥkhaṃ naiva pradhāvati

maitreṇa cetasā nityamanukampā-dayā parāḥ |te hetuphalatattvajñā duḥkhapāśād vinirgatāḥ

na saṅkalpe mano yeṣāṃ ramate doṣavarjitam |te doṣabhayanirmuktāḥ padaṃ gacchantyanuttaram

kṣāntikriyāsamāyukte mitravānakutobhayaḥ |priyo bhavati loke'smin paścād deveṣu modate

dayāratnaṃ sadā sevyammātṛvat pitṛvaccaiva sarvalokasya te janāḥ |dayāratnaṃ sadā yeṣāṃ manasi
sthitamuttamam

kṛpaiva sukhasya mūlamastisukhasya ca paraṃ mūlaṃ kṛpaiva parikīrtitā |(hṛdi ) yasya kṛpā nāsti
sa duḥkhī parikīrtyate

maitrī eva sukhāvahāekasatyottaraṃ brahma ekasyānuttaraṃ śivam |ekavidyā paraṃ mātā maitrī caikā
sukhāvahā

ahiṃsakā eva dhanyāḥahiṃsakāḥ sadā dhanyāḥ saddṛṣṭiḥ paramā śubhā |etad ṛju sadā satyaṃ pāpānāṃ
cāpi varjanam

karuṇāyā māhātmyamkarttavyaḥ puruṣaistasmāt kṛpāsaṃvegamānasaiḥ |dāna-śīla-kṣamā-maitrī-jñānābhyāsaśca
nirmalaḥ

||iti karuṇāvarga ekaviṃśaḥ ||

## 22 dānavargaḥ

(22)dānavargaḥśuddhāśuddhadānaparibhāṣāguṇadvādaśasaṃyuktaṃ malairdvādaśabhirvinā |dānaṃ bhavati
śuddhaṃ tad viparītaṃ sakalmaṣam

dānarahitā eva puruṣādhamāḥdevānāmatha (vā) nṛṇāṃ dhanasya balamuttamam |dānena rahitā pāde
patanti puruṣādhamāḥ

lobhamātsaryamalināḥ putradāravaśānugāḥ |manujā nidhanaṃ yānti kevalāhārakāṃkṣiṇaḥ

lobhagranthivimokṣāya yāñcāvṛkṣakṣayāya ca |tamonicayanāśāya pradānamiha dīyate

dātā paralokaṃ gacchatiaghatāṃ carate dānaṃ dātāpi tadanantaram |mārgasandarśavad dānaṃ paralokaṃ
samṛcchati

dānāmbhasi narāḥ snātvā śīlormiparibhāvite |jñānavistīrṇa(vimalaṃ) pāraṃ duḥkhasya yānti vai

doṣanāśakāḥ trayo dīpāḥpuruṣeṇa trayo dīpāḥ prajvālyā hitamicchatā |dānaṃ śīlaṃ tathā jñānamete
doṣavināśakāḥ

tṛṣṇāvivarṇā durgandhivitarkormijhaṣākule |duḥkhārṇave plutā hyete jñānaśīleṣu rakṣitāḥ

kleśasya bheṣajaṃ dānaṃ, śīlaṃ, jñānamkleśavyādhinihantārastrayo vai bheṣajāḥ smṛtāḥ |dānaṃ
śīlaṃ tathā jñānamete nityaṃ sukhāvahāḥ

pramādaviṣamaṃ (cittaṃ) saṃkalpakuṭilaṃ laghu |vadhyate bandhanairetaistribhirjñānādibhirnṛṇām

doṣāgnibhiḥ (sadā) pluṣṭo yairidaṃ dahyate tadā |dānādijñānayogena hatvā gacchati nirvṛtim

na dāna-jñāna śīleṣu yeṣāṃ sammiśritā matiḥ |te nityaduḥkhitāḥ sattvāḥ sukhaṃ teṣāṃ na vidyate

dātāro mātsaryamalavarjitā bhavantiadānavrīḍitasukhāścittadoṣeṇa vañcitāḥ |bhavanti vibudhāḥ
hitvā tasmād dānaparo bhavet

dānotkarṣakramairyuktāḥ mātsaryamalavarjitāḥ |bhavanti hṛṣṭamanaso devāḥ krīḍāparāyaṇāḥ

kṣutpipāsāmayo vahnirya pradhānaṃ pradhāvati |mātsarya vai phalaṃ sarva taduktaṃ tattvabuddhibhiḥ

dānapraśaṃsāyo dadāti sukhaṃ tasya nīyate jāyate sukham |sukhaṃ bhavati dānāddhi tasmād dānaṃ
praśasyate

lokālokakaraṃ dānaṃ gacchantamanugacchati |gataṃ ca mantradānena yujyate bhadravatsalaiḥ

avisaṃvādakaṃ sthānametaduktaṃ tathāgataiḥ |avisaṃvādakatvācca nityaṃ dānaparo bhavet

dānī bhavārṇavaṃ taratimātsaryāriṃ vinirjitya kṛtvā cittaṃ śubhānvitam |ye prayacchanti dānāni
te taranti bhavārṇavam

kṣayaistu trividhairdānaṃ triṣukāraṃ tridhā'rjitam |tasya kṣatānvitasyaivaṃ phalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tricakṣuṣā

kaḥ mārgaḥ sukhāvahaḥ?dānamādau sadā deyaṃ śīlaṃ labhyaṃ prayatnataḥ |tṛṣṇā jñānena hantavyā
mārga eṣa sukhāvahaḥ

anityā pāpikā tṛṣṇā lokasyāhitakārikā |na śakyaṃ tadvinā śreyaḥ prāptuṃ padamanuttamam

adānasya pariṇāmaḥadāne na manaḥ kārya nityaṃ dānarato bhavet |adānāt kṣutpipāsābhyāṃ dahyate
pretabhūmiṣu

dānena śīlarakṣā sadā kāryārājā bhavati dānena cakravartī sudhārmikaḥ |dānabhūmiṃ samāśritya
śīlaṃ rakṣanti paṇḍitāḥ

śīlavānapi kālajño'jñānād (vai) parimucyate |duḥkhanairyāṇiko mārgaḥ śasto'yaṃ munipuṃgavaiḥ

taṃ viditvā mahāvīro nityaṃ dānarato bhavet |adā(nā)dapi deveṣu devā hi na sukhā matāḥ

dānī yatra kutrāpi vasan sukhī bhavatiājanmavipine martyā bhavanti sukhabhāginaḥ |dānasya tatphalaṃ
sarvacetanābhāvitasya hi

tiryakṣvapi samutpannā bhavanti sukhabhāginaḥ |tatsarva dānajaṃ saukhyaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ

yat pretāḥ pretabhavane bhavatyākārabhojinaḥ |svayaṃ kṛtasya dānasya phalaṃ bhavati tādṛśam

dānavirahitasya durdaśā bhavati(na)dadyāt kṣutpipāse ca dahyante yena dehinaḥ |sarvadā na tapastābhyāṃ
phalaṃ bhavati śītalam

pramādī mṛtyusamaye dāhaṃ prāpnotipūrva pramādacārī yo na dānādiṣu vartate |sa paścānmṛtyusamaye
dahyate svena cetasā

dānasya phalampriyo bhavati dānena cetasāmapi tuṣyati |paścād bhavati sa śrīmān dānasya phalamīdṛśam

yatra dānādi cittasyāstyupabhogāya sarvadā |tatra nirdhanataiṣā vā dayayā parirakṣitam

yad bhujyate sadā cittaṃ gurubhyaścāpi dīyate |yad vanaṃ śobhanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ viparītaṃ yathā tṛṇam

durbalānāṃ sadārtānāṃ sattvānāṃ cakṣuranvitam |dānaṃ niḥkalpasaṃyantramasmiṃlloke paratra ca

manuṣyabhūmau dānasya phalammanuṣyabhūmau dānāni dattvā yānti śubhāṃ gatim |na devā dānapatayaḥ
phalabhūmirasau matā

karmamāhātmyamkarmabhūmirmanuṣyāṇāṃ phalabhūmiḥ surālayaḥ |karmāyattaṃ phalaṃ sarva na phalaṃ
syādahetukam

ko mṛtaiḥ samaḥ?dhyānādhyayananirmukto dānaśīlavivarjitaḥ |suvarṇakaṃkaṇairyukto jīvannapi mṛtaiḥ
samaḥ

sa jīvati hi loke'smin yo dharmamanuvartate |dharmamūḍhaḥ sadā mūḍho jīvannapi mṛtaiḥ samaḥ

ajñānī tu bāliśa evamanuṣyacarmaṇā channastiryag bhavati bāliśaḥ |yasya jñānapradīpena hṛdayaṃ
nāvabhāsitam

bhavatyetāvatā puruṣaḥ yaḥ śīlamanuvartate |śīlabhraṣṭaḥ pumān sarvaścābhistutyo'parākramaḥ

dānahīnaḥ pretavigrahavāndānahīnaḥ pramādī (ca) pāpacārī calendriyaḥ |nāsau martya iti jñeyaḥ
preto vigrahavānayam

jñānena hīno mṛta evajñānaśīlavinirmukto dānaratnavivarjitaḥ |jīvamāno'pi puruṣo mṛta ityabhidhīyate

ko devaḥ?dānaśīlatapodhyānād vīryasmṛtisamādhimān |puruṣaḥ puruṣaireje devairapi sa vandyate

guṇavāṃstu naro vandyaḥ nirguṇaḥ paśubhiḥ samaḥ |guṇāguṇavidhijño yaḥ sa deva iti kathyate

keṣāṃ saphalaṃ jīvanam?sujīvitaṃ bhavettasya yasya tyāge sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi tyāgavinirmuktaṃ
jīvitaṃ jīvanaṃ matam
