dānaṃ nityaṃ sukhāvahampañcagatyupapannānāṃ sattvānāṃ svena karmaṇā |mātṛvat pitṛvad dṛṣṭaṃ
dānaṃ nityaṃ sukhāvaham

dānaratā bhavasaṅkaṭānmucyanteetāṃ bhūmimavasthāpya sattvo dānarato bhavet |dānaśīlaratā nityaṃ
mucyante bhavasaṅkaṭāt

||iti dānavargo dvāviṃśaḥ ||

## 23 śīlavargaḥ

23) śīlavargaḥśīlaṃ sūrya iva śobhatedhanānāmuttamaṃ śīlaṃ sūryo jyotiṣmatāmiva |vihāya gacchati
dhanaṃ śīlaṃ sthitamivāgrataḥ

śīlena tridaśān yāti dhyānagocarameva vā |nāsti śīlasamaṃ jyotirasmiṃlloke paratra ca

alpena hetunā svarga prāpnoti svargakāmikaḥ |tasmād duścaritaṃ hitvā nityaṃ sucarito bhavet

cetanābhāvitaṃ dānaṃ śīlaṃ ca parirakṣitam |nīyate devasandattaṃ pañcakāmaguṇānvitam

surakṣitena śīlenaiva sukhaṃ prāptuṃ śakyatena mātā na pitā nārthā dayitā nāpi bāndhavāḥ |na
sukhā (ste) tathā dṛṣṭā yathā śīlaṃ surakṣitam

śīlavān puruṣo sukhamavāpnuteśīlaṃ trāṇamihāmutra śīlaṃ gatirihottamam |śīlavān puruṣo nityaṃ
sukhāt sukhamavāpnute

dānaśīlasamācārā ye narā śubhacāriṇaḥ |te yānti devasadanaṃ racitāḥ svena karmaṇā

nidhānamavyayaṃ śīlaṃ śīlasaukhyamatarkitam |śīlādhikā hi puruṣā nityaṃ sukhavihāriṇaḥ

śīlaṃ rakṣatu medhāvī yathā yānaṃ sukhatrayam |praśaṃsāvṛttalābhaṃ ca pretya svarge ca modate

śīlavān nirvāṇaṃ prāpnotiśīlavān yo hi puruṣaḥ śīlamevāti sevate |sasukho nirvṛttiṃ yāti yatra
mṛtyurna vidyate

anādimati saṃsāre tṛṣṇāmohādibhirvṛte |jyotirbhūta sadāśīlaṃ tasmācchīlamanāvilam

śīlaṃ dhanamasaṃhārya rājacaurodakādibhiḥ |tasmācchīlaṃ sadā sevyaṃ dauḥśīlyaṃ ca vigarhitam

śīlābhiratapuruṣaḥ nirvāṇaṃ hyantike sthitam |śīlavān puruṣo dhanyaḥ śīlavāṃścāpi sevyate

ravivad bhrājate śīlaṃ dauḥśīlyaṃ caiva garhitam |nirmalaṃ vītakāntāraṃ nirjvaraṃ vītakāṅkṣi
ca

śīlapraśastasambuddhairnirvāṇapuragāmikam |āyuryāti dhruvaṃ dhīmān nityaṃ śīlenaṃ vṛṃhitam

śīlarahitāḥ paśubhiḥ samāḥna bibhenmṛtyukāle ca śīlena parirakṣitaḥ |śīlamādyantakalyāṇaṃ sarvasaukhyapravartakam
|śīlavān puruṣo dhanyo dauḥśīlyābhirataḥ paśuḥ

tīraṃ naiva samāyānti puruṣāḥ śīlavarjitāḥ |kāryākārya na vindanti tasmācchīlaṃ samācaret

śīlavastreṇa ye channāste channāḥ puruṣā matāḥ |śīlena varjitā ye tu nagnāste paśubhiḥ samāḥ

śīlavān puruṣaḥ svarga gacchatiśīlavān puruṣaḥ svagamudyānamiva gacchati |bandhuvanmanyate tatra
śīlavān (su) pramāgataḥ

śuciśīlasamācārāḥ śubhadharmasamanvitāḥ |devalokopagāsteṣu janāḥ sukṛtakāriṇaḥ

śīlena paribṛṃhitā guṇā vardhanteyo na prārthayate kāmān śīlavān puruṣaḥ sadā |guṇāstasya pravardhante
śīlena paribṛṃhitāḥ

śīlaṃ svargasya sopānammahārghamuttamaṃ śīlamasmiṃlloke paratra ca |tasmāt prahāya traiguṇyaṃ
śīlameva sadā caret

devebhyo rocate taddhi trāṇaṃ śīlaṃ śubhānvitam |bhāvitaṃ paramaṃ dhanyaṃ paralokopagāmikam

śīlavān yadi jānīyāt phalaṃ śīlasya yādṛśam

śastraṃ sutīkṣṇamādāya vāṇaṃ chindyādihātmanaḥ |astropamasya nindyasya abhisaukhyasamanvitam

śīlasya phalaṃ sugatena pradarśitamphalaṃ śīlasya vimalaṃ sugatena pradarśitam |ādau śastaṃ
tathā madhye nidhane śastameva tat

phalaṃ śīlasya vipulaṃ sukhāt sukhamuttamam |śīlacaryā paraṃ saukhyaṃ dhanacaryā na tādṛśī

narā dhanena hīyante śīlena na kathañcana |śocate puruṣastena pṛthak vā tadvirājate

śubhaṃ tasmānmunivaraiḥ praśastaṃ sārvagāmikam |udyānamiva gacchanti puruṣāḥ śubhacāriṇaḥ |devalokasamaṃ
teṣāṃ saukhyānāmākaraṃ (param)

svargagamanārtha śīlaṃ samācaretsuśīlitasya śīlasya bhakṣitasyāpyanekaśaḥ |phalaṃ vipacyate
svargastasmācchīlaṃ samācaret

śīlaṃ svargasya sopānamākaraṃ sukhanirvṛte |śīlavarjī hi puruṣo na kvacit sukhamedhate

śīlavān asaṃkhyāni saukhyāni labhateśīlāmbhasā prasannena viprakīrṇena sarvadā |snātvā gacchanti
puruṣā devaloke ca nirvṛte |yaddivyamālyābharaṇairdivyaiḥ saukhyaiḥ samanvitāḥ

ramate devabhavane tat sarva śubhahetukam |asaṃkhyāni ca saukhyāni vardhamānāni sarvadā

labhate puruṣaḥ sarva yaḥ śīlamanuvartate |śubhacārī sadā satyaḥ pūjyate so'parājitaḥ

anekasaukhyadāyakaṃ śīlamācaraṇīyamśubhena śobhate martyaḥ pūjyate rājabhiḥ sadā |śubhena śobhate
martyastasmācchīlaṃ samācaret

anekasaukhyajanakaṃ sarvamāśvāsakārakam |śīlaṃ sucaritaṃ kārya duṣkṛtaṃ ca vivarjayet

ye dānaśīlakarttāraḥ svargatadgatamānasāḥ |teṣāṃ sakalmaṣaṃ śīlaṃ viṣamiśraṃ yathaudanam

nānāvidhasya śīlasya rakṣitasyāpyanekaśaḥ |śubhakāryavipākāya deveṣu paripacyate

śīlāmbhasā prasanneṣu saṅkīrṇeṣu ca sarvadā |snātvā gacchanti manujā devāṃścātyantikaṃ sukham

dānaśīlāḥ sadā dāntāḥ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ |jñānayuktā maitracitā gatāste devasammitim

hatadoṣāḥ kriyāvantaḥ śīlaratnena bhūṣitāḥ |sarvasattvadayāvantaḥ suralokeṣu te budhāḥ

viśuddhakāñcanaprakhyā nirdhmātamalakalmaṣāḥ |samyak karma susaṃlagnā devalokeṣu te budhāḥ

sarvasattvadayāvantaḥ sarvasattvahitaiṣiṇaḥ |sarvapāpaviraktā ye teṣāṃ vāsaḥ surālaye

ahanyahani ye śīlaṃ rakṣanti suparīkṣakāḥ |ahanyahani teṣāṃ hi sukhaṃ bhavati naikaśaḥ

śīlavājinamārūḍhā devabhavanaṃ prayāntiśīlavājinamārūḍhāḥ puruṣāstattvacintakāḥ |prayānti devabhavanaṃ
krīḍāyuktamanekaśaḥ

yā krīḍā devabhavane yacca saukhyamanuttamam |tat samagrasya śīlasya phalamuktaṃ tathāgataiḥ

devasukhaṃ śīlajamevayaddivyamālyābharaṇā divyāmbaravibhūṣitāḥ |krīḍanti vibudhāḥ sarve tatsarva
śubhahetukam

padmotpalavane ramye vanopavanabhūṣite |svarge ramanti ye devāstat sarva śubhajaṃ phalam

yadākāśa ivātasthurdivyaratnavibhūṣitāḥ |virājante'malā devāstacchīlasya mahat phalam

yatkānaneṣu ramyeṣu citreṣu puṣpiteṣu ca |ramanti giripṛṣṭheṣu surāstacchīlajaṃ phalam

svagṛhaṃ hi yathā martyāḥ praviśanti gatavyathāḥ |tathā śīlasamācārāḥ prayānti tridivaṃ narāḥ

etat sujīvitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ yacchīlaparirakṣaṇam |maraṇānāṃ paraṃ mṛtyuḥ yacchīlaparivarjanam

śīlamanupamaṃ kāryametān guṇān sadā matvā priyatvamapi cātmanaḥ |śīlaṃ surakṣitaṃ kārya dauḥśīlyaṃ
ca vivarjayet

śīlacārī sadā dāntaḥ kṣamāvāṃśca sudarśanaḥ |sopānamiva cārūḍhaṃ prayātyānandasannidhim

śīlena plavabhūtena saṃsārottaraṇamphalaṃ śīlasya tu sukhaṃ devalokeṣu pacyate |śīlena plavabhūtena
saṃsārāduttaranti ca

śīlāmbhasā viśuddhā ye svāyattā dhīracetasaḥ |jāmbūnadamayaiḥ puṣpaiste'trārcanti divaukasaḥ

ye navādātamanaso nityaṃ śīlena bhūṣitāḥ |te yānti devasadanaṃ yatra saukhyamanantakam

saukhyāt saukhyataraṃ yānti narāḥ sukṛtakāriṇaḥ |krīḍanti devasadane śīlena paribṛṃhitāḥ

śīlasopānamārūḍhāḥ sugatiṃ prayāntiśīlasopānamāruhya jñānena paribṛṃhitāḥ |narāḥ prayānti sugatiṃ
jñānena ca parāyaṇā

suprasannena manasāśīlaṃ yadabhirakṣitam |tasya śīlasya śītasva sukhametadupasthitam

śīlasya pariṇāmo sukhadāyakaḥsurakṣitasya śīlasya bhāvitasyāpyanekaśaḥ |pariṇāme sukhībhūtvā
nirvāṇaṃ cādhigacchati

śīlaṃ rakṣatyupāyena śīlaṃ nayati saṅgatim |tasmācchīlaṃ sadā rakṣyaṃ pariṇāmo'sya śītalaḥ

mṛtyukāle samutpanne śīlavānakutobhayaḥ |na me durgatinā trāṇaṃ śīlaṃ hi trāṇamuttamam

kutsitaśīlasya kutsitaḥ pariṇāmaḥkācābhrapaṭalaṃ yasya śīlaṃ bhavati kutsitam |sa kutsitena
śīlena kutsito jāyate naraḥ

śīlavirahitaḥ mūḍho bhavati svargamapi na yātiśīlamūlena labdhvedaṃ sukhaṃ svargeṣu dehibhiḥ
|tṛṣṇākṣayo na bhavati sa paścāt paritapyate

tasmācchīlavatā nityaṃ śīlameva viśiṣyate |niḥśīlaḥ puruṣo mūḍho na svargamadhirohati

pañcakāmopamaṃ divyaṃ yadidaṃ bhujyate sukham |tacchīlasya viśuddhasya prāpyate hi phalaṃ mahat

yattejaḥ kāñcanasyāsya meruparvataśālinaḥ |tacchīlatejasastejaḥ kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm

dīpyamānaiḥ sadā śīlaiḥ nirdhātukanakatviṣā |saṃyuktāstridivaṃ yānti paṇḍitā svena karmaṇā

trividhaśīlasya trividhaṃ phalamhīnamadhyaviśiṣṭasya śīlasya trividhasya vai |phalaṃ hi trividhaṃ
dṛṣṭaṃ hīnamadhyottamaṃ tathā

pramādarahitaṃ śīlamapramādopabṛṃhitam |nityaṃ tat sukhadaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ dharmateyaṃ vyavasthitā

śīlaprabhayā sūryasahastrasyādi parābhavaḥśīlodbhavā yā vimalā prabhā bhavati dehinām |na sā
sūryasahastrasya saṃyuktasya bhaviṣyati

śīlaṃ saptavidhaṃ ramyaṃ yo rakṣati narottamaḥ |sa kāmaṃ bhuñjati phalaṃ sugatena ca deśitam

śīlacaryā vinā svarga na yāntiśīlacaryā samāśritya samyagdarśanatatparaḥ |martyalokād divaṃ
yānti na kaṣṭaṃ tapacāriṇaḥ

yacchīlaṃ śīlasaṃsparśa pariṇāme'pi śītalam |niṣevate sadāmūḍhaḥ sa paścāt paritapyate

saptavidhena śīlena devasānnidhyaṃ prāpyateśīlaṃ saptavidhaṃ dhanyamavisaṃvādakaṃ padam |śīlena
rakṣitaḥ puruṣo devānāmantikaṃ gataḥ

śīlena śobhanaṃ phalaṃ milatiyathā pakṣairdṛḍhaiḥ pakṣī svedacchatraṃ nihanti (vai) |tathā naro
dṛḍhaḥ śīlairdevalokāya kalpyate

śrutimātraṃ ca (tacchīlaṃ) ramyād ramyataraṃ ca tat |labhate puruṣaḥ kartā phalaṃ śīlasya śobhanam

dāna-śīla-taporatnaṃ hṛdayaiśca samāśritam |devatā vā manuṣyo vā labhate paramaṃ padam

antarbahiśca niḥsārāḥ puruṣā dharmavarjitāḥ |saṃsārāt phalakāṅkṣibhyaḥ saddharmo na (ca) rocate

antarbahiśca ye sārāste narā vastuto dṛḍhāḥ |ye dharmacāriṇaḥ śāntāḥ parasattvahitaiṣiṇaḥ

anuttaraḥ śīlavatāṃ sugandhaḥna ketakī campaka-puṣpagandhā,tamālake nāgarucaśca gandhaḥ |prayānti
gandhā hiṃ yathā sureṇa,anuttaraḥ śīlavatāṃ sugandhaḥ

dauḥśīlyaṃ sadā varjyamtasmācchīlaṃ sadā kārya dānajñānatapodhanaiḥ |dauḥśīlyaṃ ca sadā varjyaviṣaśastrānalopanam

śīlena eva sukhamavāpnuteevaṃ surakṣitaṃ śīlaṃ narān nayati saṅgatim |na hi śīlādṛte kaścit
padaṃ sukhamavāpnute

devaguṇasadṛśaṃ śīlaṃ sadā''carettasmād devaguṇaṃ matvā śīlameva sadā''caret |na śīlasadṛśaṃ
kiñcidanyat trāṇamihāsti vai

||iti śīlavargastrayoviṃśaḥ ||

## 24 kṣāntivargaḥ

(24) kṣāntivargaḥkṣamābhūṣaṇenaiva bhūṣito bhavati pumānkṣāntyā vibhūṣitaḥ jīva bhūṣito netarairdhanaiḥ
|dhanaṃ vinā samāyāti kṣāntiṃ naiva kathañcana

kṣamāvān puruṣaḥ sarvapriyo bhavati dehinām |pūjyate daivatairnityaṃ tasmāt kṣāntiḥ parantapa
!

kṣamāvān puruṣaḥ sarvatra pūjyatekṣamāvān puruṣaḥ sarvaiḥ krodhadoṣairvivarjitaḥ |yaśasā pūjyate
nityamiha loke paratra ca

kṣāntidhanaṃ sarvottamamkṣāntirdhanaṃ dhanaṃ śīlaprajñāvardhanameva ca |dhanānyanyāni śastāni
na hitasya kathañcana

sadbhiḥ kṣamāvāneva pūjyatepūjyate satataṃ sadbhiryaśasā caiva pūjyate |kṣamāvān puruṣaḥ sarvastamāt
kṣāntiparo bhavet

krodhaviṣasya kṣamaiva bheṣajamkṣāntiḥ krodhaviṣasyāsya bheṣajaṃ paramaṃ matam |kṣāntyā'vināśitaḥ
krodho'narthāyopajāyate

jñānaśīlābhibhūtānāṃ bāliśānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |pratīpakārya kurute kṣāntirmārganidarśikā

kṣamāvanta eva loke dhaninaḥsa dharmadhanahīnānāṃ bhramatāṃ gatipañcake |yeṣāṃ kṣāntimayaṃ dravyaṃ
te loke dhaninaḥ smṛtāḥ

tamonicayakāntāre dṛḍhakrodhena dustare |kṣāntyā yathā smṛtāḥ sadbhistaranti khula mānavāḥ

saddharmapāṭhanaṣṭānāṃ deśikā kṣāntiruttamā |apāyabhayabhītānāṃ na bhayaṃ kṣāntirucyate

nṛṇāṃ kṣāntiḥ sukhāvahāsukhāvahā sadā nṛṇāṃ duḥkhasya ca vighātikā |kṣemasamprāpikā nityaṃ viśvāsaguṇakārikā

śubhāsti nāyikā dhanyā hyaśubhebhyo vivarjitā |mokṣasaṃdeśikā puṃsāṃ saṃsārabhayanāśikā

kṣāntiḥ narakāgnivināśikā svargasopānabhūtā casvargasopānabhūtā sā narakāgnivināśikā |trāyate
pretalokātsā tiryagyonau tathaiva ca

kṣāntiḥ sanmārgāmṛtadīpikāsā guṇaudhaiḥ sadā pūrṇā śivā bhavati dehinām |sā praśaste sukhe prāpte
kṣāntiḥ kāryā prayatnataḥ |sarvalokasya māteva sanmārgāmṛtadīpikā

||iti kṣāntivargaścaturviṃśaḥ ||

## 25 vīryavargaḥ

(25) vīryavargaḥdeśakriyāyuktāni kāryāṇi siddhayantideśakālopapannasya kriyātithyocitasya ca
|nyāyenārabhyamāṇasya vīryasya sakalaṃ phalam

nyāyadeśakriyāhīnā adharmeṇa vivarjitāḥ |sīdanti kāryanikarā vīryeṇa parivarjitāḥ

ārabdhavīryā mokṣaṃ prāpnuvantidhyānenārabdhavīryeṇa mokṣaṃ gacchanti paṇḍitāḥ |bhavakṣipta
ivākāro devaloke prayānti ca

yānyārabdhāni kāryāṇi vīryavad balinā nṛṇā |tāni tāni prasiddhāni vipulāni bhavanti ca

ye'rthā lokottare siddhā ye ca lokeṣu sammatāḥ |te vīryeṇa prasādhyante vīryahīnā na jātu vai

mandavīrya cirotsāhaṃ saddharmeṇa vivarjitam |naro viśati lokaṃ (ca) śaśāṅkamiva kalmaṣam

vīryavattā paramāṃ gatiṃ pradadātiāryāṣṭāṅgena mārgeṇa na jñānaparipālitaḥ |vīryavattāmahotsāho
prayāti paramāṃ gatim

bodhiḥ vīryeṇāvāpyatevīryaṇāvāpyate bodhiḥ svavīryeṇa tathā mahī |arhattvaṃ vīryavadbhiśca tasmānnāgnisamā
gatiḥ

uttamasthānaprāptyartha vīryārambhe matiḥ kāryātasmād devān guṇān matvā vīryavān niyatendriyaḥ
|vīryārambhe matiṃ kuryārnnāsti vīryasamarthanam

vīryārthī smṛtimān yaśca naro jñānaparāyaṇaḥ |prayātyanuttamaṃ sthānaṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitam

||iti vīryavargaḥ pañcaviṃśaḥ ||

## 26 dhyānavargaḥ

(26) dhyānavargaḥsvasthaḥ kaḥ?asaṃsaktamaternityaṃ nityaṃ dhyānavihāriṇaḥ |viśuddhamanaso nityamekāgrabhiratasya
ca

yasyaikāgrakaraṃ cittaṃ tasya doṣā na bādhakāḥ |sa doṣabhayanirmuktaḥ svastha ityabhidhīyate

ekāgrābhiratañceto vivekamanudhāvati |sarvatarkavinirmuktaḥ svastha ityabhidhīyate

cittasyaikāgratāṃ varṇayatiyasya cittaṃ dhruvaṃ śāntaṃ nirvāṇābhirataṃ sadā |na tasyendriyajā
doṣā bhavasya śubhahetavaḥ

yacca dhyānakṛtaṃ saukhyaṃ yacca (cittaṃ) samādhijam |cittaṃ tatsarvamekāgramate bhavati dehinaḥ

yatiḥ alaukikaṃ sukhaṃ bhuṅkteekārāmasya yatino yat sukhaṃ jāyate hṛdi |yat saukhyamativijñeyaṃ
na saukhyaṃ laukikaṃ matam

kīdṛśaṃ cittaṃ śāntiṃ samadhigacchati?ekāgrābhirataṃ cittaṃ viśuddhākṛtameva ca |doṣajālavinirmuktaṃ
śāntiṃ samadhigacchati

jñānāmbhasā tṛṣṇāgniṃ hantiekāntamanasā nityaṃ saṃkṣiptendriyapañcakaiḥ |tṛṣṇāgninātivṛddhaṃ
ca hanti jñānāmbhasā budhaḥ

tasya tṛṣṇāvimuktasya viśuddhasya sukhaiṣiṇaḥ |akṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ caiva padaṃ hi sthitamagrataḥ

nirvāṇapuragāmi vartmavitarkakuṭilaṃ ceto yatra yatropapadyate |ekālambanayuktena dhārya tena
samādhinā |tasmādetat paraṃ vartma nirvāṇapuragāmikam

manonigrahaphalametadagraṃ manaḥ kṣutvā hanyādarisamūhakam |mano hīdaṃ vinirgṛhya (sa) vetti
dhyānajairdṛḍhaiḥ

nirupamaṃ dhyānajaṃ sukham(tatra sthitāḥ narāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ śraddhāvanto manīṣiṇaḥ) |prayānti paramaṃ
sthānamaśokaṃ hatakilviṣam

nirviṣaskasya tuṣṭasya nirāgasyāpi dhīmataḥyat sukhaṃ dhyānajaṃ bhāti kutastasyopamā parā

dhyānaiḥ paramaṃ padaṃ prāpyateetatsāraṃ sudhīrāṇāṃ yogināṃ pāragāminām |yadevedaṃ manaḥ śrutvā
prayānti padamacyutam

||iti dhyānavargaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ ||

## 27 prajñāvargaḥ

(27) prajñāvargaḥprajñā māteva hitakāriṇīdharmānusāriṇī prajñā vīryeṇa parivṛṃhitā |samādhibalasaṃyuktā
māteva hitakāriṇī

prajñā gatipañcakāt trāyatesā hi santrāyate sarvān puruṣān gatipañcakāt |na mātā na pitā tatra
gacchantamanugacchati

prajñāśikharamāruhya śīlakandaraśobhanam |bhavadoṣamidaṃ sarva paśyati (jñāna) bhūṣaṇaḥ

samādhinā bhavārṇavaṃ taratiindriyāṇīndriyārthebhyo yadā vindanti tatpadam |tadā samādhinā jñāna
bhavasāgaramuttaret

dāna-śīla-tapo-dhyānai-rjñānamevāgramucyate |apavargād yadā jñānaṃ jñānaśīle sukhāvahe

prajñā aṣṭamo mārgastathāgatenopadiṣṭaḥcakṣuṣāṃ ca parā dṛṣṭā prajñoktā (yā)sunirmalā |mārgāṇāṃ
cāṣṭamo mārgaḥ śivaḥ proktastathāgataiḥ

prajñābalaṃ sarvottamamcaturṇā caiva satyānāmagre dve tu prakīrtite |bālānāṃ ca sadā dṛṣṭaṃ
prajñābalamihottamam

janmapadvatirjñānaśastreṇa chettavyājñānaśastreṇa tikṣṇena latā chedyā durāsadā |hantavyā doṣanivahāśchettavyā
janmapaddhatiḥ

na jñānātparo bandhuḥamṛtānāṃ paraṃ jñānaṃ śreyasāṃ nidhiruttamam |na jñānācca paraṃ bandhurna
jñānāddhanamuttamam

jñānaśīlayutā prajñā sevitavyājñānaśīlayutāvṛddhā vītarāgā gataspṛhāḥ |sevitavyāḥ sadā santastattvamārganidarśakāḥ

kleśādīn prajñāśastreṇa vidārayetprajñāvajreṇa tīkṣṇena mahodayavasena ca |mahāyogarathārūḍhaḥ
kleśādīn pravidārayet

||iti prajñāvargaḥ saptaviṃśaḥ ||

## 28 nirvāṇavargaḥ

(28) nirvāṇavargaḥkleśakṣaya eva nirvāṇamārgaḥkleśakṣayāt paraṃ saukhyaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ
|eṣa nirvāṇago mārgaḥ kathitastattvadarśakaiḥ

tatpadaṃ śāśvataṃ juṣṭaṃ kathayanti tathāgatāḥ |yatra janma na mṛtyurna vidyate duḥkhasambhavaḥ

vibhūtasyāpramattasya śāntasya vanacāriṇaḥ |alolupasya vīrasya nirvāṇasya vibhūtayaḥ

viṣayeṣvapramatto nirvāṇaṃ nāticiraṃ prāpnotimitrāmitraprahīṇasya bhavarāgavivarjinaḥ |viṣayeṣvapramattasya
nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

śubhakarttṛ nirvāṇaṃ prāpnotiśubhakāryeṣu saktasya maitrīkāruṇyabhāvinaḥ |saṃsārabhayabhītasya
nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

kausīdyavirahitaḥ tvaritaṃ nirvāṇaṃ yātikleśakṣayavidhijñasya nairātmyasyāpi tasya ca |kausīdyāccaiva
muktasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

vaśyendriyasya śāntasya nirvāṇaṃ samīpataramcatuḥsatyavidhijñasya tridoṣavadhasevinaḥ |vaśyendriyasya
śāntasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

sukhaduḥkhapāśairmukto muniḥ pāraga ucyatesukhaduḥkhamayaiḥ pāśairyasya ceto na hanyate |sa
doṣabhayanirmuktaḥ pārago munirucyate

śubhānveṣī nirvāṇamadhigacchatipuruṣo'pāyabhīruśca pramādabalavarjakaḥ |śubhakārī śubhānveṣī
nirvāṇamadhigacchati

||iti nirvāṇavargo'ṣṭāviṃśaḥ ||

## 29 mārgavargaḥ

(29) mārgavargaḥāryacatuṣṭayopāsakaḥ pāraṃ gacchatisatyāni catvāri śivāni tāni,subhāvitānyeva
samīkṣya vidvān|sucintako jāti-jarā-bhayebhyaḥ,pramucyaye pāramupaiti śāntaḥ

kāmeṣu saktaḥ bhavabhogabaddhaḥ bhavatiacintako yastu vibhūtabuddhiḥkāmeṣu sakto bhavabhogabaddhaḥ
|sa bandhanaiḥ kāmamayairnibaddho,na mucyate jāti-jarā-bhayebhyaḥ

bhavārṇave sukhadraṣṭā ante narakaṃ yātivicintya yo duḥkhamidaṃ viśālaṃ,na khedamāyāti bhavārṇavebhyaḥ
|sa kāmavāṇairnihato hi mūḍhaḥ,kaṣṭāmavasthāṃ narake'pi yāti

ābhyantaraṃ kṣemasukhaṃ ca hitvā,kiṃ kāmabhogābhiratā hi bālāḥ |naite bijānanti bhayaṃ ca tīvra-mabhyeti
mṛtyurjvalanaprakāśaḥ

tattvamārgapradarśakaiḥ kimuktam?anityaduḥkhaśūnyo'yamātmā kārakavarjitaḥ |saṃsāraḥ kathito
buddhaiḥ tattvamārgapradarśakaiḥ

tena sarvamidaṃ tattvajñānaṃ jñeyaṃ samāsataḥ |jñānajñeyavinirmuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ nopalabhyate

kaḥ tattvavidhijñaḥ?antapāravidhijño yaḥ ṣoḍaśākāratattvavit |ūrdhvagatividhijño hi kṣāntitattvavicakṣaṇaḥ

tattvavideva dharmatāmanuviśatiagralokaikadharmajñaḥ samanantaratattvavit |sa dharmatāmanuviśed
yathā (ca) na vikampate

dvayopāyavinirmukto naṣṭān nāśayate muhuḥ |naṣṭapāpagatirvīraḥ strotāpanno nirucyate

srotāṃsya kuśalā dharmā jīryante pāpagāminaḥ |mokṣāgninā pratapyante srotāpanno bhavatyataḥ

prasrabdhijaṃ mahodarkamuktaṃ saṃsāramokṣakam |tṛṣṇākṣayasukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ satyataḥ sukhakārakam

kaḥ sadaiva sukhī bhavati?nāvabadhnāti yaṃ tṛṣṇā na vitarkairvihasyate |samprāptabhavapārastu
sukhī bhavati sarvadā

ārya mārgacatuṣṭayam anyonyaphalasambhūtamanyonyaphalasambhūta sarvataḥ sampravartate |tadeva
kāraṇaṃ jñeyamāryamārgacatuṣṭayam

āryasatyeṣu viditaḥ puruṣo vidyate dhruvam |viṣayeṣu hi saṃghuṣṭaṃ jagad bhramati cakravat

kaḥ śreṣṭho mārgaḥ?sa mārgo deśakaḥ śreṣṭho yo mārgo bhāṣitaḥ śivaḥ |yena mārgeṇa prācīnā (dhruvaṃ)
yātā manīṣiṇaḥ

triśaraṇagata eva sukhaṃ jīvatisujīvitaṃ bhavet tasya yasya buddhau sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi buddhivinirmuktaṃ
jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet

sujīvitaṃ bhavet tasya yasya dharme sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi dharmavinirmuktaṃ jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet

sujīvitaṃ bhavet tasya yasya saṅghe sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi saṅghavinirmuktaṃ jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet

keṣāṃ sujīvitaṃ jīvanam?sujīvitaṃ bhavet tasya yasya satye sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi satyavinirmuktaṃ
jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet

sujīvitaṃ bhavet tasya yasya mārge sthitaṃ manaḥ |nahi mārgavinirmuktaṃ jīvitaṃ jīvitaṃ bhavet

nirvāṇagamane yasya nityaṃ buddhiravasthitā |sa doṣādeva nirmukto na devaḥ krīḍati svayam

kīdṛśī krīḍā sukhodbhāvikā?yā bhavavyāpinī krīḍā nityamekāgracetasaḥ |sā sukhodbhāvikā krīḍā
na krīḍā rāgakārikā

kena mārgeṇa śivaṃ sthānaṃ milati?sukhādīniha satyāni yathā dāntena vindati |tadā kṣemaṃ śivaṃ
sthānaṃ prāpnoti puruṣottamaḥ

||iti mārgavarga ekonatriṃśaḥ ||

## 3 kāyajugutsāvargaḥ

(3) kāyajugutsāvargaḥkāyasya svarūpanirūpaṇamsatkārairvṛhaṇairmāsairapi śayyāsanādibhiḥ |na
svīkartumayaṃ kāyaḥ kadācit kenacit kṛtaḥ

kāyasya riputvamkṛtaghnasyāvidakṣasya nityaṃ randhraprahāriṇaḥ |kaḥ kāyasya riporartha pāpaṃ
kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ

rogānī tamanarthānāṃ bahūnā bhājanaṃ tathā |aśucyaṅgasya nikaraṃ kāyamityabhidhīyate

avijñeyaṃ maraṇaṃ jīvitañca kṣaṇikamupasthitamavijñeyaṃ maraṇaṃ tattvacintakaiḥ |jīvitaṃ ca
kṣaṇādūrdhvaṃ na gacchati nirūpyate

kāyasya heyatvamāyuṣkarmārthanirmāṇametaṃ kāyaṃ tyajyayam |api viddhastathā śete kāṣṭhaloṣṭhasamo
bhuvi

kṣaṇe kṣaṇe'pi kāyo'yaṃ jīryate na nivartate |tathā yauvanamadairbālā muhayante (mugdhacetasaḥ)

dhanadhānyamadairmattāḥ kurvantyahitamātmanaḥ |tannāśamupayātyevaṃ sa ca pāpena dahayate

adhārmikanindāna manuṣyā manuṣyāste yebhyo dharmo na rocate |na mārge ca sthitāstattve nirvāṇapuragāmike

manuṣyayoneḥ bhavasāgarataraṇopāyaḥkathaṃ prāpya hi mānuṣyaṃ śreyasāmālayaṃ mahat |na jñānaplavamārūhaya
taranti bhavasāgarāt

jīvanasya kṣaṇikatāvidyudālātasadṛśaṃ gandharvanagaropamam |sadā tad yāti rabhasaṃ jīvitaṃ sarvadehinām

śarīre na madaṃ kuryāt kṣaṇike bhaṅgure sadā |itvare capale'sāre jarāmaraṇabhīruke

dhyāyinānnagataṃ hyetat śokānāmālayo mahān |śubhāśubhānāṃ kṣetraṃ ca śarīramavadhīyate

saphalajīvanarahasyamjñānaśīladayādānairyasya gātraṃ vibhūṣitam |tasya sattvaikasārasya śarīraṃ
saphalaṃ matam

dhātujñānāt mokṣaḥdhātūnāṃ mārakāt sarvamidaṃ muktaṃ kalevaram |śarīradhātu vicayāt sākṣād bhavati
netarāt

śarīradhātuṃ yo muktvā dhātuṣvanyeṣu rajyate |sa dhātukatvaśikṣāto duḥkhenaiva pramucyate

hiraṇyadhāturna tathā duḥkhaśāntyai hi vartate |śarīradhātutattvajño yathā duḥkhāt pramucyate

duḥkhād duḥkhodayastena duḥkhī na parirakṣyate |pravīṇo rājacaurādibhayaiḥ sarvairūpadrutam

tasmādanarthakaṃ nityaṃ dūratastaṃ vivarjayet |varjanāt sukhito duṣṭasaṅgrahād duḥkhitaḥ pumān

śarīradhātutattvajño dhātulakṣaṇatattvavit |dhyānādhyayanasaṃsakto dahati kleśaparvatān

tasmāccharīrajān dhātūn paṇḍitaḥ pratyavekṣate |teṣāṃ svalakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā mokṣo bhavati dehinām

||iti kāyajugupsāvargastṛtīyaḥ ||

## 30 bhikṣuvargaḥ

(30) bhikṣuvargaḥādarśo bhikṣuḥ lokamārgadarśakaḥyo hinasti na bhūtāni mitravettā sadā'kṣayaḥ
|pitṛvat sarvabhūtāni lokastamanupaśyati

adattādānavirato nityaṃ jñānī jitendriyaḥ |praśāntadehakarmā (ca) tīrṇasambhavasaṅkramaḥ

nāpyālekhyagatā nāpi cakṣuṣā sā nirīkṣyate |hatakāmo dṛṣṭasatyo muktastādṛśa ucyate

samaloṣṭāśmakanakaḥ vītaśokaḥ samāhitaḥ |na kleśoragasampṛktaḥ sa saukhyaṃ dhruvamāpnuyāt

kaḥ bhikṣurvidyate?arthānarthasamo yasya lābhālābhau tathaiva ca |sukhaduḥkhasamāyuktaḥ bhikṣuḥ
sa khalu kathyate

mitrāmitraprahīṇo yaḥ samacetā jitendriyaḥ |vibheti yo na viṣayaiḥ vijñeyastādṛśo yatiḥ

viṣayadveṣī nirvāṇamadhigacchativiṣamatvāddhi viṣayān dveṣṭi dhīro gatavyathaḥ |na tasya dūre
nirvāṇaṃ samyaksambuddhadeśitam

udaya-vyayatattvajñaḥ samyagdṛṣṭiralolupaḥ |himavāniva niṣkramya saṃsārānmuktahetukaḥ

tṛṇacandanatulyo hi samatṛṣṇāmbarāśinaḥ |sa kauśeyasaṅghaṭitatṛṣṇayā naiva bādhate

lābhasatkārasantuṣṭaḥ santuṣṭastṛṇasaṃstaraiḥ |vahnivallābhasatkāraṃ yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati

buddhadeśitāḥ bhikṣudharmāḥvāhyate yo na viṣayaistṛṇanadyā na vāhayate |svakarmaphalatattvajñaḥ
sa bhikṣurbuddhadeśitaḥ

nātītaṃ śocate yo hi buddhayā (caiva) gataspṛhaḥ |pratyutpannakriyāyogī na buddhistasya lipyate

nirvāṇe (ca) matiryasya dharme nityaṃ sthitā bhavet |na vartate sa saṃsāre śukladharmasamāvṛtaḥ

nāvilaṃ kriyate yasya cittaṃ vidyāgnikalpayā |dāruvadviṣayā yasya tasya duḥkhaṃ na vidyate

indriyāṇi vaśe yasya cendriyeṣu vaśānugaḥ |hriyate yaḥ pumarthaino nikaṣastādṛśo muniḥ

sādhuvaddhimano yasya kṣamāvān priyadarśanaḥ |prahlādayati cetāṃsi sa nṛṇāṃ śaśivanmuniḥ

aruṇābhirato yastu harmyāgreṣu na rajyati |santuṣṭaḥ pāṃśukūlena bhikṣurbhikṣārato bhavet

śānto dāntaḥ sudhīrarthāt tattvavit sukhaduḥkhayoḥ |sa yātyuttamamadhvānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati

ṛjumatpātakānyasya nityaṃ dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ |prākṛtaiśca malai(rhīnaḥ) sa yogī satyavartmani

sarvendriyavighātī yaḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |śānto dāntendriyaḥ svastho bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

ṣaḍindriyarathārūḍho rāgaśatrunivārakaḥ |prajñādhīraḥ kriyāvān yaḥ sa śāntipadamaśnute

araṇyavāsī santuṣṭo bhūmivāsī samāhitaḥ |dhunāti pāpako dharmaścāyurmeghānivāmbaraḥ

śubhaṃ vā dehakarmāntaḥ śubhacaryāsu saṃrataḥ |tattvadṛṣṭiḥ kriyādakṣo nāśayanmārasādhanam

dayālurbhikṣurnirvāṇamārge sthito bhavatirāgātyaye na bādheta śubhacittaṃ gatālayam |maitryā
kāruṇyabahulo bhikṣurnairyyāṇike sthitaḥ

yasya rūpādayo neṣṭā viṣayā bandhahetave |sa yāti paramāṃ śāntiṃ yatra gatvā na śocyate

hetupratyayatattvajñaḥ sūkṣmārthe kṛtaniścayaḥ |mokṣasrotasyabhiratastṛṣṇayā naiva rajyate

yo nādatte'śubhaṃ karma śubhakarmarataḥ sadā |candrāṃśunirmalagatiryogī bhavati tādṛśaḥ

pradahan pāpakān dharmān śuṣkendhanamivānalaḥ |vibhrājate tribhuvane muktapāyo gatavyathaḥ

mokṣe'sti yasya tu mano na saṃsāre kathañcana |nāsau badhnāti saṃsāre muktaḥ pakṣī yathāmbare

vedanodayatattvajño vedanāphalaniścayaḥ |sa mukta iti vijñeyastattvavid ṛtavāṃśca saḥ

tathāpyete sukhaduḥkhe mṛṣṭāmṛṣṭairna lipyate |dīptaṃ paśyati saṃsāraṃ yaḥ sa yogī satāṃ mataḥ

bhikṣurbhavati kīdṛśaḥ?athāmūḍhamatirnityaṃ nityaṃ dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |bhikṣuvṛttāvabhirato bhikṣurbhavati
tādṛśaḥ

na tṛptirdarśanārāmaiḥ sādhūnāṃ darśane ratiḥ |niṣkrāntagṛhakalmāṣo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

na nṛtyagītasandarśī (satyaṃ) ca punarīkṣate |saṃrakṣito śmaśāneṣu bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

ekāhaṃ paramaṃ piṇḍamādatte'nyatra kāṅkṣati |tribhāgakukṣisantuṣṭo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

vastrottamavivarjī yaḥ pāṃsukūleṣu rajyate |muktāhāravihāro yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

karmāṇyārabhate yo na nirāśaḥ sa ca karmasu |niruddhako noparato bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

kāyakoṭivinirmukto mohadhvāntavivarjitaḥ |aliptaḥ pāpakairdharmaibhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

sarvāśayajanānītaḥ sarvāśayavivarjitaḥ |sarvāśayavinirmukto bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

āryāṣṭāṅgena mārgeṇa nirvāṇapurataḥ sthitaḥ |sarvārthadharmatā hyeṣā bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

śāntendriyo dṛḍhamatiḥ kāmapākavivarjitaḥ |ekāgrasaṃsthitamanā bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

bhūmisaṅkramaṇajño yo bhūmitattvanidarśakaḥ |bhūmeḥ parāparajño yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

sambhavāsambhavān dharmān hetupratyayasambhavān |jānīte vidhivat sarvān bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

brahmacārī ṛtujñānī styānamiddhavivarjitaḥ |kalpodagro'vanau dakṣo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

śamasthovipaśyanāśca caturdhyānarataśca yaḥ |ālaye muditārāmo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

pakṣiṇo gaganasthasya chāyevānugataḥ sadā |saddharmasyānujīvī sa bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

kleśopakleśabadhakaḥ samadarśī śubhānvitaḥ |anāpānavidhijño yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

anukramavidhijño yo yogavit tattvadarśakaḥ |pārāpāravidhijño yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

yo na hṛṣyati harṣeṣu bhayeṣu na bibheti ca |mukto harṣabhayodvegairbhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

janmamaraṇatattvajñaḥ surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ |parāvarajñaḥ sattvānāṃ bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

saṅghāṭimātrasaṃhṛṣṭaḥ sañcayeṣu na rajyate |alpeccho brahmacārī yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

ekāśī vṛkṣamūle yaḥ sadā dhyānaṃ samīhate |lābhasatkāravirato bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

upekṣākaruṇārāgo mokṣadoṣavivarjitaḥ |nirdagdhadoṣasarvasvo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

mandavīryakusīdānāṃ bhikṣūṇāṃ darśanāya ca |nānyayogābhirakto yo bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

kausīdyābhirato bhikṣuḥ nahi kalyāṇamarhatina śayyāsanasambhogī bhikṣurbuddhena bhāṣitaḥ |kausīdyabhirato
yastu nāsau kalyāṇamarhati

kleśānāṃ mūlapākaṃ hi kausīdyaṃ yasya vidyate |tasya duḥkhaṃ mahāghoraṃ saṃsāre sampravartate

kausīdyameva yasyāsti tasya dharmo na vidyate |kevalaṃ vastramātreṇa 'bhikṣuḥ' sa iti kathyate

bhikṣurbhavati na tādṛśaḥnādhyāpane ratiryasya na dhyānenāśu rakṣati |kevalaṃ vastumātreṇa bhikṣurbhavati
tādṛśaḥ

vihārābhirato yastu na rato dharmagocare |strī-madyalolupamatibhikṣurasti na tādṛśaḥ

(bhavenmatiryasya nityaṃ vividhe) pāpakarmaṇi |sa bhikṣurdeśito buddhaiḥ na bhoktā svakagocare

varamāśīviṣaviṣaṃ kathitaṃ tāmrameva ca |bhuktasyātyantaduḥśīlairadhikaṃ pāpabhojanam

yo hi nārhati piṇḍāya nāsau piṇḍāya kalpate |yasya piṇḍikṛtāḥ kleśāḥ sarpā iva vileśayāḥ

sa bhikṣuḥ piṇḍabhojī syānna strīdarśanatatparaḥ |bandhakaṃ yadi cātmānaṃ kṛtvā paraśubhakṣatim

bhikṣurdurguṇānāṃ svarūpamkathaṃ sa bhikṣurvijñeyaḥ saṅgharatnapradūṣakaḥ |yasyeṣṭā lābhasatkārā
viṣayā yasya sammatāḥ

nāridarśanasākāṅkṣī na bhikṣurna gṛhīva saḥ |rājaseviṣu sṛṣṭāśo madyapaḥ krodhanastathā

sadā bhikṣurbañcayate dāyakānnanu cetasā |upāyanānyupādāya rājadvārāśritā hi ye |saṃrabdhā gṛhibhiḥ
sārdha yathā nāgā vanāśritāḥ

tasmāt tāneva puṣṇanti vāterṣyāste samāgatāḥ |putradārān parityajya ye śāntā ratnamāśritāḥ

bhikṣorguṇānāṃ māhātmyamprahāya doṣān yo bhikṣurasti darśanatattvavit |rūpādiskandhatattvajño
mokṣāya yatate sadā

dharmāvabodhābhirato dhyānārāmavihāravāntattvalakṣaṇasambodhāt prāpnuyāt padamavyayam

maitryārāmo hi satatamudyukto dharmagocare |tattvalakṣaṇatattvajño bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

yoniśastu matiryasya kāmakrodhairna hanyate |sa bhikṣuriti vijñeyo viparītastato'nyathā

sarvabhūtadṛḍhaḥ śāntaḥ sarvasaṅgativarjitaḥ |sarvabandhananirmukto bhikṣurbhavati tattvavit

karmaṇi yasya vijñānaviṣayairyo na hanyate |nirmalaḥ syāt kanakavat santuṣṭo bhikṣurucyate

priyāpriye mano yasya na lepamanugacchati |saṅkalpānāṃ vidhijño yaḥ sarvapāpavivarjitaḥ

anyasaṃduṣṭacarito dharmaśīlo jitendriyaḥ |ahīnasatvo matimān bhikṣurbhavati tādṛśaḥ

śāstre śāstrārthavijñāne matiryasya sadā ratā |na pānabhojanarataḥ sa bhikṣuḥ śāntamānasaḥ

bhikṣoḥ svarūpanirūpaṇamvanāraṇyavihāreṣu śmaśāne tṛṇasaṃstare |ramate yasya tu mano bhikṣurbhavati
tādṛśaḥ

doṣāṇāṃ karmatattvajñaḥ phalavit pariśeṣataḥ |hetupratyayatattvajño bhikṣuḥ syād vītakalmaṣaḥ

(hata) kilviṣakāntāro hatadoṣo jitendriyaḥ |punarbhavavidhijño yo bhikṣuḥ śāntamanāḥ (smṛta)

notkarṣo hṛṣṭahṛdaye nindayā naiva rūṣyati |samudratulyagāmbhīryo yogavān bhikṣurucyate

āveṇiko dṛḍhamatiḥ sūkṣmavādī na lolupaḥ |kāmavādī samo dakṣaḥ sa bhikṣuḥ śānta ucyate

kāmadhātūpagān hetūn rūpadhātau tathaiva ca |āruṣyeṣu ca tattvajñaḥ śāstrā bhikṣuḥ sa ucyate

na laukikakathāsaktaḥ śatrudoṣabadhe sadā |viṣavad yasya viṣayāḥ sa bhikṣurdeśito budhaiḥ ||83|śuddhā
yasya (hi)kāmeṣu matirbhavati nityaśaḥ |sa nirmuktamatirbhikṣurmuktaḥ saṃsārabandhanāt

dhyānādhyayanakarmaṇyaḥ kausīdyaṃ yasya dūrataḥ |hitakārī ca sattvānām āraṇyo bhikṣurucyate

praśnottaramatiryasya pratibhāvan jitendriyaḥ |sa dharmaḥ kathito jñeyo viparītastṛṇaiḥ samaḥ

buddhaśāsane kīdṛg bhikṣuḥ śastaḥ?kāyamānasabhīryasya sarvadā naiva khidyate |sarvakṛtyakaro
jñeyo yaḥ saṅghāya ca tatparaḥ

na parārtha na lobhārtha yaśo'rtha kurute na tu |saṅghakārye matiryasya sa muktaḥ sarvabandhanaiḥ

na svargārtha matiryasya lābhārtha yaśase na vā |nirvāṇārtha kriyā sarvā sa bhikṣuḥ srota ucyate

pāpebhyo nityavirataḥ satkṛtyeṣu rataḥ sadā |na pāpamitrasaṃsargī bhikṣuḥ syād buddhaśāsane

maitryā bhāvitacittasya dakṣasya ṛjucetasaḥ |śikṣāpadeṣu raktasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

jarāmaraṇaśīlasya saṃsāravimukhasya ca |dhyāne'pi na pramattasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

anityatāvidhijñasya śūnyatāvatkriyāvataḥ |dhyānotkarṣavidhijñasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ

dhīro'yamagracoro'yaṃ yo'yaṃ bhikṣurasaṃvṛtaḥ |antaḥpurīvarasrāvī bahiścīvarasaṃvṛtaḥ

dharmavinayād rikto bhikṣurduḥkhabhāgī bhavedevayathā yatnamayo rāśiḥ sarvo'sāraśca durbalaḥ
|evaṃ sañcarati rikto vitatho bhikṣuvādikaḥ

sa nārakeyo duḥśīlaḥ saṅgharatnabahiṣkṛtaḥ |kāyasya bhedānnarakaṃ nīyate cittavañcitaḥ

vañcito dharmavinayād yāti tat svena karmaṇā |malinastamasā baddho duḥkhabhāgī bhaviṣyati

aprāvṛtaḥ śubhadhaman nagnaḥ sādhujugupsitaḥ |nayate narakaṃ ghoraṃ yathā dharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ

aśobhanasya nicayo duḥkhadvāramanāvṛtam |saṃsārabandhanaṃ tīvraṃ dauḥśīlyamiti kathyate

asaṃvareṇa yo dagdhaḥ sa dagdho vahninā bhṛśam |tasya saṃvarakṣīṇasya vinipāto dhruvaṃ sthitaḥ

kukartṛbhikṣurapi narakaṃ yātimanasā saṃvarasthena svācāraiḥ saṃvarāyate |samūḍhacaryāmāruhya
narakāyopakalpate

aśubhaṃ vardhate tasya divārātrau ca sarvataḥ |yasya śīlamayaṃ ratnaṃ dauḥśīlyena nivāritam

dharmaśūnyasya riktasya tamasā saṃvṛtasya ca |vidyate'saṃvarastasya yo na śaucāya kalpate

asaṃvaramayaḥ pāśo malinaḥ sādhuvarjitaḥ |ākarṣati sa duḥśīlān pāpiṣṭhān śīlavarjitān

asaṃvaraiśca dauḥśīlyaiḥ pāpaiśca saha saṅgatiḥ |dūtakā narakasyaite kāmānāmapi sevakāḥ

asaṃvṛtaprasūtasya capalasya viśeṣataḥ |pāpakarmābhiyuktasya narakaṃ nātidūrataḥ

kimete nāvagacchanti karmaṇāṃ sadṛśaṃ phalam |akṣipātāya mūḍhāya durmatau (ye) vimohitāḥ

ahanyahani vardhante pāpanadyo durāsadāḥ |duḥkhormimālāścapalāḥ pāpiṣṭhajanahāriṇaḥ

na teṣāṃ sukaraṃ janma na teṣāṃ sukaraṃ manaḥ |aśīlāḥ puruṣā ye vā śukladharmavivarjitāḥ

dharmo'tyuccaḥ śubho mārgaḥatyuccaśca śubho mārgaḥ sa 'dharma' iti kathyate |taṃ prāpyamanujaḥ
śīghraṃ prayāti padamacyutam

tato'pavādāḥ sādhyante śaktimantaḥ sukhāstu ye |saṃvarasya sadā dāsāsteṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na vidyate
|dauḥśīlyaparamo hyeṣa malinīkurute nṛṇām

ye śaikṣyapadavibhraṣṭā bhāgino narakasya te |evaṃ jñātvā naraḥ sarva saṃvaraṃ pratipadyate

śubhadharmī bhikṣurnirvāṇaṃ nāticiraṃ prāpnotibhavārṇasya sarvasya setubhūto hi saṃvaraḥ |śuddhājīvaviśuddhasya
śāntavaktrasya karmaṇaḥ

dhyāyino vipramuktasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ |dhūrdharasyāpramattasya śmaśānavanasevinaḥ

śāyino bhūtale nityaṃ nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ |pāṃśuśayyāvalambāṃsapātamekaṃ sajarjaram

santoṣaḥ phalamūlaiśca sa sukhī buddhasambhavaḥ |vipramuktasya kāmebhyaḥ santoṣo hītarasya ca

savimuktakacittasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ |kuhakāmalamuktasya rajo vā tasya tāyinaḥ

ākāśasamacittasya nirvāṇaṃ nātidūrataḥ |bahubaddhapadairyuktā vijñeyā (bhava)cārikā

nāśikā brahmacaryasya nirvāṇagatiduḥkhikā |sevyate yā janairnityaṃ prākṛtaiḥ śīlavarjitaiḥ

ajastraṃ parivarjyā sā dhyāyibhistattvadarśibhiḥ |daurbalyamūlamekā sā manaskārapraṇāśikā

nāśinī brahmacaryasya narakasya pradarśikā |bhraṃśikā svargamārgasya duḥkhasāgaraśoṣikā

dūtikā pretalokasya tiryagyoninipātikā |nāmnā saṅgaṇikā sevā saṃsāre bandhamātṛkā

dhyānādhyayanaśaktaiva varjyā nityaṃ hi bhikṣubhiḥ |dhyānādhyayananirmukto nimittābhirataḥ sadā

paradharmo bhayāvahaḥvivarjitaḥ śubhairdharmairapāpagamanāya saḥ |svadharma yaḥ parityajya paradharmeṣu
rajyate

dharmadvayaparibhraṣṭo vinipātāya kalpate |svagṛhaṃ yaḥ parityajya paraveśyāni tiṣṭhati

sadā'yaṃ lāghavaṃ yāti nidhanaṃ cāśu gacchati |tathā yo vimatirbhūto vidvanmānī janecchayā

svadharmaviratiṃ kṛtvā paradharmeṣu vartate |adharme cāśayastasya paraliṅgopajīvinaḥ |

tṛṇavidyābhilipto'yaṃ pretaḥ pāpeṣu pacyate |yaśo'ntaṃ padamāsthāya pāpakarmaṇi vartate

nāsau bhikṣurihocyateśaśvat sa patito dṛṣṭaḥ śāsanāntāt pravartate |nispṛhaḥ kāmacaryāsu nirāmodaḥ
pravarjitaḥ ||ārabdhavīryaḥ santuṣṭo dhyāyī bhikṣurihocyate

na ca kāmeṣu saṃsakto nityāhāravihāravān |kāṣāyasaṃvṛtaḥ kṣauro nāsau bhikṣurihocyate

nimittabodhako ( yastu) nakṣatragaticintakaḥ |rājasevāpramattaśca na sa bhikṣurihocyate

vaidyakarmāṇi kurvaśca śruti saṅgrathanaṃ tathā |saṅkīrṇā dinacaryā ca kurvan bhikṣuḥ praṇaśyati

dhyānādhyayanavidveṣī rataḥ saṅgaṇikāsu ca |lobhasatkāralābhaṃ ca kurvan bhikṣuḥ praṇaśyati

suvarṇadhātusaṃsakto bahumitraratiśca yaḥ |anyalābhābhilāṣito bhikṣuḥ patati śāsanāt

tapasaḥ saṅganirmukto na pāpagaṇasevakaḥ |saktūdakena santuṣṭaḥ sa bhikṣurniṣṭhuraḥ smṛtaḥ

kaḥ śuddho bhikṣuḥ?āgatān viṣayān sarvān tyajati jvalanopamān |viśuddhadoṣo maṇivacchuddho bhikṣurihocyate

antarbahirviśuddhātmā jñānādibhiralaṅkṛtaḥ |śraddhayā śīlavastreṇa kriyāvān bhikṣurucyate

lobhadharmavyatīto yaḥ sthito merurivācalaḥ |sarvalokapriyaḥ śāntaḥ pārago bhikṣurucyate

trirātrivāsī kutrāpi kuśāsanavidhārakaḥ |girigahvarasevī ca vimukto bhikṣurucyate

pāpabhīrurasaṃsparśī saṃvṛtaḥ ca susaṃvṛtaḥ |jñānasevī sthiraḥ śānta ekākī bhikṣurucyate

acalaḥ priyavādī ca pāpamitravivarjitaḥ |aśaktaḥ sarvakṛtyeṣu mukto bhikṣurihocyate

rājasevā kukaṣāyoktisevārājasevā vigarhyāsti bhikṣoścāraṇyavāsinaḥ |kukaṣāyoktisevā'sau mṛtyutaskarajīvikā

na hi rājasevako bhikṣuryaḥ sevyo devatairapi |na hiṃsāsavasaṃsṛṣṭo mahate'śucisevakaḥ

bhikṣoḥ rājasevā na śobhatenirmalasya nirāmasya nispṛhasya ca dehinaḥ |saṃsārabhayabhītasya
rājasevā na śobhate

vanāraṇyaśmaśāneṣu palvalo giribhūmiṣu |prāntabhūmiṣu grāmasya sthitaḥ bhikṣuḥ praśobhate

vanāraṇyaśmaśāneṣu bhikṣurna rājasevayā |dhyānādhyayananirmuktaḥ kavalāhārabhojitā |na bhikṣuriti
vijñeyaḥ piśācasamamānasaḥ

dhyānāddhi vimalaṃ saukhyaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ |na tatsukhātsukhaṃ cānyadasti loke kathañcana

taduttamadhyānasukhaṃ muktvā yaḥ puruṣādhamaḥ |raseṣu ramate bālastena mūḍho vihanyate

viṣayairbhrāmitasyāsya nityaṃ tadgatacetasaḥ |vardhante'kuśalā dharmāḥ paralokāpakarṣakāḥ

ātmajño bhikṣurnirvāṇamadhigacchatiātmano yānahīnaśca gurupṛcchanakastathā |bhikṣurudyuktavīryaśca
nirvāṇamadhigacchati

śrutaṃ yāvad bhavatyeva tāvadeva prabhāṣate |ātmajño mānahīnaśca bhikṣurbhavati tattvavid

mānāpamānahīno yo mārgāmārgavicakṣaṇaḥ |svaparārthavidhijño yaḥ sa tuṣṭo bhikṣurucyate

māninaḥ kutaḥ śāntiḥ?māninaḥ krūramanasaścapalasyālpamedhasaḥ |lābhasatkārayātasya kutaḥ śāntirbhaviṣyati?

prasannācārayuktasya jñānagocarasevinaḥ |saṃsāradoṣabhītasya pravrajyā saphalā matā

svabhāvaparabhāveṣu yasya buddhirnamuñcati |na karmaṇyavipāke ca mārgāmārge tathaiva ca

nivāsopahato bhikṣuḥ sukhaṃ na vindatisadācāraviyuktasya sukhaduḥkhābhayasya ca |nivāsopahato
bhikṣurbālavad dṛśyate paraiḥ

tṛṇavallaghutāṃ yāti svārthācca parihīyate |nivāsopahato bhikṣuḥ parihīṇavane sthitaḥ

dhyānādhyayanakṛtyeṣu mano naiva pravartate |nivāsopahato bhikṣurjanasañcayatatparaḥ

sañcayavyagramanasā jīvitaṃ parihīyate |kṣiṇoti retasaṃ svasya jīvitaṃ naiva gacchati

kaḥ durgatiṃ yāti ?na ca vindati yat kṛtvā sukhamanyatra bhujyate |nivāsopahato bhikṣurjanasañcayatatparaḥ
|pāpāni yāti nityaṃ sa tena gacchati durgatim

śrāmaṇyadharmasya mahattvamanabhipretamanaso nirāśasya ca dehinaḥ |sarvasaṅgavimuktasya śrāmaṇyaṃ
saphalaṃ matam

girigahvaravṛkṣeṣu nityaṃ dhyānavihāriṇaḥ |prasīdati śubhaṃ jñānaṃ dauḥśīlyaparivarjitam

keṣāṃ saphalaṃ jīvanam?sarvasaṅgavinirmukto viṣayairna ca vañcitaḥ |(sa) bhikṣu niṣphalo jñeyaḥ
śuṣkendhanamivānalaḥ

nirvāṇābhiratasya bhikṣoḥ praśaṃsānirvāṇābhirato yo hi bhītasya vibhavārṇavāt |bhikṣurbhavati
śuddhātmā na nivāsena karhicit

tṛṣṇā eva anarthakarīlobhamoheṣu ye śaktāste śaktā tṛṣṇayā sadā |tṛṣṇābandhanabaddhānāṃ nāyaṃ
loko na cāparaḥ

dharmajño durgati na labhateasaṃśaktā matiryasya mithyākarmasu sarvadā |apakṣapātī dharmajño
na sa gacchati durgatim

kaḥ munirucyate?doṣapaṅkemano yasya na limpati kathañcana |ekārāmavihārīyo nirāśo munirucyate

nirmukto vimalācāro nivṛttamalakalmaṣaḥ |mukto yo viṣayaiḥ sarvairāraṇyo munirucyate

lokadharmairna nirvedaṃ samāyāti kathañcana |sukhaduḥkhasamaprajño nirmalo munirucyate

santoṣaḥ paramo(dharmo) nityaṃ kāmavivarjitaḥ |nirāmayaḥ kṛcchrajīvī śucirmunirihocyate

nayenna tena saṃśleṣaṃ yatra yatrānugacchati |ekacārī dṛḍhamatiḥ kriyāvān munirucyate

śubhāśubhānāṃ sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalatattvavit |śubhāśubhaparityāgī loke'sau munirucyate

udyukto doṣanāśāya nityakāmagatiḥ smṛtaḥ |udayavyayatattvajño buddhimān munirucyate

deśakālavidāṃ śreṣṭho'dvayavādī jitendriyaḥ |saṃsārabhayabhīto'yaṃ praśānto munirucyate

kaḥ bhikṣuḥ nirvāṇamadhigacchati?ekārāmagato bhikṣuḥ saṃkṣiptendriyapañcakaḥ |dehalakṣaṇatattvajño
nirvāṇamadhigacchati

vīryavān ( satyavāk) bhikṣurnityaṃ doṣavivarjitaḥ |udyānamiva krīḍāyā nirvāṇamadhigacchati

kalyāṇadharmī bhavadagdhe kleśe vayaṃ dagdhā vanaṃ dagdhaṃ yathāgninā |kalyāṇadharme saṃraktā
na raktā kāmabhojane

māyayā janāḥ vañcakāḥ bhavantinityaṃ prāptyutsukā ye (hi) nityaṃ svajanasaṃratāḥ |māyayā vañcakā
(ye tu ) mūḍhāste dharmavartmani

śubhakarmaṇi manaḥ kāryaḥramaṇīyānyaraṇyāni tatraiva ramate manaḥ |ramante vītarāgāste na tu
kāmagaveṣiṇaḥ

sa kathābhirato yastu rato viṣayatṛṣṇayoḥ |na yāsyati puraṃ śāntaṃ yo ca mṛtyuṃ na vindati

advayavādī bhavayo'tyantaśāntamanasā nityaṃ dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ |ādimadhyāntakalyāṇo nityamadvayagocaraḥ

||iti bhikṣuvargastriṃśaḥ ||karuṇā-dāna-śīlāni-kṣāntirvīryamathāpi ca |dhyānaṃ prajñā'tha nirvāṇo
mano bhikṣuśca te daśa ||||iti tṛtīyam udānam ||

## 31 puṇyavargaḥ

atha caturtham udānam(puṇya-deva-sukhairmitra-rāja-stutibhiranvitāḥ |saddharmasmṛtivaipulyai
gṛhīto'yaṃ samuccayaḥ ||)(31) puṇyavargaḥpuṇyapraśaṃsāramaṇīyāni puṇyāni phalaṃ teṣāṃ paraṃ
śubham |tasmāt kuruta puṇyāni nāsti puṇyasamaṃ dhanam

puṇyaṃ nidhānamakṣayyaṃ puṇyaṃ ratnamanuttamam |pradīpasadṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ mātṛvat pitṛvat sadā

puṇyaṃ kṛtvā gatā devāḥ puṇyaṃ nayati sadgatim |puṇyaṃ kṛtvā narā loke modante tridive hi (te)

puṇyaṃ paraṃ sukhampuṇyādhikā hi puruṣā bhavanti sukhinaḥ sadā |tasmāt kuruta puṇyāni nāsti
puṇyasamaṃ sukham

puṇyaṃ kṛtvā gatā devāḥ puṇyapriyadhanasya ca |hetubhūtaṃ sadā dṛṣṭaṃ tasmāt puṇyaṃ paraṃ sukham

puṇyādṛte sukhamasaṃbhavampuṇyaṃ nityottamaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ chāyāvadanugāmikam |tasmāt sukhaṃ paraṃ
puṇyaṃ nāsti puṇyādṛte sukham

puṇyāpuṇyaphalayorantarampuṇyottīrṇāḥ punardevā patanti sukṛtānugāḥ |puṇyāpuṇyaphalo lokastasmāt
puṇyaṃ samācaret

apuṇyanindāye puṇyahīnā durdāntā nityaṃ kugatigāminaḥ |kutasteṣāṃ sukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sikatāsu yathā
ghṛtam

vittena vañcitā mūḍhāḥ puṇyena parivañcitāḥ |na teṣāṃ vidyate śarma duḥkhaṃ teṣāmanuttaram

puṇyavaśād devalokaṃ gacchatimānuṣyaṃ sukṛtaṃ hyetat kṛte bhavati dehinaḥ |tena karmavipākena
svargalokeṣu jāyate

priyo bhavati yasyātmā yasya saukhye sthitā matiḥ |sa karotu mahatpuṇyaṃ devalokopapattaye

dharmacārī puruṣa eva sukhamavāpnutedharmacārī hi puruṣaḥ sukhāt sukhamavāpnute |nirmalaśca
parāṃ śāntiṃ kṣipramevādhigacchati |tasmāt kuruta puṇyāni (yannityaṃ) sāṃpracāyikam

puṇyakarttṛ avyayaṃ sukhamaśnutepuṇyakārī sadā dānto padaṃ gacchati cāvyayam |ramaṇīyāni puṇyāni
karaṇīyānyanekaśaḥ

puṇyasya vaicitryaṃ dharmasya upādeyatā cavicitraṃ hi kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ vicitraṃ paripacyate |dharmādharmapradhānasya
jīvalokasya sarvataḥ |śamatrāṇo yathā dharmastasmād dharmarato bhavet

adharmī duḥkhaṃ prāpnotiyo (hi) dharma parityajya ramate kukṛte naraḥ |tasya duṣkṛtadagdhasya
duḥkhaṃ bhavati nityaśaḥ

dharme eva manaḥ kāryamyāvannābhyeti maraṇaṃ yāvat sakalacintanam |tāvad dharme manaḥ kāryamupaśāntirbhaviṣyati

paropakartṛ nirvāṇapuraṃ yātiyo hi deśayate dharma pareṣāṃ hitakāṅkṣayā |sa mātā sa pitā caiva
nirvāṇapuradeśakaḥ

śāstuḥ subhāṣitamamūlyamśubhādhikaparaścaikaḥ yo deśayati deśikaḥ |sa gatyantaramārgajño nātho
bhavati dehinām |na mūlyaṃ vidyate śāstuḥ subhāṣitapadasya vai

sādhāraṇadravyād dharmadravyasya vailakṣaṇyamna padaṃ labhate śāntaṃ yad dhanairupalabhyate
|dravyaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ na dharmo buddhibandhanam

dharmadravyamakṣuṇṇamastidravyaṃ vinaśyati nṛṇāṃ dharmadravyaṃ na jātu vai |ābhyantarasahastrāṇi
dharma eko'nugacchati

na dhanaṃ padamapyekaṃ gacchantamanugacchati |hīyate draviṇaṃ teṣāṃ rājacaurokāgnibhiḥ |dharmavittaṃ
na tacchakyamapahartu kathañcana

ato dharmaparo bhavetacireṇāpi kālena bhuktvā saukhyamanekaśaḥ |bhavatyavaśyaṃ patanaṃ tasmād
dharmaparo bhavet

dharma ekaḥ paraṃ trāṇaṃ dharma ekaḥ parā gatiḥ |dharmeṇa pūrvavartyeṣa maraṇaṃ cāpyadharmataḥ

dharmacāriṇaḥ praśaṃsāvaraṃ dharmo dharmacārī dharmameva niṣevate |sa sukhāt sukhamāpnoti na
duḥkhamanupaśyati

adharmacāriṇo nindāadharmacārī puruṣo yadā'dharma niṣevate |sa tadā duḥkhamāpnoti narakeṣu punaḥ
punaḥ

nirvāṇamahattvamratnatrayaprasādasya bhāvitasyāpyanekaśaḥ |phalaṃ bhavati nirvāṇaṃ pūrvasvargopajīvinaḥ

ātmanaiva puṇyamācaraṇīyamātmanā kriyate puṇyamātmanā pratipadyate |sukhaṃ vā yadi vā duḥkhamātmanaivopabhujyate

śīlavataḥ puṇyaprabhāvo vipulaḥnadīstrota ivājastraṃ puruṣasya pravartate |puṇyaprabhāvo vipulo
yasya śīle rataṃ manaḥ

bhavajanyaṃ phalaṃ yasya (yasmai) dharmo na rocate |dharmo hi nayati svarga dharmacārī sukhānvitaḥ

dharmādṛte puruṣaḥ narakaṃ yātietadeva hi paryāptaṃ yad dharmaparipālanam |dharmādṛte hi puruṣo
narakānupadhāvati

dharmavigarhaṇānmaraṇaṃ śreyaḥśreyo bhaveddhi maraṇaṃ na tu dharmavigarhaṇam |dharmeṇa varjito
lokaḥ saṃsāre sarvadā bhramet

dharmavirahitasya duḥkhamayaṃ jīvanamdharmacakṣurvimuktasya mohenākrāntacetasaḥ |vṛthā saukhyamidaṃ
dṛṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā yāto yathā'paraḥ

dharmāṅkuro manaḥ kṣetre naiva rohatyacetasaḥ |yasya śīlapradā buddhiḥ dharmācaraṇatatparā

śubhena saviśuddhena bhāvitena prayatnataḥ |prayānti tat padaṃ śāntaṃ yatra duḥkhaṃ na vidyate

indriyavaśī māraṃ nātivartateindriyāṇāṃ vaśe yastu viṣayeṣu tathaiva ca |sa sarvabandhanairbaddhaḥ
sa māraṃ nātivartate

pāpakairdharmairalipta eva svasthaḥaliptapāpakairdharmaiḥ nirdhanāt kanakadyutiḥ |sa muktabhavakāntāraḥ
svastho bhavati sarvataḥ

buddhādīnāṃ pūjayā nirvāṇalābhaḥbuddho yeṣāṃ bahumato nityaṃ dharmaśca gocaraḥ |śuśrūṣā''cāryapādānāṃ
śraddadhānaśca karmaṇām

triratnapūjayā nityaṃ sadbuddhiśca (su) nirmalā |mātāpitṛṇāṃ pūjātaḥ nirvāṇapuragāminām

pravrajyābhāvadharmāśca sameṣāṃ samprakīrtitāḥ |brahmacaryāttacaryāṇāṃ sarvasaukhyāgrakārakāḥ

dharmadānaṃ sarvottamamdānānāmuttamaṃ dānaṃ dharmadānaṃ prakathyate |udyogānāṃ sadā dhyānaṃ
yena gacchati nirvṛtim

agrayastathāgataḥ proktaḥūrdhvādhastiryaguktasya lokasyānekakarmaṇaḥ |agrayastathāgataḥ prokto
dharmāṇāṃ tattvadarśakaḥ

vargāṇāṃ cārthasaṅghore pravaraḥ śānta ucyate |kṣetrāṇāṃ trividhaṃ puṇyaṃ guṇaduḥkhobhayaṃ tataḥ

mātā pitṛsamaḥ pūjya upādhyāyaḥ sadā bhavet |sa unmīlayate cakṣurvaśagocaratāṃ prati

niḥsukhā viṣayā matāḥagrāhyā vā sadā dṛṣṭā muninā tattvadarśinā |sukhasya bhūmayo hyetā niḥsukhā
viṣayā matāḥ

yadyevaṃ kurute dharma nirmalaṃ mārgadarśinam |saukhyaṃ tasya bhavennityaṃ na saukhyaṃ devabhūmiṣu

bhavāntareṣu sukṛtaṃ pṛṣṭhato dehināṃ sthitam |sa āhūya prayatnena sevitavyaḥ sadā naraiḥ

anāgate bhayaṃ yo hi paśyati buddhacakṣuṣā |sa paṇḍitaḥ sadā dhīro mūrkhatvādatibhīrukaḥ

vipattijaṃ bhayaṃ dṛṣṭvā (mārga) paśyati buddhimān |sa hi vighne tu samprāpte na viṣādena bādhyate

atha mūḍhamatirnityaṃ viṣayāneva sevate |vimohitaḥ sa viṣayaiḥ paścāttāpena dahyate

samagraṃ janma puṇyāni karaṇīyāniyāvat samagraṃ janmedaṃ jñānaṃ yāti vināvilam |tāvat kuruta
puṇyāni duḥkhaṃ hayakṛtapuṇyatā

kṣayaṃ prayānti puṇyāni tvaritaṃ yāti jīvitam |dharmasaṅgrahaṇe yatnaḥ karttavyastuṣite suraiḥ

yo hi dharma parityajya pramādopahato naraḥ |na sañcinoti puṇyāni sa paścādapi tapyate

na yāvadāyāti jarā na vyādhiḥ saha mṛtyunā |tāvat kāryāṇi puṇyāni mā paścāt paritapyatha

asaṅgṛhītapuṇyasya pramādopahatasya ca |narake kāraṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ pramādastvāṃ haniṣyati

kiṃ tasya jīvitenārthaḥ kiṃ bhāgyaiḥ kiṃ ca bāndhavaiḥ |sabalendriyatāṃ prāpya yo na dharmarataḥ
sadā

ahanyahani karttavyaṃ dharmasaṅgrahaṇe manaḥ |viratiścāpi pāpebhyaḥ sādhūnāṃ darśane na ca

śīlena yaḥ suro janma labdhvedaṃ kāmamohitaḥ |na sañcinoti puṇyāni sa bhavaṃ nātivartate

dharmaratāḥ sadā vandyāḥjñānārambhābhiratayaḥ śīlaratnavibhūṣitāḥ |kāmarāgāddhi ye bhītā devānāṃ
devasammatāḥ

devāste hi sadā vandyā ye dharme niratāḥ sadā |ye tu nityaṃ bhavāsaktāste sarve nidhanaṃ gatāḥ

dharmasetumimaṃ prāpya pārāvāragataṃ mahat |na sañcarati yastūrṇa bhavānnaiva pramucyate

śubhaṃ naiva praṇaśyatiśubhānucāridharmaiśca śubhaṃ bhavati sarvadā |kalpakoṭisahastreṇa śubhaṃ
naiva praṇaśyati

puṇyaprāptyarthe karaṇīyāni karttavyānisaṅgṛhītaṃ sadā śīlaṃ jñānaṃ ca parivartitam |dānaṃ cābhikṣayā
dattaṃ bhavati svarasātmakam

sadaiva guṇāḥ sevyāḥdoṣāstrayaḥ praṇaśyanti tribhirdānādibhirnṛṇām |tasmād doṣān parityajya
guṇāḥ sevyāḥ prayatnataḥ

jñānena laukikaṃ duḥkhaṃ naśyatisaṃsargo dharmaśīlānāṃ jñānārambhaḥ prayatnataḥ |naśyati bhavajaṃ
duḥkhamarkapādairyathā tamaḥ

abhyupeyo devarato devatābhiśca vandyate |prāpya janmāntara cāpi nirvṛtiṃ cāśu gacchati

||iti puṇyavarga ekatriṃśaḥ ||

## 32 devavargaḥ

(32) devavargaḥsaugatamārge carantaḥ puruṣā devatulyāḥpanthāno muninā śāstre uktā ye tattvadarśinā
|taistu samprasthitāste (hi) puruṣā devasammatāḥ

sugatokto mārgaḥsatyaṃ hi dānaṃ ca tathaiva maitrīsattveṣu rakṣā priyavāditā ca |samyaktvadṛṣṭirvimalaṃ
manaścapanthānamāhustridivasya buddhāḥ

śukladharmasamāyuktaḥ śuklacittasamanvitaḥ |sukhāt sukhataraṃ yāti jyotirjyotiḥparāyaṇaḥ

jyoti(hi)rjyotiṣā pūrṇa dīpo dīpāntarād yathā |tasmāddhi paramāṃllokān prayātā samprapadyate

ācāravān devānāmantikaṃ vrajetyasya śuddhaṃ sadā cittaṃ nirmalaṃ maṇivat sadā |sa śānto nirmamo
dhīmān devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

dhyānaśīlasamādhibhyo yasya cittaṃ śubhānvitam |sa dhīmān kāñcanaprakhyo devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

prāṇātipātād virataḥ sarvasattvadayāparaḥ |ṛtusroto'nukampāśca devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

śrutavān sarvalokasya krūrakarmavivarjitaḥ |aliptaḥ pāpakairdharmairdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

tṛṇavat kāñcanaṃ yasya kāmā yasya viṣopamāḥ |sa kāmavarjako dhīmān devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

nākṛṣyate manaḥ kāmairviṣayai rāgahetubhiḥ |samantātbhavakāntārairdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

bhinnāḥ paramparā ādau mitravān dhanabāndhavaḥ |yaḥ karoti susaṃśliṣṭā devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

yasya buddhisthitaṃ veśma na buddhiḥ kvāpi rāgiṇī |sa jitārirviśuddhātmā devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

praśastakāyakarmānto yaḥ pāpavirataḥ sukhī |sa kāmavirato dhyāyī devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

pāpamitravinirmuktastṛṣṇāviṣavivarjitaḥ |na baddhaḥ strībhayaiḥ pāśairdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

prayatnavādī yo dharme dānaśīlasamādhimān |nityodyukto dṛḍhamatirdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

samyagbandhano yena pāśaśchinno yathā'sinā |sacchinnapāśaḥ svavaśī devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

śubhakarmavipākena devaloke udbhavaḥmanuṣyabhūtā ye sattvāścaranti sukṛtāvahāḥ |tena karmavipākena
suraloke prasūyate

dharmapathāśritā eva balinaḥmanuṣyāṇāṃ balād devā devānāṃ balino narāḥ |anyonyabalino te ye
saddharmapathamāsthitāḥ

tistro'pāyabhūmayaḥdevānāṃ sugatirmartyāḥ martyānāṃ sugatiḥ surāḥ |apāyabhūmayastisraḥ śubhakarmavivarjitāḥ

sarva sukhaṃ dharmādhīnamdharmādhīnaṃ sukhaṃ sarva dharmādhīnā hi nirvṛtiḥ |dharmaḥ supteṣu
jāgarti dharmo hi paramā gatiḥ

devairasurā jitāḥdharmeṇa nirjito'dharmaḥ satyenānṛtiko jitaḥ |jñānena varjito moho devaistu
hyasurā jitāḥ

devalokaṃ sukhodayamsopānabhūtā ye tāni karmāṇi tridivasya hi |yoniṃ tyaktvā narā yānti devalokaṃ
sukhodayam

vāksaṃyamena budhāstridivaṃ sukhaṃ bhuñjanticaturvidho vāṅniyamaḥ ko'pi trividhapañcadhā |saptasopānamārūpyaṃ
gacchanti tridivaṃ budhāḥ

prabhayā te ca divyantaḥ svaśarīreṇa jātayā |ramante svargabhuvane rañjitāḥ svena karmaṇā

śīlameva śubhasya kāraṇamnityāmodavihārā(ye) nityaṃ saukhyavihāriṇaḥ |yad devā devabhavane śīlaṃ
tatra hi kāraṇam

yadapsaraḥ parivṛtā yat sūryaśaśisannibhāḥ |devāḥ samantād deveṣu tatsarvaśubhahetukam

yadīpsitaṃ sambhavati sambhūtaṃ ca na hīyate |vartate ca śubhaṃ nityaṃ tat sarva śubhahetukam

śubhacārī devānāṃ samatāṃ vrajetśubhacārī sadā dānī sarvabhūtadayārataḥ |dānamaitryā sadā yukto
devānāṃ samatāṃ vrajet

prāṇātipātād virataḥ sarvasattvadayāparaḥ |samyagājīvakarmānto devānāṃ saṅgatiṃ vrajet

adatte na ratiḥ kiñcid dāne cāsya sadāmatiḥ |śāntendriyamatirdhīmān devasaṅgatimaśnute

mithyākāmairvimukto yaḥ satpathābhirataḥ sadā |nirvāṇakāṃkṣī vimalo devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

vimanaskaṃ hi yat prīte puruṣe kurute laghu |madyavarjī paraṃ dhīro devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

pramādavirahitaḥ sukhamāpnotisukha (prāṇo) hi yo devaḥ pramādaṃ nānusevate |sukhāt sukhamavāpnoti
nirvṛttiṃ cādhigacchati

kṣayāvasānaṃ tat saukhyaṃ nirvāṇamiti śāśvatam |tat saprāpyavimāneṣu rājante puruṣottamāḥ

uccāduccaro merustasmāduccaṃ sadā sukham |śubhena niyato janturakaniṣṭhān surān (jayet)

niravadye kutastṛptirdevaloke viśeṣataḥ |atīva saukhyaṃ labhate kasmād deveṣu sarvadā

kaḥ saukhyamupalabhyate?tṛṣṇāgniparidagdhena na saukhyamupalabhyate |evaṃ sukṛtadagdhena na
saukhyamupalabhyate

trividhaṃ sukṛtaṃ kṛtvā triprakāraṃ trihetukam |etadagryaṃ tribhūmiṣṭhaṃ (triguṇaṃ ca) phalaṃ
mahat

ahiṃsādānaparamā ye saddharmaparāyaṇāḥ |satyakṣāntidamairyuktāḥ tridivaṃ(te) samāgatāḥ

divyābharaṇasampannā divyamālyavibhūṣitāḥ |yad devā divyamatayaḥ (kurvanti) śubhameva tat

devānāṃ yanmahatsaukhyaṃ(na)nyūnādhikyamāsthitam |nyūna-madhyaṃ tu yasyaitat phalaṃ puṇyasya
dṛśyate

puṇyakartā devalokaṃ gacchatiyena yāvaddhi yat puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati dehinā |tasya tāvaddhi tat
saukhyaṃ devalokeṣu pacyate

śīlasaṃrakṣaṇamāvaśyakamsvāgataṃ tava bho bhadra! sukṛtaṃ kṛtavānasi |saptadhā rakṣitaṃ śīlaṃ
tasyaitat phalamāgatam

ramasva saha daivataiḥvanopavanaśaileṣu padmākaravaneṣu ca |harmyāgreṣu ramasva (tvaṃ kāñcaneṣu)
sadaivataḥ

vanopavanaśaileṣu vaidūryaśikhareṣu ca |vanādriṣu ca naikeṣu ramasva saha daivataiḥ

kalpavṛkṣeṣu ramyeṣu nadīprastravaṇeṣu ca |saritsu ca viśālāsu (ramasva) saha daivataiḥ

strotasvinyādiyukteṣu parvateṣu nadīṣu ca |nagareṣu mahārtheṣu ramasva saha daivataiḥ

madagandhipraroheṣu nīlotpalavaneṣu ca |yakṣasadmasu ramyeṣu ramasva saha daivataiḥ

bhūmibhāgeṣu cānteṣu ratnākaravaneṣu ca |vimāneṣu ca ramyeṣu ramasva saha daivataiḥ

pañcāṅgikena tūryeṇa manaḥprahlādakāriṇā |nṛtyamānaḥ sukhī nityaṃ ramasva saha daivataiḥ

śīlabījaṃ śodhayitvā śīleṣu vividheṣu ca |krīḍa tvaṃ vividhairdivyairyathārthamanusevase

yatprabhāmālino devā ramante vividhaiḥ sukhaiḥ |tacchubhasya phalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ nirmalasya viśeṣataḥ

yadetairvividhaiḥ saukhyairdevāḥ krīḍantyanekaśaḥ |na vayaṃ hetavastatra (tatra hetuḥ) purākṛtam

kūṭāgārāṇi sarvāṇi karmacitrāṇi sarvadā |bhunakti devo deveṣu satkṛtenopabṛṃhitaḥ

pāśatrayavimuktasya pañcabhiḥ pālitasya vai |ekadharmavyatītasya devaloko mahīyate

pramudyaccetasāṃ puṃsāṃ spaṣṭaceṣṭā samāhitā |āgatā devasadanaṃ svakarmaphalasākṣiṇī

sukṛtaphalamuparyupari saukhyāni (tathā ca) sukṛtasya vai |bhuñjanti vibudhāḥ svarga yaddhi
pūrvakṛtānugam

sākṣibhūtā ime sarvakarmaṇāṃ vividhā drumāḥ |nirantaraṃ susadṛśaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ

bhāgyaṃ phalati sarvatrayena yena vipākena yatra yatropapadyate |puruṣo labhate svasya prārabdhasya
śubhāśubham

śubhakarmaṇā prāṇī nityaṃ deveṣu jāyateśubhena karmaṇā janturnityaṃ deveṣu jāyate |tathā'śubhena
narake patanti puruṣādhamāḥ

kāmino maraṇaṃ nāvagacchantiśubhāśubhābhyāṃ saṃraktāḥ kāminaḥ kāmamohitāḥ |nāvagacchanti maraṇaṃ
yadavaśyaṃ bhaviṣyati

śubhāśubhavipāko'yaṃ yo vṛkṣeṣūpalabhyate |na saukhyād viramantyete manaḥ saukhyena mohitāḥ

sukṛtaṃ kṛtvā mānavāḥ deveṣu yāntitrividhaṃ sukṛtaṃ kṛtvā bhāvayitvā ca saptadhā |trisaṃkhyākān
ripūn hatvā yānti deveṣu mānavāḥ

kaḥ devānāmantikaṃ vrajet?nāsūyati kriyākleśān na ca nandīmasūyati |sa nandyasūyakaḥ śuddho
devānāmantikaṃ gataḥ

vinindya mātsaryamidaṃ duḥkhasyāyatanaṃ mahat |samaṃ ca trividhaṃ dattvā devānāmantikaṃ gataḥ

prāṇināṃ praṇayaṃ nityaṃ rakṣayitvā'nukampayā |maitracittaḥ sadā dānto devānāmantikaṃ gataḥ

adattaṃ ca dhanaṃ dattvā dattvā''nandaṃ ca sarvataḥ |cetanābhāvitamatirdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

mātṛvat paradārāṃśca dṛṣṭvā tattvārthacintakaḥ |alipto pāpakairdharmairdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

(kṛtaḥ) svacittaprītyartha jihvāraṇisamudbhavaḥ |kathyate sa mṛṣāvādastaṃ hitvā sugatiṃ vrajet

paiśūnyaṃ ca sadā hitvā maitryanarthakaraṃ padam |ślakṣṇaprabhaḥ ślakṣṇamatiḥ devānāmantikaṃ
vrajet

pāruṣyaṃ śatruvaddhīro varjayatyeva sarvadā |ślakṣṇaprabhāmatirnityaṃ sarveṣu gatigāmikaḥ

adharmo yasya jihvāgre na bhūto na bhaviṣyati |sadā duṣkālatattvajño devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

yenedaṃ rakṣitaṃ śīlaṃ saptadhā buddhadeśitam |sa dhīraḥ śīlatattvajño devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

kaḥ saphalaḥ dharmajñaḥ?vividhakarmavaśagaṃ janmedaṃ labhate suraiḥ |tatprāpya yo na dharmajñaḥ
sa paścāt paritapyate

sukarmaṇā śubhajaṃ phalamvanopavanaramyo'yaṃ latāvedikamaṇḍapaḥ |yadvicitramayo lokastat sarva
śubhajaṃ phalam

yena yena yathā karma kṛtaṃ bhavati śobhanam |tasya tasya tathā dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ tadanugāmikam

pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate devairhīnamadhyottamaṃ sukham |yena yena yathā cīrṇaṃ tasya tasya tathā phalam

kukarmaṇā duḥkhajaṃ phalamvicitraveṣāḥ saṃmūḍhā devā mohavaśānugāḥ |tannāśānmanasā mūḍhā na
paśyanti mahadbhayam

vicitrakāmaratayo vicitraphalakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |na vā kurvanti karmāṇi te'surā mūḍhacetasaḥ

ke premaparāyaṇāḥ?phalaṃ yeṣāṃ priyaṃ cittaṃ na ca śīle ratā matiḥ |te pradīpaṃ parityajya premālokaparāyaṇāḥ

hetuphalatattvajñāḥ sukhino bhavantiye hetuphalasādṛśyenecchanti surasattamān |te hetuphalatattvajñā
bhavanti sukhabhāginaḥ

jñānādeva muktiḥvinābījaṃ phalaṃ nāsti vinā dīpaṃ kutaḥ prabhā? |vinā śīlaiḥ kutaḥ svargo muktirjñānaṃ
vinā kutaḥ ?

kaḥ dhīmataḥ ?tatsukhaṃ tadvimuktasya gatakāṅkṣasya tāyinaḥ |vimuktakāmatṛṣṇasya nirmamasya
ca dhīmataḥ

sukarmaiḥ sukhaṃ bhavatyevayadidaṃ karmajaṃ saukhyaṃ sarva (tajjñeya) kalmaṣam |yaṃ neṣṭakaṃ
bhavatyeva tat sarvamamalaṃ smṛtam

devalokasya varṇanamyo manorathakṛtsnasya bahirantaśca (sarvataḥ) |sarvālokaḥ sadāloko devatāgaṇasevitaḥ

virājate girivaro ratnamāṇikyasannibhaḥ |prabhūtasalilo yaśca padminībhiḥ samāvṛtaḥ

vanopavanaramyo'yaṃ mṛgapakṣiniṣevitaḥ |kandarodarasaṃrambho bhitvā gaganamutthitaḥ

kutra devataiḥ sevyate?sevyate devatairnityaṃ divyamālyavibhūṣitaiḥ |nṛtyagītaprakṛṣṭābhirdevatābhiśca
sarvataḥ

pañcāṅgikena tūryeṇa prerita iva lakṣyate |śirobhūto mahāramyaḥ prabhāmālī samantataḥ

sukṛtena śubhenāyaṃ karmaṇādhiguṇena vai |yaṃ samāśritya krīḍanti devavṛndāni sarvataḥ

ke svarga gacchanti?dānaśīlayutā vṛddhā nityaṃ tadgatamānasāḥ |ye bhavanti sadā dāntāste janāḥsvargagāminaḥ

saṃkṣiptamanasaḥ śāntāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ |vairiṇāṃ viṣayo nityaṃ praśāntamanasastu ye

(vītarāgā vītamohāste janāḥ) svargagāminaḥ |bhavābhavena tīvreṇa bādhate ( yatra) sādhanam |sudāntamānasāṃ
dhīrāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

satyamārgavilambena hayatīva sukhabhāginaḥ |sārāsāravidhijñāśca te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

saṃsāre ye na rakṣanti svamano (nanu ) dehinaḥ |nirvāṇābhiratā nityaṃ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

vṛkṣamūle śmaśāne vā tathā ca girikandare |dhyāyinaḥ sattvamanasaste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

mātrajñā deśakālajñāḥ pāpamitra(vi)varjitāḥ |maitreṇa cetasā ye tu te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

na snānadarśanaratā narāḥ manmathavāriṇā |ekāntagāminaḥ śāntāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

kṣaṇe kṣaṇe sadā kāyaṃ paśyantyaśucisambhavam |kāryākāryavidhijñā ye te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

dharmāṇāṃ dharmatāṃ ye ca paśyanti vividhā samāḥ |na ca rakṣanti saṃsāre te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

vedanā madhyatattvāntamanekavidhasambhavam |paśyanti ca na rakṣanti te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

māyopamaṃ ca kṣaṇikaṃ gandharvanagaropamam |ye jānanti (sadā) cittāḥ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

ekalakṣaṇatattvajñā vilakṣaṇavidaḥ svayam |nirvāṇarāgamanasaste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

mātṛvat paradārān ye pitṛvat sarvadehinaḥ |paśyanti ye bhayaṃ loke te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

śūnyavargagato nityaṃ sattvānāṃ priyavādinaḥ |akrūrā ṛddhimanasaste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

kāṣṭhavalloṣṭhavat sarva paravittasamīkṣakāḥ |saṃtuṣṭāḥ svena cittena te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

na rātrau na divā yeṣāṃ kausīdyadyutiriṣyate |nityodyuktavihārā ye te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

kaukṛtyaṃ styānamiddhaṃ ca kausīdyaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |varjayanti sadā dhanyāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

dauḥśīlyaṃ pañcarandhrebhyaḥ pariśuddhamanekadhā |saṃkṣipanti sadā duḥkhaṃ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

upādānacatuṣṭvajñāḥ satyāni ca tathaiva ca |ye paśyanti budhāḥ prajñāṃ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

duḥkhaṃ duḥkhavipākaśca duḥkheṣu ca manaśca yat |paśyanti ye sadā tattvaṃ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

tīvravyasanamāpannā ye (ca) dharmāvimuñcakāḥ |śāntāśca dharmamatayaste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

śuklāvadātaṃ ye vastraṃ pāṃsukūlaṃ tathaiva ca |piṇḍapātaratā nityaṃ te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

adaṇḍāḥ śāntamanaso nityaṃ dhyānavihāriṇaḥ |naiṣkarmyaniratāḥ sarve te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

mṛṣṭaṃ ca yadi vāṅmṛṣṭaṃ yathecchāvidhimāgatam |santuṣyanti na kupyanti te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

śuklāvadātaṃ ye vastraṃ pāṃsukūlaṃ tathaiva ca |saṃvṛtau caiva saṃtuṣṭāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

śayyātale yathā bhūmau harmyāgre vā tathā'pare |na duṣyanti (na hṛṣyanti) te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

cakṣurviṣayamāpannaṃ yatkarma sāmparāyikam |tattvato ye prapaśyanti te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

apriyaṃ vā priyaṃ vāpi ye śrutvā tīvrasambhramāt |akṣubdhamatayo muktāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

ṣaḍindriyāṇi sarvāṇi viṣayāstu tathaiva ca |saṃkṣipanti na rakṣanti te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

yathā karma kṛtaṃ sarvamaviśeṣeṇa tattvataḥ |paśyantyamanaso dhanyāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

karmaṇāṃ ca vipākaṃ ca kṛtaṃ (ye dhīracetasā) |bibhyatīha sadā duḥkhete janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

ityetāni mahārthāni nityaṃ duḥkhakarāṇi ca |kurvanti vidhivat sarva te janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

||iti devavargo dvātriṃśaḥ ||

## 33 sukhavargaḥ

(33) sukhavargaḥsukhasvarūpanirūpaṇamanuttareṣu saukhyeṣu dhyānopātteṣu ye ratāḥ |teṣāṃ sukhaṃ
yathāvat syāt nirvāṇapuradarśakam

navena sukhaduḥkhena purāṇamabhihanyate |devasyaitannavenaiva purāṇamabhihanyate

dhū(ma) miśraṃ yathā kāṣṭhaṃ vi(ṣṭhā) miśraṃ yathodanam |tathā sukhamidaṃ sarvamasvatvaṃ nāvagamyate

nirvāṇapuragāmināṃ sukhamtat sukhaṃ yad vitṛṣṇānāmekāntasukhacāriṇām |nirmohiṇāmarāgāṇāṃ nirvāṇapuragāminām

teṣāṃ vimalamādyantaṃ saukhyānāmapi tat sukham |yeṣāṃ tṛṣṇānugā cāśā sarvathā naiva cetasi

saṅgṛhītasya cittasya nirātmasya ca sarvataḥ |kāryākāryeṣu mūḍhasya sukhaṃ nityamupasthitam

kaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sukhī?sā bahirnihatā yena nandisaṃsārahetukī |sa dhīraḥ pāragaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sukhī
nirvāṇamāśritaḥ

naitat sukhena tṛṣṇānāṃ yad rāgadveṣasaṃyutam |yatra rāgādinirmuktaṃ tat sukhaṃ nirmalaṃ matam

kutra tṛṣṇā na bādhate?devaloke samāsādya yaḥ suro nāvamanyate |sa sukhāt sukhatāṃ yāti yatra
tṛṣṇā na bādhate

tadantyasukhi śreyo yatra mṛtyurna vidyate |mṛtyupāśairna baddhasya na sukhaṃ vidyate kvacit
|yat sukhaṃ kāmajanakaṃ na tat saukhyaṃ satāṃ matam

yatra kāmavinirmuktastatsukhāt sukhamuttamam |yat sukhaṃ janayet śreyaḥ (payomiśraṃ) yathodanam

yatra tṛṣṇāvinirmuktiḥ payomiśraṃ yathodanam |yathā padmavane gṛddhā yānti(te) kravyabhakṣiṇaḥ

evaṃ śānteṣvaraṇyeṣu na bhāntyaśubhacāriṇaḥ |kvacicchāntaṃ vanaṃ ramyaṃ kvacid devāḥ pramādinaḥ

kaḥ paramaṃ sukhaṃ prāpnoti?viparītaṃ na sadṛśaṃ bhānoḥ śītā yathā prabhā |gatatṛṣṇasya yat
saukhyaṃ muktaduḥkhasya tāyinaḥ

tasyāntareṇa saukhyasya sukhametanna gaṇyate |dhyāyinastvapramattasya muktapāpasya sarvadā

tat sukhaṃ tat paraṃ saukhyaṃ nedaṃ tṛṣṇāvidāṃ matam |munisevyaṃ vanamidaṃ sevitaṃ ca subhāṣitaiḥ

nārhā (yūyaṃ) rāgagaṇaṃ sevituṃ bho surottamāḥ |yadetad bhavatāṃ saukhyametanna khalu śāśvatam

tat sukhaṃ paramaṃ śāntaṃ vītatṛṣṇai niṣevyate |niḥsevitaṃ vanamidaṃ ye gatāḥ paramaṃ padam

yat prāpya sarvaduḥkhasyacchedo bhavati sarvathā |brahmacaryādanirmuṣṭāḥ śīlālāpena vañcitāḥ

bhikṣūṇāṃ vane vāsa eva sukhāvahaḥnārhanti sevituṃ ramyaṃ vanaṃ śāntaṃ subhāṣitam |śāntaṃ ca
bhāvitaṃ (caiva) ramate śubhagocare

na rāgacāriṇāṃ cittaṃ ramate vanagocare |na rāgavyākulaṃ cittaṃ vaneṣu labhate dhṛtim

kaḥ puruṣottamaḥ?śravyā'mūḍhā matiryasya nityaṃ tribhuvanaṃ kare |sa ratiṃ labhate śāntiṃ vane
puruṣasattamaḥ

sukhāya vanaṃ sevyamsa kalparāgakuṭilo nityaṃ rāgādibhirvṛtaḥ |sa śāntiṃ naiva labhate vane
śānte sukhāvahe

yeṣāṃ tu manasā nityaṃ vane dhyānaniyoginām |vanaṃ teṣāṃ sadāramyaṃ na tu rāgagaveṣiṇām

vaneṣu bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ nagareṣu na kupyate |tasmād vanaṃ sadā sevyaṃ nagaraṃ naiva śasyate

vikṣipyate hi nagare nṛṇāṃ rāgādibhirvṛtaḥ |vikṣipta mohakuṭilaṃ vanaṃ bhūyaḥ prasīdati

tasmād vanaṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ yogināmālayaṃ mahat |saṃsevyaṃ vītamanasā yasya tad vītakalmaṣam

ratiṃ mā kṛthāpraśāntendriyacittasya yā ratiryogino hṛdi |nāsau śaktiḥ sahasrasya (mānavānāṃ)
bhaviṣyati

yā dhyāyino ratirdṛṣṭā vyavadānāya sarvadā |na yāmeṣvapi sā dṛṣṭā nityaṃ rāgānurāgiṇī

ratiryā kāmavaśagā sā nityaṃ duḥkhasambhavā |yā tu kleśavaśāt prītiḥ (sā prītiḥ) śāśvatā nahi

kaḥ śreyaspadaṃ prāpnoti?śreyo vaneṣu caritaṃ tattaduccaritaṃ nṛbhiḥ |yasmāt tat pratibaddhaṃ
hi śreyasāṃ padamucyate

susambhṛtena dharmeṇa rakṣiteneva cetasā |sudṛṣṭaṃ labhate sthānaṃ yatra doṣo na vidyate

yaḥ kṣiptamanasā nityaṃ na ca dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |teṣāṃ vṛthā sukhamidaṃ gacchati na nivartate

tattvajñā duḥkhaṃ na paśyantiye tu tattvavido dhīrāḥ paśyanti jagataḥ sthitim |anityaduḥkhaśūnyānāṃ
teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na vidyate

sukhadharmasya caraṇaṃ jñānasya ca niṣevaṇam |ahiṃsā satyavacanaṃ tadapyekāntataḥ sthitam

kaḥ svarga yāti?ekadharmavyatītā ye ye'dharmaparivañcakāḥ |tristhānalakṣaṇāviṣṭāste janāḥ svargagāminaḥ

sukhasya svarūpamudayavyayadharmāṇāmanityaṃ karmajaṃ hi tat |tat sukhaṃ sāstravaṃ nityaṃ na
bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati

tat sukhaṃ tadvitṛṣṇasya nīrāgasya hi dehinaḥ |muktirbhavati doṣasya pārasthasya hi tāpinaḥ

tat kiñcit sāsravaṃ saukhyaṃ tat sarva kṣaṇikaṃ matam |rāgabandhād vinirmuktaṃ tat sarva niścalaṃ
sukham

ye na kṣipanti duḥkhena sukhe yeṣāṃ na saṅgatiḥ |te duḥkhasukhanirmuktā nirvāṇasukhagāminaḥ

anupāyena ye mūḍhāḥ prārthayanti sukhaṃ sadā |bālukābhiryathā tailaṃ yallabhyaṃ nityameva tat

na cetasā naraḥ prājño manorathaśatairapi |śakro'pi tat sukhaṃ kartu yathā karma kṛtaṃ mahat

sukhāya dharmamācaretsasukhaṃ yasya tu manaḥ saddharmānucaro bhavet |duḥkhairmuktyabhilāṣo'yaṃ
sa dharme kurute matim

nāhetukaṃ sukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā trividhātmakam |sukhe duḥkhe pṛthagbhāve tasmānnu sukṛta
caret

nedaṃ saukhyaṃ sadā śastamadhruvaṃ vipralopi ca |tṛṣṇāviṣeṇa sammiśraṃ viṣamiśraṃ yathodanam

tat saukhyaṃ (hi)satāṃ śastaṃ yatra mṛtyurna vidyate |na ca priyeṇa viśleṣo nāpriyeṇa samāgamaḥ

kīdṛśaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhajanakam ?yadetat strīmayaṃ saukhyametad duḥkhāya kalpyate |tadbījavartakā
dṛṣṭā narakeṣūpapattaye

yat sukhaṃ duḥkhajanakaṃ kathaṃ tat sukhamiṣyate?duḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ jñeyaṃ pariṇāmavaśena tat

yadetad bhujyate saukhyametat kālena naśyati |sūryastāstaṅgatasyaivaṃ raśmayaḥ saha cāriṇaḥ

vikṛtiṃ yasya (ca ) manaḥ sukhaduḥkhairna gacchati |sa dhīmān suraloke ca gatvānyallabhate sukham

bhuktaṃ sukhaṃ purāṇaṃ tu hīnakarma karoti ca |purāṇaṃ sukṛtaṃ śīrṇa mṛtyukāle na budhyate

sarva sukhamanityaṃ bhavatiyadidaṃ dṛśyate saukhyaṃ manovākkāmajaṃbhṛśam |anityaṃ tad vināśatvamacireṇa
bhaviṣyati

phenabudbudasaṅkāśaṃ marīcyudakasannibham |cañcalormi sukhaṃ sarva vinipāto bhavārṇave

niṣpratīkāraviṣamaḥ sarvabhūtabhayāvahaḥ |cakravātapravego (vai) mṛtyurājaiṣa dhāvati

nāśayitvā sukhaṃ sarva nāśayitvā ca jīvitam |karmasaṅkalpavāhyeṣu lokamanyatra neṣyati

yadatīva sukhaṃ nṛṇāṃ taddhi saukhyāya kalpyate |yannaiṣyati sukhaṃ kiñcit taddhi naiva vigaṇyate

vartamānaṃ tu yat saukhyaṃ tṛṣṇāviṣavivarjitam |sarva hyanātmajaṃ duḥkhamanityaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ balam

laukikaṃ sukhaṃ na sukhamyad sukhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu na śastaṃ tattvadarśibhiḥ |tena matvā kathaṃ
devā bhavanti vigatajvarāḥ?

aviṣṭovatakālo'yaṃ bhairavo yāti sattvaram |yo bhokṣyate surān sarvān śuṣkendhanamivānalaḥ

atiyāti sukhaṃ sarva kriyatāṃ śraiyasaṃ manaḥ |mā paścāt saṃbhavo yoge mṛtyukālo bhaviṣyati

sukhamasthiraṃ bhavatijanmāntarasahasreṣu yad muktaṃ karmajaṃ sukham |taraṅgasannibhaḥ kvāyaṃ(jānīyād)
bāliśo'sthiram

kaḥ sukhena prasīdati?na sukhaistṛpyate bālastathā kāṣṭhairyathā'nalaḥ |tasmānna (sukha) saktasya
sukhaṃ bhavati naiṣṭhikam

viṣasya doṣamuktasya kāmadoṣānudarśinaḥ |dhyāyinaścāpramatasya tat sukhaṃ yadanāvilam

sukhī bhavati tat prāpya na sukhaṃ bhavajanmanaḥ |bandhamiśraṃ viṣaṃ yadvad dharmasaukhyodayastathā

kāmavirahitaḥ sukhamaśnuvatetasmāt tatsukhasaktānāṃ nityaṃ kāmagaveṣiṇām |bhavantyanekasaukhyā(ni)tasmāt
kāmo na jāyate

jñānenaivendriyāṇi svagocare nivartantenendriyāṇāṃ jayaḥ śakyaḥ karttu viṣayagocare |jñānena
hi nivartante indriyāṇi svagocare

bālā eva gatipañcake bhramantiduḥkhe sukhābhisaṃsaktā nityaṃ bālā (haya)medhasaḥ |viparyayā
paribhrāntā bhramanti gatipañcake

kutra sukhaṃ duḥkhasadṛśaṃ bhavati ?yadatyantasukhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tat sukhaṃ satyamucyate |yatra duḥkhaṃ
vipākaṃ syāt tat sukhaṃ duḥkhameva tat

pāpasyākaraṇameva sukhamanyāgatasya duḥkhasya pratighātayate budhaḥ |pāpasya hetujaṃ duḥkhaṃ
pāpasyākaraṇaṃ sukham

||iti sukhavargo trayastriṃśaḥ ||

## 34 mitravargaḥ

(34) mitravargaḥkaḥ pāpānnivārayati?tanmitraṃ mitramityuktaṃ yanmitraṃ sāmparāyikam |nivartayati
yaḥ pāpād vyasanāccāpi rakṣati

praveśayati yannityaṃ taddhitaṃ sāmparāyikam |mitraṃ bhavati tannṛṇāṃ na mitraṃ pāpakārakam

saṃsargajā doṣaguṇā bhavantiapūtiḥ pūtisaṃśleṣāt pūtirevopajāyate |na pūtiḥ pūtasaṃśleṣamapūtiṃ
karttumarhati

yādṛśena (hi) saṃśleṣaṃ kurute puruṣaḥ sadā |taddoṣāt sadṛśo dṛṣṭaḥ śubho vā yadi vā'śubhaḥ

na śubhaṃ duḥkhakārakamśubhārthī puruṣaḥ sarvānaśubhānnaiva sevate |tenāsau duḥkhamāpnoti na
śubhaṃ duḥkhakāraṇam

guṇadoṣayorlakṣaṇamsaṃśleṣajā guṇāḥ dṛṣṭā doṣāḥ saṃśleṣajātayaḥ |lakṣaṇaṃ guṇadoṣāṇāmidamuktaṃ
svabhāvajam

yaśasā yujyate yo hi nityaṃ sādhusamāgamāt |asādhu'saṅgamācchrīghraṃ prayāti puruṣādhamaḥ

satsaṅgatiphalametat sāraṃ sadā kārya yadasādhuvivarjanam |sādhubhiśca sadā vāso duṣṭāṇāṃ ca
vivarjanam

doṣān samuddhareddhīmān guṇavṛddhiṃ samācaret |(sādhu) mitraṃ prakurvīta kausīdyavimukho bhavet

na māninaṃ kusīdaṃ vā nityaṃ sarvānuśaṅkinam |liptapāpamatikrūraṃ mitraṃ kuryānna paṇḍitaḥ

udyuktaṃ mṛdujātīyaṃ dharmiṣṭhaṃ doṣavarjitam |samyagdṛṣṭiracapalaṃ mitraṃ seveta paṇḍitaḥ

na pāpakaṃ bhavenmitraṃ bhaveduttamapauruṣaḥ |uttamaṃ bhajamānasya na doṣebhyo bhayaṃ bhavet

kaḥ laghutāṃ yāti?rūpaiśvaryakulādīni bhidyante (yasya) dehinaḥ |bhayapradaṃ taṃ mātaṅgaḥ prayāntaṃ
naiva paśyati

udvṛttaḥ puruṣo nityaṃ pramādākulitendriyaḥ |laghutāṃ yāti loke'smin pretyapāpeṣu pacyate

rūpaiśvaryamadārthā ye te narāḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ |teṣāṃ na suśamaṃ (karma) pretyapāpeṣu pacyate

rūpaiśvaryakulārthā ye na te tattvasya bhāginaḥ |atattvabuddhayo bālā na taranti bhavārṇavam

jñānaśīlādiyutaṃ kulaṃ śreṣṭhametatkulaṃ ye vibhavā yaccānyat sukhamiṣyate |sarvāṇyetānyanityāni
tasmātteṣu na viśramet

na jñānaśīlanirmuktaṃ kuśalaṃ yānti paṇḍitāḥ |yeṣāṃ jñānaṃ ca śīlaṃ ca te kule mahati sthitāḥ

carituṃ cāmalaṃ śīlaṃ śīlameva mahādbhutam |mahākulaprasūtāste (paṇḍitāḥ) vaśamāninaḥ

dānaśīlatapodhyānasatyaiśvaryaparākramaiḥ |saṃyuktā ye kulīnāste ye na dharmavivarjitāḥ

naiśvaryajñānahīnasya na kulaṃ nāpi saṅgatiḥ |tasmātkulaṃ jñānamayaṃ jñānahīnaṃ na tat kulam

||iti mitravargaścatustriṃśaḥ ||

## 35 rājāvavādavargaḥ

(35) rājāvavādavargaḥdhārmiko rājā svarga yātibhuvaṃ parijano paśyan dharmacārī jitendriyaḥ
|sa rājā dhārmiko dhīmān svargalokopapattaye

kaḥ lobhanirmukto rājā?niyataṃ yaḥ karaṃ kāle dharmeṇa paribhujyate |sa rājā lobhanirmukto yāmānāmadhipo
bhavet

rājñaḥ svarūpamkṣamāvān priyavākyo yaḥ krodhaharṣādidhārakaḥ |sa mahīṃ pālayettvenāṃ loke hi
śreṣṭhatāṃ gataḥ

apakṣapātinaḥ śraddhā mitreṇa ca (vi)hanyate |sa rājanyasabhājetā devalokāya kalpyate

baddhadarśī mahātmā yo gurupūjaka eva ca |alolo yo dṛḍhamatirdevānāmantikaṃ vrajet

pūrve yat pitṛbhirdattaṃ devānupadiśanti ca |na ca hiṃsati bhūtāni sa deveṣūpapadyate

dānaśīle sadā dakṣo dharmavādī jitendriyaḥ |sa matvāryāṃ mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ devalokaṃ mahīyate

nādhārmikaṃ dhārayati dhārmikeṣu ca rakṣati |sa dharmaśīlasaṃśuddho devānāmantikaṃ vrajet

na strīṇāṃ vaśago rājā sādhūnāṃ ca vaset sadā |sa nirmalamatirdhīraḥ suralokopapattaye

na sarvasya vacogrāhī priyaḥ sādhujanasya tu |so'mṛtastattvadarśīvā nākṛṣṭa iva rohati

ko yāmānāmadhipo?yo dharmalobhamāyāti draviṇaṃ naiva lapsyate |sa lobhamalanirmukto yāmānāmadhipo
bhavet

na mithyādarśanenāpi strītvadarśanatatparaḥ |sa śuddha eva vimalo yāmānāmadhipo bhavet

kaḥ rājā devapriyo bhavati?prājñaḥ śīle sadāyukto dānenābhīkṣṇatāṃ gataḥ |pravijitya mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ
pretya devapriyo bhavet

priyasya tu bhaved vākyaṃ stotrotsavakaraṃ param |āhlādayitvā vasudhāmante devopapattaye

avisaṃvādakaṃ vākyaṃ yasya merurivācalam |satyasopānamāruhya devānāmantike gataḥ

hrāsa-vṛddhī ca bhūtānāmakasmāt kurute hi yaḥ |sa rājā vai paro devairdevaloke ca tiṣṭhati

manuṣyāntaratattvajño yo vetti hi balābalam |sa dhī-balābhyāṃ saṃyukto yāmānāmadhipo bhavet

traidhātukapadaṃ yacca ratnatrayamihocyate |yastat pūjayate rājā sa deveṣūpajāyate

kālaṃ niyatadarśī yaḥ prajānāṃ ca hite rataḥ |sarvato bhadrakāntāro devānāmadhipaḥ smṛtaḥ

nindāmalavinirmuktaḥ saṅgadoṣavivarjitaḥ |jñānagocarasampūjyo niyataṃ deva eva saḥ

kaḥ svarga yāti ?kausīdyadoṣarahito nityaṃ dṛḍhaparākramaḥ |nāśayitvā sa doṣaughān pretya svargeṣu
jāyate

sanmitraiḥ parivārito rājā devādhipo bhavatihitāni yasya mitrāṇi karmakartṛṇi nityaśaḥ |sa mitraiḥ
samparivṛto nṛpo devādhipo bhavet

nānuseveta durvṛttān vākkṣepeṇa ca varjitaḥ |sa sadyo viṣanirmuktaḥ surāṇāmadhipo bhavet

krodhaharṣavighātāya na ca pāpeṣu rakṣyate |sa pāpapaṅkanidhautaḥ suralokādhipaḥ sadā

na śaktaḥ pānabhojyeṣu saṃsaktastu śubhe sadā |sa śuddhadharmasandarśī vibudho'dhikatāṃ vrajet

kaḥ padamuttamaṃ prāpnoti?sucintitaṃ cintayati (yo) dharmeṣu ca vartate |dharmodayena dṛṣṭena
yathā yāti triviṣṭapam

saṃsārād dīrghasūtrād yastvaritaṃ dharmamācaret |sa dīrghasūtranirmuktaḥ prayāti padamuttamam

dharmeṇa prajāpālakaḥ svargasukhaṃ yātidharmeṇaiva prajā nityaṃ prapālayati (yo) nṛpaḥ |sa dhārmikaḥ
praśastātmā suraloke mahīyate

daśeme kuśalā dharmā ihoktāstattvadarśinā |yaste prakurute dharmān sa surādhipatāṃ vrajet

hetupratyayasandarśī mārgāmārgau tathaiva ca |sa dṛṣṭimalanirmukto vibudho'dhikatāṃ gataḥ

kīdṛśairguṇayutaiḥ rājā devānāmadhipo bhavatiyo devatāṃ pūjayati yathā cāhni mahīpatiḥ |sa devapūjito
bhūyo devānāmadhipo bhavet

anāvilena manasā prasannaścādhidārakaḥ |svasvadāraiśca santuṣṭo devānāmadhipo bhavet

kaḥ śīlavān?hīyate yo na viṣayaiḥ sarvabālāpahāribhiḥ |sa śīlavān divaṃ yāti nityaṃ śīlena rakṣitaḥ

avidyāvarjakān nityaṃ sevate yaḥ sudhārmikān |saddharmacintakaḥ saukhyaṃ kalpate sura(saṃ)sadi

(vyāpāraiḥ svasthacitto yaḥ ) pārṣadālāpahāribhiḥ |sa śīlavān divaṃ yāti nityaṃ śīlena rakṣitaḥ

saddharmī rājā eva vasudhādhipatiḥsadvyāpārāddharmamimaṃ pālayan vasudhādhipaḥ |praśāsti ca
mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ yāmānāmadhipo bhavet

satkarmaniratasya rājño bhṛtyo'pi śobhatesatkarmanirato bhṛtyo nṛpe sadguṇaśālini |jano nirmalatāṃ
yāti śaraccandra ivāmbare

kaḥ rājā devatulyo bhavati?hetulakṣyavidhijñā ye aviruddhāḥ parasparam |samyaksvāmyarthakarttāraḥ
devānāṃ vaśamāgatāḥ

||iti rājāvavādavargaḥ pañcatriṃśaḥ ||

## 36 stutivargaḥ

36) stutivargaḥbuddha-stutiḥsamasattvāgravedāya sarvasattveṣu bandhave |sanmārgasārthavāhāya
bhavabandhanabhedine

(nā)nādṛṣṭivibhedāya sarvasaṃśayamocine |samyagdṛṣṭiniveśāya namaḥ sañjñānacakṣuṣe

sarvasaṅkaṭabhedāya tridoṣamalaśodhine |namo nakṣatrabhūtāya sarvabījaphaloruhe

sarvaprajñākarāgrāya sarvadhyānāgravedine |sarvaratnottamāryāya namo'lānāgradarśine

iyaṃ sā lokanāśasya viprayuktasya tāpinaḥ |pratimā dṛśyate śāntā mokṣamudghāṭyakārikā

samarcatīmāṃ nityaṃ (yaḥ) puruṣaḥ śāntamānasaḥ |sa mucyate bhavabhayānnivṛttiṃ cādhigacchati

etacchāntapadaṃ ramyametannaiṣṭhikamucyate |yadayaṃ bhāṣate dharma nirvāṇapuradeśikaḥ

asya vākyaṃ samālambya puruṣādhīnavikramāḥ |ākarṣanti padaṃ nityaṃ yadanantasukhāvaham

etat pūrva samāruhaya puruṣāstattvacintakāḥ |trilokaughārṇavaṃ ghoraṃ taranti bhavasāgaram

ayaṃ sa cakṣurlokasya samantāddhi vicakṣuṣaḥ |ayaṃ jyotiḥ paraṃ jyotiryajjyotiḥkāṣṭhasambhavam

kalpāntaṃ prāṇināṃ citte nṛṇāṃ rāgādibhirmalaiḥ |jñānatoyena mahatā śodhayatyeva vāṅnṛpaḥ

yanna dṛṣṭaṃ padaṃ sarvaistīrthikairjñānapāṇibhiḥ |tatpadaṃ vimalairvākyaistvayā nṛṇāṃ pradarśitam

pramādaparamo'nātho jano'yaṃ tāritastvayā |tīrṇaḥ pāragato nāthastārayatyaghanāśanāt

hitārtha sarvajagatastvamevaiko vyavasthitaḥ |ahitānāṃ hitāyaiva tvameva puruṣottamaḥ

anādimati saṃsāre nṛṇāṃ kleśāpahārakaiḥ |tvayā viśodhito vākyaistamaḥ sūryodaye yathā

akṣayaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ jñānalokakaro mahān |tvamevaiko jagannātha lokottaraguṇārṇavaḥ

||iti stutivargaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ ||puṇya-deva-sukhairmitra-rāja-stutibhiranvitāḥ |saddharmasmṛtivaipulyai
gṛhīto'yaṃ samuccayaḥ ||||iti caturtham udānam ||ye ca dharmā hetubhavā teṣāṃ tathāgato'vadat
|teṣāṃ ca yo nirodhaścaivaṃvādīmahāśramaṇaḥ ||puṇyamavāptamantrakleśaṃvibhidyājanavalāśrīkam
|yāvajjagadvyākulaṃ tarkaniṣṭhaiḥsamākulaṃ vetti satyavacanaiḥ ||vaipulyamahāgambhīrodadhisūtravarād
bhikṣu(ṇā)avalokitasiṃhenoddhṛtamiti |mohā'śivādirahitasya vākyavidyasya
vipulārjanasya 'dharmasamuccayo' nāmadharmaparyāyaḥ samāptaḥ |||iti śubham ||

## 4 parivartavargaḥ

(4) parivartavargaḥkasya kālaḥ parivartatekāmairevāvitṛṣṇasya tṛṣṇayā tṛṣitasya ca |cañcalendriyacittasya
kālo'yaṃ parivartate

anityadhyeyatā yasya sukhasaktasya dehinaḥ |strīdarśanena mattasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

jātimaraṇavaśyasya mohitasya ca tṛṣṇayā |bālasya tu janasyāsya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

gaticārakabaddhasya udvegavaśagasya ca |pramādaviṣamūḍhasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

auddhatyādiprasaktasya gātraśobhāratasya ca |lābhairatṛptamanasaḥ kālo'yaṃ parivartate

pañcabandhanabaddhasya ṣaḍbhirvyāmohitasya ca |trailokyavidhimūḍhasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

vinipātānabhijñasya vitarkopahatasya ca |janasya saktamanasaḥ kālo'yaṃ parivartate

tatkālaramaṇīyeṣu pariṇāmahiteṣu ca |kāmeṣu saktamanasaḥ kālo'yaṃ parivartate

pūrvaduḥkhānabhijñasya divyasaukhyaratasya ca |viprayogānabhijñasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

karmajālena baddhasya manasyeva viceṣṭinaḥ |satpathāt paribhraṣṭasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

bhavadoṣānabhijñasya tṛṣṇayā mūḍhacetasaḥ |mohāndhakāramagnasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

kāmaikapāśabaddhasya niḥsahāyasya dehinaḥ |devīgaṇavimūḍhasyaṃ kālo'yaṃ parivartate

indriyād vyapakṛṣṭasya satpathabhrāmitasya ca |traidhātukarasajñasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

saṃvarāsaṃvarajñasya vyāpādabahulasya ca |naṣṭasambuddhamārgasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

hitāhitavahirgasya kāryākāryajaḍasya ca |krīḍābāla sadṛśasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

nadīprastravaṇocceṣu vanopavanabhūmiṣu |(saṃ)krīḍātatparasyāsya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

vimānagiripṛṣṭheṣu dharmākaravaneṣu ca |ramataḥ kāmabhogeṣu kālo'yaṃ parivartate

karmadharma vipākeṣu nirāsaktasya dehinaḥ |kevalāhārasaktasya kālo'yaṃ parivartate

śarīrasthitasyā'pi śarīrānabhijñatākarmaṇāyuḥ pariśrāntaṃ traidhātukamidaṃ sadā |bhramanti cakravannityaṃ
na ca vindanti bāliśāḥ

jagataḥ doṣānabhijñatāvinipātocchru yamayaṃ yo nāmārohate jagat |na ca doṣāvadheyatvaṃ prakurvanti
vimohitāḥ

dhīrasya kāmaparivarjanaṃ saukhyahetuḥetat (tu) paramaṃ saukhyaṃ yat kāmaparivarjanam |vītakāṃkṣasya
dhīrasya gataśokasya tāpinaḥ

munīnāṃ caramapadaprāptiphalamtadādimadhyanidhane kalyāṇaṃ kṣemamuttamam |yad prāpyamunayaḥ
śrāntāścaranti vigatajvarāḥ

duḥkhahetavaḥ kāmāḥsatkāmajaṃ bhavet saukhyaṃ vinipātagataṃ tu tat |na hi kāmaviśeṣaṃ ca kiñcid
duḥkhavipākajam

tasmāt kāmeṣu matimān na lubhyet kathañcana |te hi saṃsāraduḥkhānāṃ hetubhūtāḥ sudāruṇāḥ

vanopavanaśaileṣu padmākaravaneṣu ca |vibhrāntāstṛṣṇayā bālāḥ patanti saha daivataiḥ

śubhakarmāsakteḥ upadeśaḥkāñcaneṣu (ca) śaileṣu vaidūryaśikhareṣu ca |kṣīyate hi śubhaṃ karma
yatasva saha daivataiḥ

kalpavṛkṣeṣu ramyeṣu nadīprastravaṇeṣu ca |caratastaiḥ śubhaṃ kṣīṇaṃ yatasva saha daivataiḥ

bhūmibhāgeṣu ramyeṣu ratnākaravaneṣu ca |na śubhaṃ te sadā cīrṇa yatasva saha daivataiḥ

pañcātmakena vīryeṇa manaḥprahalādakāriṇā |hatasya gatakālasya yatasva saha daivataiḥ

devānāṃ hitānabhijñatākāmaṃ saṃsakta manasairnityaṃ viṣayatatparaiḥ |na jñāyate hitaṃ devairyadamantrāhitaṃ
bhavet

devanindāalpaśiṣṭamidaṃ puṇyaṃ cyavanaṃ samupasthitam |gantavyamanyatra suraiḥ sukarmaphalabhojibhiḥ

ye nityaṃ śubhasaṃsaktāḥ kurvanti vā śubhaṃ sadā |teṣāṃ visadṛśo heturmūrkhāṇāṃ hi vivartate

ke mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣantebhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |śubhaśīlatamo'jñānāḥ sarvadā
na parāyaṇāḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |ye na karmavipākasya jñānaṃ roṣavadhe ratāḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |prajñāpayanti na samā stṛṣṇāgniparivāritāḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |viprayogakṛtaṃ duḥkhaṃ ye na paśyanti dāruṇam

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |strījanāsaktahṛdayā na vindanti punarbhavam
|37||bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |vipattivyādhiśokebhyo nodvijanti kathañcana

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |ye pāpamitrasaṃsarga prakurvanti mahārūṣam

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |bhāvitaṃ cetasā yairna na ca tattvapraveśitā

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |yena sarvāsvavasthāsu saṃsārabhayabhīravaḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |kāryākāryeṣu ye nityaṃ na subhāṣitacetasaḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |ye na tattvadhiyo nityaṃ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ

bhuktvā mṛtyuṃ pratīkṣante te janāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |ye śarīra sukhārthena dharma hiṃsanti mohitāḥ

paṇḍitalakṣaṇamekāntamanasā nityaṃ śubhaṃ kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ |aśubhaṃ ca sadā varjyametat paṇḍitalakṣaṇam

vinipātasādhanānikṣayaṃ yātaṃ ca yat śīlaṃ spṛṣṭvā yaḥ samupāgataḥ |pramādāvañcito'vaśyaṃ vinipāto
bhaviṣyati

viṣayiṇaḥ parivartanaśīlatā na tu viṣayāṇāmgatā gacchanti yāsyanti deveśāścaiva sarvataḥ |tiṣṭhanti
śikhare ramye nānāratnavibhūṣite

vaidūryaśikharā ramyāstathānye vanamālinaḥ |viṣayāstādṛśā eva janastu parivartate

vanopavanaramyāṇi bhūmibhāgāni sarvaśaḥ |tiṣṭhantyavikalānyeva janastu parivartate

vaidūryamayanālāni kāñcanāni viśeṣataḥ |padmākarāṇi tānyeva janastu parivartate

sarāṃsi sarito ramyāḥ pakṣisaṅghairnirantaram |tathaivāvikalā hyete janastu parivartate

vimānāni rathāścaiva harmyāṇi ca tathaiva ca |tiṣṭhantyavikalā hyete janastu parivartate

parivartanaṃ lokasya viṣayairvañcitasya ca |tathāpi nāmalokasya nodvego hṛdi jāyate

abhyāsena kharī bhūtaṃ cittaṃ saṃsāracāriṇam |yena manye mahad duḥkhaṃ hṛdaye naiva vartate

śūlikena yathā baddhāḥ paśavo gṛhapañjare |ekaikaśo vinaśyanti śeṣāṇāṃ nāsti sambhramaḥ

sukhāya kāmināmeva iyaṃ bhūmiravasthitā |cyutāścaivopapannāśca tathāmī bāliśāḥ surāḥ

viṣayonmukhānāṃ maraṇānabhijñatvampramādāya hatāḥ sattvā mṛtyuḥ kṣaṇikaduḥkhadaḥ |nāvabuddhayanti
maraṇaṃ paśuvad vītasambhramāḥ

te paścād dīrghamanasaḥ kālasya vaśamāgatāḥ |paścāttāpamayo vahnirdhakṣyate niṣpratikṣayaḥ

dāruṇaṃ niṣpratīkāramavaśyaṃ rūpadehinām |maraṇaṃ kālavaśagaṃ tad viditvā''gamaṃ caret

viprayogāntāḥ sarve saṃyogāḥsamāgamāḥ priyāloke viyogāścāpriyāḥ sadā |saṃyogo viprayogānto dharmateyaṃ
sanātanā

kṣaṇe lave muhūrte ca divārātrau tathā'dhvani |maraṇaṃ cintayed dhīrastasya nāsti pratikriyā

gatakalmaṣā eva śāntimadhigacchantismṛtimagnyāṃ praśaṃsanti yeṣāṃ maraṇasaṃbhavā |śreyasaikaparāṃ
śānti prayāntiṃ gatakalmaṣāḥ

mṛtyorarthamanusmṛtya doṣo'yaṃ kasya sammataḥ |nirdoṣaṃ hi manaḥ sarvaṃ śāntaṃ bhavati nityaśaḥ

tathāgataiḥ apramādaparaṃ śreṣṭhamuktamapramādaparaṃ śreṣṭhamidamuktaṃ tathāgataiḥ |yanmṛtyoḥ
smaraṇaṃ nityamaśubhānāṃ ca varjanam

||iti parivartavargaścaturthaḥ ||

## 5 anityatāvargaḥ

(5) anityatāvargaḥsukhaṃ kṣayāntamkṣayāntaṃ hi sukhaṃ sarva na sukhaṃ vidyate dhruvam |saṃsṛjenna
sukhaṃ tasmāt yadīcchet sukhamātmanaḥ

mṛtyuḥ sarvānapi ākarṣatigacchatāṃ tiṣṭhatāṃ caiva hasatāṃ krīḍatāmapi |avisahyo mahāvego mṛtyurnaśyati
dāruṇaḥ
