na tatstha mihāmutra yatrāsau pratipadyate |tathāpi nāma saṃmūḍhā janayitvā vaśaṃgataḥ

(na bālaṃ na yuvānaṃ vā) na sthavirameva vā |gṛhasthamagṛhasthaṃ vā yadā'sau nāpakarṣati

sukhitaṃ duḥkhitaṃ vāpi guṇavantaṃ tathaiva ca |vratasthamavratasthaṃ vā yadā'sau nāpakarṣati

duḥśīlaṃ śīlavantaṃ ca (dāninaṃ ca tathottamam ) |rājānaṃ cottamaṃ cāpi yadā'sau nāpakarṣati

daivaṃ vā nārakaṃ vāpi tiryañca pratajaṃ tathā |pramattamapramattaṃ vā yadā'sau nāpakarṣati

kāmadhātūpapannaṃ vā rūpadhātau sthitañca yaḥ |ārupyadhātūpapannaṃ vā yadā'sau nāpakarṣati

karmapāśo jarādaṇḍo vyādhidaṇḍo mahāvanam |raktopamo mṛtyurayaṃ sattvānādāya gacchati

evaṃvidhe pratibhaye mṛtyau paramadāruṇe |(upasthite pravartante) surāḥ kāmavimohitāḥ

patanāntaṃ sarvasukhaṃ santyajetyad duḥkhaṃ cyavamānasya devalokāt surasya ca |narake yadbhavaṃ
duḥkhaṃ viprayogapuraḥsaram

viṣamākṣikasaṃyuktaṃ yathānnaṃ cāpi (śobhanam) |(tato) devagataṃ saukhyaṃ patanāntaṃ visarjayet

kṣīṇapuṇyasya dīnasya svadārairvarjitasya ca |yad duḥkhaṃ cyavamānasya tasyaupamyaṃ na vidyate

sukṛtakṣīṇamandasya dvipasyeva gatattviṣaḥ |śāmyati devasya yadduḥkhaṃ mahadduḥkhaṃ prajāyate

devasyāpi cyavanamtṛṣṇāviṣayavṛddhasya śokopahatacetasaḥ |mandavāgdehaceṣṭasya devasya cyavanād
bhayam

duḥkhasya viṣayataratvamyathā yathā sukhāsvādāḥ kāmā paramavañcakāḥ |(tathā) tathā bhavatyeva
teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ mahattaram

yatamānasya yad duḥkhaṃ devalokān prajāyate |tasyāntareṇa narakaṃ kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm

cyavanāntaṃ devalokaṃ maraṇāntaṃ tadā bhuvi |dṛṣṭvā duḥkhaṃ ( bhavatyeva śreyase kriyatāṃ manaḥ)

saukhyaṃ patanāntampatanāntaṃ sadā saukhyaṃ nāsti lokasya nityajāḥ |tad buddhvā kāraṇaṃ sarva
śreyase kriyatāṃ manaḥ

sarva duḥkhāntamsarve kṣayāntā nicayā patanāntā samucchrayāḥ |saṃyogā viprayogāntā maraṇāntaṃ
hi jīvitam

jātasya maraṇaṃ nityaṃ na tasyāstīti nityatā |na bhūtārāvitānena triṣu dhātuṣu dṛśyate

garbhameke vinaśyanti tathaike sūtikāgṛhe |parisarpaṇakāścaike tathaiva ( ca vinaśvarāḥ)

maraṇaṃ dhruvamudayāstaṃ ca gamanaṃ bhāskarasya pradṛśyate |tathā jātasya sarvasya sthitaṃ maraṇamagrataḥ

sarva sukhaṃ kṣayāntamkṣayāntaṃ hi sukhaṃ sarva yo na vindati mohadhīḥ |sa paścānmaraṇe prāpte
(duḥkhāya janibhāgbhavet )

kuśalaṃ kārya- (karaṇaṃ kuśalī ca) priyaḥ sadā |sukhaṃ hi dharmācaraṇaṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ

anityamadhruvaṃ sarva pariṇāme kaṭuḥ sadā |saṃsāraḥ kathito buddherhetustattvavicāraṇe

devāḥ sukhapramattā ye teṣāṃ sukhamaśāśvatam |cyavamānasya ca yadduḥkhaṃ devalokāt surasya hi

tasyopamānaṃ naivāsti narakānnarakaṃ hi tat |kimete nāvabudhyante patanāntaṃ sadā sukham

ajarāmaravallokaṃ paśyanti sukhamohitāḥ |cyutyupapattimallokaḥ saṃkhyā tasya na vidyate |na
codvijanti puruṣāstṛṣṇayā parivañcitāḥ

abhiyāti sadā janma tvaritaṃ yāti yauvanam |saṃyogo viprayogaśca sahajaḥ paridṛśyate |vimūḍhā
nānupaśyanti viṣayaiḥ parivañcitāḥ

tṛṣṇāvivaśā devāḥ cyavanticyavanti devāḥ vivaśāstṛṣṇayā parimohitāḥ |sā bhūmistāni padmāni vanāni
vividhāni ca

sarvapadārthānāmāpātaramaṇīyatvamśikharāgrāṇi ramyāṇi ratnavanti mahānti ca |sarāṃsi nadyo vividhā
ratnapāṣāṇabhūṣitāḥ

vṛkṣā vicitrakusumāḥ paṅktibhirvividhaiḥ sthitāḥ |harmyāgrāṇi ca ramyāṇi ratnavanti mahānti
ca

kalpavṛkṣā hemamayā kecidvaidūrya nirmalāḥ |rājatāstapanīyāśca vimiśrā vararohiṇaḥ

bhramarairūpagītāśca vibhānti kamalākarāḥ |vibhūṣaṇāni ramyāṇi devārhāṇi ca sarvataḥ

sarva kṣaṇikamsarvametat tathaivāsti janastu parivartate |māyābudbudaphenābhaṃ gandharvanagaropamam

sukhaṃ sarvasya devasya tṛṣṇayā vañcitasya hi |sa vināśya janaṃ sarva bhrāmayitvā bhavārṇave

tṛṣṇaiva duḥkhamūlamtṛṣṇā viṣāgnisadṛśā lokānāmatra tiṣṭhati |avitṛptā kāmabhogairapi taptā
sukhodbhavā

kālānalena nirdagdhā kva te devagaṇāḥ gatāḥ |sarvathādhigatā yeyaṃ tṛṣṇā lokavitanvinī

yayā vimohitā devāḥ kālasya vaśamāgatāḥ |devāsurā narā yakṣā narake yānti jaṅgamāḥ

kālapāśayojitaṃ traidhātukamidamgacchanti vivaśāḥ sarve kālapāśaṃ durāsadam |traidhātukamidaṃ
sarva kālapāśena yojitam

tṛṣṇāvimohitāḥ maraṇaṃ na paśyantina ca paśyanti vivaśāstṛṣṇāmohena mohitāḥ |anena hi yathā
devāḥ śataśo'tha sahastraśaḥ

gatāḥ kālāgninirdagdhā viṣayairdhanasaṃbhavaiḥ |parasya dṛśyate maraṇamātmano naiva dṛśyate

paścātte vyasane prāpte vindante duḥkhamātmanaḥ |pramādakaluṣaṃ ceto viṣayābhirataṃ sadā

na vindanti dhruvaṃ mṛtyuṃ dehināṃ sahajasthitam |sukhottaramanāśasya pramādābhiratasya ca

mṛtyusainyamupaitīdaṃ pramāthi viṣasannibham |na mantrauṣadhakarmāṇi na devā nāsurāstathā

kālapāśabaddhasya na ko'pi trātākālapāśena baddhasya trātāro na bhavanti te |rajasā guṇṭhitaṃ
vaktraṃ ātmano naiva vindati

tṛṣṇāyā heyatve hetuḥprāpteścyavanabhūto'yaṃ mṛtyuḥ paścād bhaviṣyati |lolupasya sadā yatnairavitṛptasya
tṛṣṇayā

sahasā'bhyeti maraṇaṃ yannadṛṣṭaṃ sukhārthinām |ayaṃ tavāgraho duḥkhaṃ mṛtyunā preṣito mahān

asmādanantaraṃ mṛtyurbhaviṣyati sudāruṇaḥ |mahāgirivarādasmāt vanopavanabhūṣitāt

baddhānarā viṣayagā svakarmaphalabhoginaḥ |krīḍāvihāriṇastāvat kvacit tṛṣṇāsukhaṃ na hi |tarṣyayā
tṛṣṇayā mūḍhāḥ patanti vivaśā bhuvi

dhūmasyānantaraṃ vahniryathā bhavati nityaśaḥ |tathā cyavanaliṅgasya pṛṣṭhataścyavanaṃ sthitam

jātyā jātyā dhruvaṃ mṛtyuścārogye sati ruk sthitā |sampattau vyasanaprāptiyauvane sahajā jarā
|sarvaiḥ priyairviyogaśca na saṃyogo dhruvaṃ sthitaḥ

dharmatāyāḥ mahattvamdharmateyaṃ sadāloke samyagbuddhena deśitā |ubhayasya kṣayo nāśo janasyo
parivartate

jātau satyāṃ yathā mṛtyuravaśyaṃ sthita eva hi |kṣīyate sukṛtaṃ karma āyūṃṣi kṣaṇikāni hi

saṃvarādikaṃ bhajadhvamsaṃvara dharmavinaye bhajadhvaṃ puruṣottamāḥ |abhyeti yauvanaṃ sarvaṃ
jīvitaṃ cānugacchati

sarvasya nāśaśālitvamnaśyanti sarvadā sarvā mā pramāde manaḥkṛthā |na nityaṃ labhyate svargo
na nityaṃ kṣaṇasampadā

yasya yāvannābhipatanaṃ tāvat kṣipraṃ (pra)yujyatām|(tasya jāte hi patane tat prayogo vṛthā
bhavet )

ayaṃ sa hi drumavano nānāratnavibhūṣitaḥ |latāgahvarasaṃcchannaḥ padmākaravibhūṣitaḥ

jāmbūnadamayaiśśuddhaiḥ prāsādaiḥ ratnacitritaiḥ |nānāvidhaiḥ prastravaṇairlatābhirupaśobhitaḥ

karmasākṣījanasyāsya bhitvā gaganamucchritaḥ |kalpāgnivirato meruḥ sarvathā na bhaviṣyati

kiṃ punarye surāstatra phenabudbudasannibhāḥ |utpadyante vinaśyanti pramādamadamohitāḥ

sukhaduḥkhamanantaṃ ca svecchākāmaphalodbhavam |devalokād yathā devāḥ patanti narake punaḥ

tad duḥkhaṃ paramaṃ kaṭuśarīramānasaiduḥkhairvidyamāneṣu sarvataḥ |na tacchakyaṃ pramatte syāt
tadduḥkhaṃ paramaṃ kaṭu

viyogaduḥkhaṃ duḥkhanāmagrataḥ paripaṭhyate |taccadevagatau nityamagrataḥ sampravartate

sarvapriyairmanāpaiśca viprayogo bhaviṣyati |na ca devāḥ prapaśyadhvaṃ maraṇāntaṃ hi jīvitam

cyutikāle tu samprāpte vihvalendriyacetasā |jānīdhvaṃ tatparaṃ duḥkhaṃ yadavaśyaṃ bhaviṣyati

sarvaṃ kṣaṇikamakālacakrapratimaṃ gandharvanagaropamam |tribhave saṃbhavaṃ sarva phenabudbudasannibham

yaḥ phenarāśau saṃmūḍhaḥ kuryācchādanasaṃstaram |sa mūḍhaḥ saṃskṛte kuryādabhisvādaṃ bhramātmakam

mṛtyumahimāna devā na narā yakṣā nāsurā garuḍāstathā |trāyante mṛtyusamaye karmaṇā paritapyatām

yāvannāyāti samaye mṛtyurājaḥ sudāruṇaḥ |tāvattu sukṛtaṃ kārya mā paścāt paritapyatha

yadbhūtakāmajaṃ saukhyaṃ prākṛtaṃ viṣayodbhavam |mṛtyukāle samutpanne tiṣṭhanti na budharṣamā

kiṃ kariṣyanti saukhyāni kiṃ striyaḥ kiñcabāndhavāḥ |mṛtyunā hriyamāṇasya devalokāt samantataḥ

sthirāstā bhūmayaḥ sarvā vanopavanabhūṣitāḥ |mṛtyurajjvā vibaddhaśca tvantu kālena nīyase

ākāśe jaladā yadvada vāyunā samudīritāḥ |saṃśliṣṭāśca viyujyante saṃsāre prāṇinastathā

ṛtau ṛtau yathā puṣpamāgatairdṛśyate naraiḥ |vyatīte tu ṛtau sarve pratiyānti yathāgatāḥ

yathā kāle samutpanne modante sukṛtaiḥ surāḥ |vyatīte tu śubhe kāle pratiyānti yathāgatāḥ

ṛtau ṛtau yathā vṛkṣe parṇajāyate'nekaśaḥ |vyatīte tu śubhe kāle śīryate tat samantataḥ

yathā parṇopamādetanna saukhyamabhayaṃ bhave |śīryate satataṃ saukhyaṃ nāsaukhyaṃ vidyate dhruvam

prābṛṭkāle yathā varṣamākāśe na nivartate |tathā saukhyamidaṃ sarva gacchati na nivartate

barhiṇāṃ ca yathā nādo vāyunā samudīritaḥ |śrūyate tatpraṇāśāya tathā saukhyamidaṃ smṛtam

yathā hi jvalane kṣiptaṃ dhruvaṃ kālaṃ pradahayate |tathā vahanyupamaḥ kālaḥ kāṣṭhavaccaratāṃ
smṛtaḥ

āgatāśca gatāścaike śataśo'tha sahastraśaḥ |saṃvego na bhavatyeṣā tṛṣṇayā mohitāstathā

yathā yathā mahāsaukhyaṃ svayaṃ bhogo yathā yathā |tathā tathā mahad duḥkhaṃ patanāntodbhavaṃ
dhruvam

jātyādi duḥkhaparamparājātirduḥkhā jarā duḥkhā maraṇaṃ duḥkhameva ca |priyaiśca saha viśleṣo
duḥkhasyaiṣā paramparā

mṛtyunā na bibhetekasya patanaṃ dṛṣṭvā kathaṃ nodvijate janaḥ |mamāpi patanaṃ hyetat sarvapāpairbhaviṣyati

yaḥ paśyati bhayaṃ dūrāt pratipattau ca vartate |(sa)mprāpte mṛtyusamaye na vibheti gatavyathaḥ

vibhujyamāno daunaiśca mṛtyuḥ svajanabāndhavaiḥ |yadduḥkhaṃ mṛtyusamaye tanna śakyaṃ prabhāṣitum

patanāntaṃ hi maraṇaṃ patamāno yathā giriḥ |niṣpratyanīkaṃ balavat puruṣānapakarṣati |saṃśliṣyate
mahāvegaḥ samprahārī ca sarvadā

taṃ jñātvā mā pramādeṣu stubadhvaṃ devasattamāḥ |saṃśleṣayati viśleṣaṃ yauvane ca sadā jarā

jīvite ca sadā mṛtyuḥ padametat sadā sthitam |anityenā'gninā'vaśyaṃ dagdhabyo yatra gocaraḥ

kalpānte kiṃ punardevā ye phenakadalīsamāḥ |na tadasti bhavet kiñcid yad jñānaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ calam

yannityaṃ (ca) sukhaṃ vā syādetatsthānaṃ na vidyate |upasthitamiva jñeyaṃ maraṇaṃ sarvajantubhiḥ

yasmāt tasya pratīkāraḥ sarvopāyairna śakyate |rūpaṃ kṣaṇikameteṣāṃ vayo'pi kṣaṇikaṃ tathā

kṣīyate ca tathā saukhyaṃ na ca buddhayantyamedhasaḥ |yadā tat sarvameteṣāmanekaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam

janmataḥ kālacakreṇa paripāṭayā vināśitam |tathaivaitat purā naṣṭamanekaśatalakṣaṇam

tathaitadapi janmaiṣāṃ mṛtyurājo hariṣyati |pramādamanasāṃ hyeṣāṃ nityaṃ sukhavihāriṇām

nidhanāya kālapāśo'yamabhyeti sukhanāśakaḥ |sukhārogyabalaprāṇapriyaviśleṣakārakaḥ

balavanmṛtyurājo'sau samīpamupasarpati |yaḥ samīpagato hyeṣāṃ pramattānāmanekaśaḥ

sukhanāśaḥ suniścitaḥjīvitaṃ saha saukhyena tvaritaṃ nāśayiṣyati |dīrghakālamidaṃ dhvastaṃ pramādāpahatāḥ
surāḥ

nāvabuddhayanti mohāndhāḥ saukhyena pratibodhitāḥ |yadā hīnaprabhāhyete vihvalendriyacetasaḥ

yamalokaṃ patiṣyanti tadā jñānasya tatphalam |jīyate bata kālo'yaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ ca yathā sukham

tathāpi raktamanaso nāvabuddhayantyacakṣuṣaḥ |ramyād ramyataraṃ yānti nityaṃ sukhavihāriṇaḥ

prayāsyanti yathā hayete mṛtyurājo hariṣyati |jarā jātivipattīnāṃ yo na mūḍho viṣīdati

sa paścāt mṛtyusamaye tapyate (hīnacetasā) |sukhaṃ deveṣu samprāpya (tadetad) guṇasammatam

kṛtahutaṃ vināśāntaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ jīvitaṃ calam |yathā hi yad vināśāntaṃ tathaiṣāmapi jīvitam

acireṇāpi kālena bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |yāvannāyāti maraṇaṃ yāvad buddhiranāvilā

manasā dharmaratiḥ kāryātāvaddharmarataṃ dhārya manobalena dhīmatā |sarvajīvitanāśāya sarvasaukhyakṣayāya
ca

priyaviyogaḥ niścitaḥsarvapriyaviyogāya mṛtyurāśrayameti ca |tasminnāpte mahādhyāne mṛtyuḥ paramadāruṇaḥ

nānyat trāṇamṛte dharmāt tasmād dharmarato bhavet |upapannasya devasya yā prītirupapadyate

nāsau cyavanaduḥkhasya kalāmarhati ṣoḍaśīm |mṛtyukāle samutpanne na kaścit kasyacit tathā

sarvasya nāśatvamna ca gacchantimapyekaṃ padamekaṃ hi gacchati |yadā jātaṃ tadāramya naro mṛtyuparāyaṇaḥ

nānāmohaparītastu bhayametanna vidyate |yadā te cyavanaṃ prāpte bhavabhogakaraṃ param

tadā vindati mūḍhātmā yad bhayaṃ mṛtyujaṃ mahat |viṣayoragasandaṣṭā tṛṣṇā viṣavimohitā

na vindanti sadā devāścyavanānte mahad bhayam |cyavanti ca tathā duḥkhamutpādayanti dehinām

yathopapattiścyavanaṃ devānāṃ samprajāyate |karmavāyusamudbhrāntaṃ prayogeṇa ca duḥkhitam

puruṣaṃ nayate mṛtyuravaśaṃ (sukhavañcitam ) |na mātaro na pitaro na mitrāṇi na bāndhavāḥ

sahāyatāṃ vrajantīyaṃ puruṣaṃ kālacoditam |asahāyo janaścāyaṃ janaḥ prakṛtivañcitaḥ

mana eva pāśabandhanamkevalaṃ tu manaḥpāśairbandhanairbadhyate janaḥ |na janaḥ svajanaśceha
trātā bhavati kasyacita

gṛhīta iva keśeṣu mṛtyunā dharmamācaretmṛtyukāle samutpanne svajano'pi yathā janaḥ |yastvaṃ
paśyasi devānāṃ sukhametanmahodayam

tat prasahyamahādevo mṛtyurājo hariṣyati |tvaritaṃ yāti janmedaṃ na ca vindati bāliśaḥ

paścāttu vyasane prāpte prativindanti duḥkhinaḥ |yadi duḥkhādbhayaṃ tvaritaṃ yadi mṛtyubhayaśca
yaḥ

dharme hi kriyatāṃ buddhirevaṃ sukhamavāpsyatha |vināśāntaṃ sadā saukhyamanityaṃ sarvataścalam

devatvaṃ patanāntamsarvadevakṛtaṃ saukhyaṃ na ca vindanti mohitāḥ |abhūtvā ca cyavantyete bhūtvā'vaśyaṃ
hi cañcalāḥ

devāḥ patanabhāvāya dharmateyaṃ vyavasthitā |pramattāḥ kāmabhogeṣu ye devāścalamānasāḥ

na paśyanti surāstīvraṃ vyasanāntaṃ hi jīvitam |yadaiva jāyate devastadaiva maraṇāya saḥ

śreyase matiḥ karttavyādivasānte yathā rātriravaśyaṃ pratipadyate |divasaṃ jīvitaṃ proktaṃ rātriḥ
sthānmaraṇopamā

tasmāt tadubhayaṃ jñātvā śreyase kriyatāṃ manaḥ |tadetadartha nṛṇāṃ hi strīrāgavaśavartinām

tadiṣṭaṃ mṛtyusainyena prasahayamavamarditam |bhāvābhāvādibhirmūḍhān vañcayitvā striyo narān

prayānti mṛtyusamaye svakarmaphalabhoginaḥ |padmopavanaśaileṣu nadīnāṃ nirjhareṣu ca

bahuśaḥ krīḍanaṃ kṛtvā kūpāyā'bhigatāḥ priyāḥ |avaśyambhāvi maraṇamavaśyaṃ ca viyogitā

tathāpi nāma puruṣā nityaṃ rāgavaśānugāḥ |sukhitaṃ duḥkhitaṃ vā(pi) jīrṇamanyatra yauvanam

mṛtyurājo'vamardatiduṣkulīnaṃ kulīnaṃ vā mṛtyurājo'vamardati |surūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā balavantaṃ
tathā'balam

sanāthaṃ naṣṭanāthaṃ vā mṛtyurājo'vamardati |rājānaṃ vā tathā mṛtyuḥ gṛhiṇaṃ vā tathā yatim

krūraṃ vā mṛdukaṃ vāpi mṛtyurājo'vamardati |nirdhanaṃ vā daridraṃ vā saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ tathā

striyaṃ vā puruṣaṃ vāpi mṛtyurājo'vamardati |pravāsinaṃ gṛhasthaṃ vā jale vāpi sthalesthitam

sthitaṃ vā giriśṛṅgeṣu mṛtyurājo'vamardati |jāgarantaṃ tathā suptaṃ bhuñjānaṃ vā tathā sthitam

pramāthī sarvalokasya mṛtyurājo'vamardati |bhūmisthaṃ (vā) vimānasthaṃ videśasthaṃ tathā gṛhe

krakacaḥ kālacakro'yaṃ mṛtyurājo'vamardati |bhāgyavantaṃ tathā dhanya nistriśaṃ (caiva) dharmikam

rogiṇaṃ svasthadehaṃ vā mṛtyurājo'vamardati |caṇḍaṃ suśīlinaṃ cāpi kadarya dhaninaṃ tathā

pramattamapramattaṃ vā mṛtyurājo'vamardati |nārakeyaṃ tathā pretaṃ tiryañco manujaṃ tathā

anivṛttabalotsāho mṛtyurājo'vamardati |kāmadhātau ca ye devā rūpadhātau ca ye sthitāḥ

tān sarvān hi pratibalān mṛtyurājo'vamardati |ārūpyeṣu ca ye devāḥ samāpattiniveśakāḥ |tān sarvānapi
vai devān mṛtyurājo'vamardati

sarva vināśāntamyad jātaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ kiñcid tad vināśāntameva hi |tannāsti saṃskṛtaṃ kiñcid yasya
nāśo ca vidyate |mṛtyorbalamidaṃ jñātvā kāmadoṣaṃ ca sarvataḥ

tṛṣṇayā vañcanaṃ cāpi viramadhvaṃ bhavārṇavāt |tadaitad vyasanaṃ matvā mṛtyorapi calācalam

sudāntaṃ kriyatāṃ cittamsudāntaṃ kriyatāṃ cittaṃ yenedaṃ bhrāmyate jagat |vanopavanaśailebhyo
vimānebhyaśca sarvataḥ

sarvadevāḥ paciṣyanti kālāgnivinipātitāḥ |pramādamanaso mūḍhā bhūyo viṣayajihimatāḥ

tṛṣṇāpāśena sambaddhā devāḥ yāsyanti durgatim |rabhasaṃ jīvitaṃ saukhyaṃ prayāti khalu dehinām

na ca vindanti vibudhā jātyandhā iva satpatham |jalabudbudasaṃkāśaṃ jīvitaṃ sarvadehinām

nadītaraṅgacapalaṃ yauvanaṃ vyativartate |anityā (hi)kaṭākṣekṣāpraduḥkhāstuṣitāḥ surāḥ

rāgasya heyatvamnirvāyatyavaśo dīno dīpaḥ snehakṣayādiva |karmakṣayaparikṣipto dvādaśāro mahābalaḥ

dharmacakro bhavatyeṣa nipātī syānna cañcalaḥ |vicitraviṣayāramyā anekasukhamaṇḍitāḥ

kālagrāsāḥ sarve bhavantituṣitāḥ patanti vivaśāḥ kālasya vaśamāgatāḥ |kālasya vaśamāpannā yathā
rohanti pādapāḥ

te punaḥ kālamāsādya bhavanti vigatatviṣaḥ |yathā kālaṃ samāpadya bhavanti sukhitāḥ surāḥ

punastamaiva saṃsṛtya patanti vivaśā hi te |karmakālaṃ samāsādya loko'yaṃ parivartate

sukhaṃ vrajati duḥkhaṃ hi kālasya vaśamāgataḥ |yadvṛttaṃ prākśubhaṃ karma tacca duṣṭamahodayam

tadidaṃ bhuñjate svargaṃ kṣīyate ca tataḥ punaḥ |sahetukasya sarvasya kṣaṇikasya viśeṣataḥ

anityatāparāmarśo nāsau bhavati sarvataḥ |ye bhāvāḥ saṃskṛtā nityāḥ sarve te vipralobhinaḥ

vipralobhya janaṃ yāti dharmateyaṃ vyavasthitā |śīghrasrotā yathā nadyastathā saukhyaṃ śarīriṇām

kṣaṇikaṃ jīvitaṃ sarva na ca vindanti bāliśāḥ |jarā vyādhiśca mṛtyuśca vipattiḥ karmasaṃkṣaye

bhavantyetāni devānāṃ nityānāṃ kāmacāriṇām |nāyurdhruvaṃ bhavatyeva na saukhyaṃ triṣu dhātuṣu

na (ca) vindanti vivaśā devāḥ kāmena mohitāḥ |varṣadhārā yathā''kāśe duḥsthā bhavati sarvataḥ

tathā saukhyamidaṃ sarva varṣadhāropamaṃ sadā |vāyunā pāṃsavo yānti anyonya parighaṭṭitāḥ

bhrāmyate gagane 'vaśyastathā śleṣaḥ śarīriṇām |suciramapi saṃramyaṃ nityatā nāsti dehinām

acintyārthe saukhyamidaṃ na ca vindanti bāliśāḥ |vāteritaṃ tu yat saukhyaṃ vināśāntaṃ bhaviṣyati

sukhasya duḥkhamiśritatvamvināśaṃ naiva budhyanti kāminaścittavañcitāḥ |duḥkhamiśraṃ sukhamidaṃ
pracchannamiva vidyate

padmamālāparicchinno viṣapūrṇo yathā ghaṭaḥ |odanaṃ viṣasammiśraṃ maraṇāntaṃ (hi) tat tathā

tathā saukhyamidaṃ sarva tasmāt tat parivarjayet |ādau madhye tathā cānte narakāya bhaviṣyati

utpādasthitibhaṅgāni sarvāṇi vastūniutpādasthitabhṅgāntaṃ sarva saṃskṛtamucyate |sarva ca saṃskṛtaṃ
saukhyaṃ nanu bhaṭṭārameva tat

bhaṭṭārañca vilāsaśca (su)vyayakṣaṇikaṃ tathā |sukhaṃ ca jīvitaṃ sarva tasmāt tat parivarjayet

ādau madhye tathā cānte śreyasyeva manaḥ sadā |sudāntaḥ śuddhacitasya mṛtyukāle na sīdati

maraṇasyāvaśyambhāvitvamavaśyambhāvi maraṇaṃ priyasya ca viyogatā |na ca cintayataḥ kāle viṣaye
vipralobhinaḥ

jarāmaraṇacakraṃ tamaviṣahyaṃ sudāruṇam |pādapān devagān sattvān na ca buddhayantyacakṣuṣaḥ

sarvasya jātasya cyavanaṃ bhavaticyavamānasya devasya vikalendriyacetasaḥ |yad duḥkhaṃ sukaraṃ
tasya naupamyamiha vidyate

yathā yathā mahat saukhyaṃ tṛṣṇopahatacetasaḥ |tathā tathā mahad duḥkhaṃ cyavamānasya duḥsaham

kṛtvā hi saṃskṛtaṃ karma na jñātvā karmaṇaḥ sthitiḥ |mṛtyukāle samutpanne paścāttāpena dahayate

mṛtyoḥ pūrvameva svahitamādheyamyāvannāpyeti maraṇaṃ yāvacca kṣaṇasampadaḥ |tāvat svahitamādheyameṣa
mārgaḥ sukhāvahaḥ

cyavanaṃ devalokeṣu maraṇaṃ narabhūmiṣu |viditvā kaḥ pumān āsthāṃ kuryātsopadrave bhave

sarva vināśitailavartipraṇāśena dīpanāśo yathā dhruvam |karmakriyā tathā kasya vinipātaḥ surālayāt
|bhittināśo yathā'vaśyaṃ cittanāśo dhruvaṃsthitaḥ

karmanāśāttathā saukhyamavaśyaṃ vinipadyate |kṣīṇapuṇyāḥ nipatyante devāḥ sarve surālayāt

anityataiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ sattvānāṃ vinipātikā |mṛtyuḥ pivati bhūtāni jarā pivati yauvanam

vyādhiḥ pivati cārogyaṃ na ca loko'vabudhyate |upapannāḥ subahuśaḥ pratītāścāpyanekaśaḥ

jāyate jīryate cāyaṃ cyavane copapadyate |na kṣaṇo'sti muhūrto vā parivartastathāpi vā

yatra mṛtyurvilambena na ca loko'vabudhyate |upapannāstu bahuśaḥ pratītāścāpyanekaśaḥ

yanna mṛtyuvilambaḥ syānna ca loko'vabudhyate |anityaṃ jīvitaṃ sarva na vilambi ca yauvanam

sarvabhūtānyanityāni na ca loko'vabudhyate |ante krameṇa naśyanti bhāvāḥ kṣaṇavilamvinaḥ

sambaddhāḥ karmasūtreṇa na ca loko'vabudhyate |devakoṭisahastrāṇi vanopavanasevinām

nimīlitāni kālena na ca loko'vabudhyate |paṅkābhāvacarā devāḥ pramattā bhogatṛṣṇayā

yāsyanti vilayaṃ sarve na ca loko'vabudhyate |phenabudbudasaṅkāśaṃ svapnadravyopamaṃ sukham

kṣayaṃ prayāti śīghraṃ ca na ca loko'vabudhyate |sarvasya sarvathā sarvo vināśo niyato bhave

na ca bālasya saṃsārānnirvedamupapadyate |na sarvaḥ sarvathā sarva sarvopāyaiḥ prayatnaśaḥ |saṃtrātā
bhavane prāpte mṛtyukāle sudāruṇe

mṛtyurityabhidhīyatevyucchettā sarvasaukhyānāṃ duḥkhanāmākaro mahān |viśleṣaḥ sarvabandhānāṃ
mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

bhayopanetā bhūtānāṃ duḥkhānāmudadhiḥ samaḥ |vyāmohakarttā buddhīnāṃ mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

sañchettā jīvitānāmindriyāṇāṃ ca nāśakaḥ |amṛśyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

adhṛṣyaḥ sarvabhūtānāmajeyaḥ sarvakarmaṇām |vināśaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

avaśyambhāvi tattvānāmantakaṃ sarvadehinām |nikāyabhāganāśo'yaṃ mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

sadeva yakṣagandharva-piśācoraga-rakṣasām |kālacakravināśī (ca) mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

asaṃyamī pramāthī ca bahnivat sarvadehinām |krūraścāvinivarttyaśca mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

skandhāyatananāśaśca ārupyasyātha cetasaḥ |kāladharmo mahāveśo mṛtyurityabhidhīyate

śreyase matiḥ karaṇīyā na tu pramādesa eva dhā(ta)ve śīghraṃ vinipātāya dehinām |śreyase kriyatāṃ
yatno mā pramādeḥ manaḥ kṛthāḥ

gataṃ jīvanaṃ nāyātiyathā (hi śīghra) gamanaṃ pakṣiṇāṃ pavanasya vā |tathā śīghrataraṃ yāti
jīvitaṃ sarvadehinām

gato nivartate vāyurnivartante ca pakṣiṇaḥ |jīvitaṃ yanniruddhaṃ tu tasya nāgamanaṃ punaḥ

drutaṃ kṣīyanti karmāṇi drutamabhyeti cāntakaḥ |avaśyambhāvi viśleṣaṃ na ca vindanti devatāḥ

anirvāyo mahāvegaścatuḥsatyanivarhaṇaḥ |avaśyambhāvi balavāneṣa mṛtyurupaiti ca

śruti pramādino devāḥ sukhena parivañcitāḥ |na vindanti mahad duḥkhaṃ yadavaśyaṃ bhaviṣyati

saṃskṛtasya hi sarvasya samayasya viśeṣataḥ |vināśo bhavati (cā)sau dharmateyaṃ bhave bhave

jarāyauvananāśāya prāṇanāśāya cāntakaḥ |vipattibhūtanāśāya sthitā (vai) nāśahetavaḥ

evaṃvidhe mahāghore vyasane (vai) mahad bhaye |pramādino hi yad devāḥ nūnamete ca cetanāḥ

anāgatabhayaṃ yo hi paśyati jñānacakṣuṣā |sa paṇḍita iti jñeyo viparītastu bāliśaḥ

karmakṣaye sukhasya nāśaḥvimūḍhamanasaḥ sarve vañcitāḥ svena cetasā |karmakṣaye tu naśyanti
dīpā snehavaśādiva

anekaviṣayāṇāṃ ca idaṃ saukhyamanuttamam |bhuktvā karmakṣayo bhūta vinipāto bhaviṣyati

anityatvānavagamādadharmaratiḥjalataraṅgacapalaṃ jīvitaṃ yāti dehinām |na ca budbudaniḥsāramavagacchanti
bāliśāḥ

phenarāśiściraṃ tiṣṭhed upāyaiḥ katibhirnanu |na tu devāściraṃ tiṣṭhantyanityo bhāvanamiti

lobhavaśādeva mṛtyorupekṣākṛṣyate bhūriyaṃ sarvā manujaiḥ phalakāṃkṣibhiḥ |na ca saṃdṛśyate
mṛtyurlābhasaktairapaṇḍitaiḥ

kṣaṇe kṣaṇe vivardhante lobhāśā mohite jane |na ca vindanti saṃsārāḥ kṣīyamāṇāḥ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe

daṇḍatrayamupasaṃharatijarādaṇḍo'yamabhyeti yauvanāntakaro nṛṇām |ārogyaśaktināśāya vyādhidaṇḍo'yamāgataḥ
|daṇḍatrayamidaṃ ghoraṃ sasurā'suranāśakam

abhyeti na ca mūḍho'yaṃ lokaṃ paśyati bāliśaḥ |parasparaṃ pralobhācca svajanaṃ snehacañcalam

mohabaddhāḥ mṛtyuṃ viyogaṃ ca nāvagacchantigacchatyanyonyasaṃśliṣṭo mohabaddhe jane janaḥ |putrapautraprapautrāṇāṃ
vaśagāścāpi ye narāḥ

karmaskandhasya duḥkhāvahatvamsarve tena bhaviṣyanti tvaṃ tu saṅgena bādhyase |jīvitāntakaraḥ
śatrurna viśaṅko mahābalaḥ

so'vilambī mahāvego mṛtyureṣo'bhyupaiti ca |calācalavidhirjñeyaḥ karmaskandhairnidarśitaḥ

devādīnāṃ kṣayo bhavatidevanāgāḥ (sa)gandharvā piśācoragarākṣasāḥ |na śaktāścyavane kṣāntaṃ
balaṃ tasya tathāgatam

tattvadarśako durgatiṃ nādhigacchaticalācalavidhirjñeyaḥ karmaṇā tattvadarśakaḥ |aliptaḥ pāpakairdharmairna
sa gacchati durgatim

iti anityatāvargaḥ pañcamaḥ

## 6 apramādavargaḥ

(6) apramādavargaḥpramādaratasya na mokṣaḥyaḥ pramādarato janturnāsau mokṣāya kalpate |pramādaviṣamūḍhasya
nirvāṇaṃ dūrameva tat

na pramāde manaḥ kāryamna pramādeṣu badhyet pramādaḥ śatruruttamaḥ |pramādaparamā devāḥ patanti
narake punaḥ

svabhāvatāmimāṃ jñātvā dharmāṇāmudayavyayam |na pramāde manaḥ kārya pramādaviṣamuttamam

pramādāpramādayoḥ parasparavirodhitvamapramādo'mṛtapadaṃ pramādo mṛtyunaḥ padam |apramattā na
bhriyante pramattāstu sadātmṛtāḥ

yathā viṣaṃ tathā vahniḥ pramādaḥ prāṇināṃ tathā |pramādonmāditāḥ satvāḥ duḥkhād duḥkhaṃ prayānti
te

apramattāḥ sadāmartyāḥ sarva prakramasaṃskṛtāḥ |prayānti paramāṃ śānti pramādo yatra nāsti hi

yaḥ pramāde rato janturna śubhānyanuceṣṭate |tasya dharmavimūḍhasya kutaḥ svargo bhaviṣyati?

pramādaṃ varjayed yasmāt pramādaṃ viṣamuttamam |apramattā na bhriyante pramattāstu sadātmṛtāḥ

saubhāgyaṃ prāpya yo martyaḥ pramādeṣu ca vartate |na paṇḍita itikhyāto viparītastu nānyathā

na pramādasya kālo hi harṣasya na kathañcana |vyāmohajananācceto mṛtyukāle mahābale

pramādamukhena pramādavarṇanamharṣo dhūmaḥ pramādo'gnirdevān dahati naikaśaḥ |viṣayairmohitā
mūḍhā na ca vindantyacakṣuṣaḥ

avyucchinnāni paśyanti saṃskārāṇi ca dehinām |yathā hi viṣayairmūḍhā nāvagacchanti yaddviṣaḥ

tasmāt pramādaṃ viṣavat kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |sukhaṃ na dṛśyate pūrva pramādomandabuddhibhiḥ

(yathā) pramādopakṛtāḥ prayānti narakaṃ punaḥ |phale prāpte pramādo'yaṃ paścāt santapyate vyathā

pramādo'narthakaraḥsarvānarthakaro dṛṣṭaḥ pramādaḥ paṇḍitaiḥ naraiḥ |tathā ca kuśalaṃ prāyo
yaḥ pramādaṃ na sevate

duḥkhaṃ yasya bhavediṣṭaṃ sa pramādaṃ niṣevatām |na pramādaparaḥ kaścit kadācit sukhamāpnuyāt

pramādaśūnyānāmacyutapadaprāptiḥpramādaviratāḥ santo gacchanti padamacyutam |nā pramādaparaḥ
kaścinnarake duḥkhitānnayet

yadi devāḥ sahantīmaṃ ramante mandamedhasaḥ |tiraścāṃ hi surāṇāṃ ca viśeṣo naiva vidyate

pramādavaśāt devalokād patantipratyekaṃ karmavaicitryaṃ pramādaparamāḥ surāḥ |nāvagacchanti
patanaṃ devalokād bhaviṣyati

sannikṛṣṭaṃ sadā duḥkhaṃ patanaṃ vā bhaviṣyati |devalokottarā devā yathā syāt sukhamātmanaḥ

devalokaṃ samāsādya yaḥ pramādeṣu rakṣate |sa kṣīṇaśubhakarmāntaṃ cyavanānte vibudhyate

mā pramādaparobhūyāḥ pramādo nocitaḥ suraiḥ |pramādairdoṣavihatāścyavanti tridaśālayāt

pramādavaśagaḥ puruṣaḥ nāśametidahyate puruṣaḥ sarvaḥ pramādena vimohitaḥ |sa paścād vigate
tasmin pratyādeśena rakṣyate

doṣodbhavāmimāṃ bhūmiṃ pramādāvṛttaśādvalam |vicaranti sadā mūḍhāḥ surāḥ satkṛtamohitāḥ

cañcalā viṣamāstīvrā pramādāḥ kāmahetavaḥ |na teṣāṃ viśvased vīro yasmāt svapnopamā hi te

kāmaḥ nārakasya heturitina svapno narake hetuḥ kāmāḥ svapnasya hetukāḥ |tasmāt kāmamimaṃ muktvā
nityaṃ sucaritaṃ caret

apramādātsarva prāptuṃ śaknotiyatrāvāptaṃ padaṃ kṛtsnaṃ suraiḥ kāmavimohitaiḥ |tat prāpyate
padaṃ vīrairapramādaparairnaraiḥ

pramādamūlasaṃsāraḥpramādamūlasaṃsāro devānāmālayastathā |ye pramādaviṣairmattāste magnā bhavasaṅkaṭe

tamaḥ pramādamūlañca avidyā hayapakārikā |andhakāreṇa ye mūḍhāsteṣāṃ cakṣurna vidyate

pramādaḥ mohātmakaḥtejasā hi tathā mūlairagninā na ca kathyate |mohānāṃ pravarastadvat pramādaḥ
parikīrtyate

pramādānalataptena manasā tad viceṣṭate |muhayante yena te bālāḥ prayānti narakaṃ punaḥ

pramādonmāditaḥ sukhamevānubhavatipramādonmāditā devāstrividhā ye calātmakāḥ |viyogaduḥkhaṃ
vismṛtya saṃyoge sukhakāṃkṣiṇaḥ

patanāntameva jīvanamupasthite mahāduḥkhe patanānte hi jīvite |paścād vahanti virasaṃ patanāntaṃ
sukhaṃ calam

saṃyogo viprayogāntaḥ patanāntaḥ sukhaṃ sadā |jarāntaṃ yauvanaṃ sarva karmāntaḥ sarvadehinām

saṃsāraḥ karmanāṭakasambaddhaḥśubhāśubhena baddhā hi karmaṇā sarvadehinaḥ |naṭavannaṭayantyete
gatyāṃ gatyāṃ pṛthak pṛthak

karmanāṭakasambaddhaḥ saṃsāro bhramate sadā |na tatra viśvaseddhīmānanityā karmaṇāṃ gatiḥ

pramādaḥ paramodoṣaḥsarvapāpāni viṣavat pramādaḥ paribarjyatām |pramādena tu ye muktā (ste)
tīrṇāsti bhavārṇavāt

prapātapatito doṣī kadācidapi jīvati |na pramādaprapatitaḥ kadāpi sukhavān bhavet

pramādaḥ paramo doṣaḥ kadācit sarvakarmasu |na rātrau na divā tasya śubhaṃ bhavati karhicit

yat sukhaṃ laukikaṃ kiñcid yacca lokottaraṃ matam |naśyate tat pramādena tasmāt tat parivarjayet

apramādo'mṛtapadamapramādo'mṛtapadaṃ pramādo mṛtyunaḥ padam |apramādena te devāḥ devānāṃ śreṣṭhatāṃ
gatāḥ

pramādamūlaḥ saṃsāraḥ apramādaśca (sad) gatiḥ |tasmād pramādavirataḥ sukhī bhavati sarvadā

duḥkhād vibheti cet pramādaṃ parityajeticchate yatsukhaṃ nityaṃ yaśca duḥkhād vibheti (vai)
|sa (hi) pramādād viramet pramādo viṣavannṛṇām

pramādanidrāvihatā pramādaviṣamohitāḥ |prapātaṃ hi prapadyante janāḥ śukla kṛtātmakāḥ

apramādaḥ paraṃ śreyaḥapramādaḥ paraṃ śreyo na pramādaḥ kathañcanaḥ |apramādena sukhinaḥ pramādena
(su)duḥkhitāḥ

pramādamūḍhāḥ narakaṃ vrajantipramādamūḍhā hi narā bhūyo madyena mohitā |dhik pramādavimūḍhāste
caranti narakaṃ punaḥ

pramādavāgurābaddhā viṣayārṇavasaṃsthitāḥ |tṛṣṇāviṣayasambaddho devaḥ krīḍatyanekaśaḥ

jātamātrasya devasya tat kṣaṇaṃ kṣīyate sukham |na vindanti vivaśāḥ pramādāvṛttacetasaḥ

pramādavaśaḥ kāmavaśago'tṛpta eva tiṣṭhatipramādod bhrāntamanaso nityaṃ viṣayatatparāḥ |atṛptāḥ
kāmabhogeṣu sukhena sukhitā hi ca

kāmānalena sandagdhāḥ vipramādena mohitāḥ |na vindanti balaṃ sarva vināśāntaṃ hi jīvitam

anekāni sahastrāṇi surāṇāṃ niyutāni ca |kāmānalena sandagdhāḥ pramādena vimohitāḥ

pramādayutāḥ patantihriyate puruṣaḥ sarvaḥ pramādena vimohitaḥ |sa paścād vyasane prāpte budhyate
tasya tatphalam

viṣavat prāṇahṛd dṛṣṭaḥ pramādastattvadarśibhiḥ |agnivad dahyate nityaṃ śastravacca nikṛntatiḥ

mitravad dṛśyate pūrva paścād bhavati śatruvat |vahiśaḥ sarvadevānāṃ pramādaḥ sampravartate

devāsuramanuṣyāṇāṃ nāgānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |pramādaḥ kāraṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sarvānnarthān karoti saḥ

yaḥ pramādahato nityaṃ nāsau kalyāṇamarhati |kalyāṇavarjitaḥ puruṣo narakānupakarṣati

vividhā kṛtayastiryag pramādaparivañcitāḥ |maithune bhojane caiva yeṣāṃ buddhiḥ sadāratāḥ

yadā sucaritaṃ karma cāntībhūtaṃ divaukasām |bhaviṣyati tadābhūyaḥ kva yāsyanti pramādinaḥ

pramādavāriṇāṃ duḥṣṭāḥ karmavāyubhirāhatāḥ |patanti vṛkṣavad devā gatyāṃ gatyāṃ pṛthak pṛthak

surāṇāmapi saṃvegajanitaḥ śoko jāyateśataśo na hutaścaiva divi jātā divaukasaḥ |na ca saṃvegajanito
hṛdi śokaḥ prajāyate

pramādakaluṣaṃ pītvā mohātpānaṃ divaukasaḥ |patanti narakaṃ tīvraṃ vahninā parivāritam

sudurlabhasya mānuṣasya janmanaḥ sāphalya-vaiphalyesulabdhaṃ mānuṣaṃ janma ye labdhvā apramādinaḥ
|kurvanti sukṛtaṃ karma devalokopagāminaḥ

durlabhaṃ mānuṣaṃ karma labdhvāpi ye pramādinaḥ |te pramādāt paribhrāntāḥ patanti narake narāḥ

mṛtyusamaye pramādī tapatyevapramādaratasattvo yaḥ kṛpayā parivartate |sa paścānmṛtyusamaye
tapyate svenacetasā

pramādasya dāhakatvamna tathā dahyate vahnirna ca śastraṃ vikṛntati |pañcendriyasamudbhūtaṃ
pramādo dahyate tathā

sukharūpaṃ tathā duḥkhaṃ mitrarūpaṃ tathā ripuḥ |pramādināṃ hi tannityaṃ tasmāttaṃ parivarjayet

pramādatṛṣṇayā miśro rāgastadanuvardhakaḥ |trayaste ripavaḥ kruṣṭāḥ nāśayanti sukhaṃ nṛṇām

ye pramādaratā nityaṃ na ca dharmaratā budhāḥ |te mṛtyusamaye prāpte yamadūtaiḥ parākṛtāḥ

viṣavatpramādaṃ matvā tatparityāginaḥdhanyāpramādaṃ viṣavad ye tu parirakṣanti paṇḍitāḥ |te
mṛtyusamaye prāpte bhavanti sukhabhāginaḥ

pramādaḥ paramo mṛtyurapramādaḥ paraṃ sukham |tasmāt sukhārthināḥ nityaṃ mā pramāde matiṃ kṛthāḥ

dhātvāyatana sammohaḥ śreyasāṃ vidhnadhātvāyatanasammohaḥ śreyasāṃ vighnakārakaḥ |sanyāsaḥ sarvakarmāṇāṃ
pramādaḥ sampravartate

doṣayantravilagnāya māyādvārasya dūtakaḥ |sammohasyāgraṇī pāpaḥ pramādaḥ sampravartate

pramādaratāḥ mṛtyumukhaṃ prati gacchantiye pramādaratā bālāste mṛtyorhastamāsthitāḥ |pramādasevakā
ye tu te sarve nidhanaṃgatā

pramādino devā api kṣīṇapuṇyāḥ bhavantipuṇyakṣayāya devānāṃ pramādena vihiṃsitāḥ |patanti sukhasaṃmūḍhāstrāṇaṃ
teṣāṃ tadā kutaḥ

saddharmācaraṇaṃ bhūtadayaivaeko dharmastathāśreyaḥ kṛpayā saṃyataḥ pṛthuḥ |kṣāntiścāpi sadā
yuktā dayā bhūteṣu sarvadā

balabhūtā bhavantyete mṛtyukāle mahābhaye |tasmāt pramādarahitaiḥ sevanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ

vidyāvidhijñasyaiva prāṇinaḥ śāntirjāyateavidyāvartanaṃ śreyo vidyāyā rakṣaṇaṃ sadā |vidyāvidhijño
yaḥ (satvaḥ) pramādastasya śāmyati |puruṣārtho niyato (hyatra) yat pramādasya varjanam

apramādaratasyaiva puruṣārthasiddhirbhavatiapramādaratasyaiva puruṣārthaḥ satāṃ mataḥ |pramādo
bandhanaṃ prāyo muktistasyāpramādataḥ

pramādasevanād bandhanammokṣabandhanayoretallakṣaṇaṃ syāt satāṃ matamevaṃ matvā sadā devo yaḥ
pramādena rakṣati |sa paścānmṛtyusamaye jñāsyate tasya tatphalam

santoṣa eva nirvāṇasādhanamyathā yathā hi santoṣaḥ sevyate yatibhiḥ sadā |tathā tathā hi nirvāṇamanike
tasya vartate

virāgaḥ sarvakāmeṣu nirvāṇe ca pravartatām |nāsau mārasya viṣayaḥ kadācit sampravartate

jñānenaiva duḥkhaprahāṇamnarāṇāṃ paśya manaso nityaṃ vyāpārameva ca |udyogaśca sadājñānaṃ sa
kathaṃ duḥkhameṣyati

kaḥ bandhanamukto bhavati?atītabhayasampannaḥ pratyutpanne ca buddhimān |anāgatavidhijño yaḥ
sa muktaḥ kleśabandhanāt

apramādarato nityamaviśvāse ca kātaraḥ |saṃjñānasevī vimalo nirvāṇasyāntike sthitaḥ

mahatsukhaṃ pramādena naśyatipramādenāpi naśyanti devāḥ prāpyamahatsukham |kiṃ punaryena vā
mūḍhāḥ pramādavaśasevinaḥ

mṛtaḥ sa naro bhavati yaḥ pramādavihāravān |jīvite ca pramatto'yaṃ satataṃ jñānadhāraṇe

pramādāpramādayorantaramapramādapramādābhyāmidamantaramiṣyate |mṛtyuṃ ca varjayed doṣaṃ pramādaṃ
duḥkhamūlakam

nityaṃ pramuditā devāḥ nityaṃ pramuditā vayam |pakṣiṇāṃ ca surāṇāṃ ca viśeṣo nopalabhyate

na dharmācaraṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mokṣacaryā na yātyasau |tathaiva yadi devānāṃ te gatāḥ pakṣibhiḥ samā

ye pramādavinirmuktā ye ca dharmaratāḥ sadā |te devāḥ satpathā loke na pramādavihāriṇaḥ

yadi krīḍāratā devāḥ pramādacaritāḥ sadā |devānāṃ ca khagānāṃ ca viśeṣo nopalabhyate

dharmapatitaḥ jātyā na śobhatekarmaṇāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa jātirdharmairviśiṣyate |na dharmapatitaḥ
kaścid jātyā bhavati śobhanaḥ

yena nindanti saṃsāramimaṃ sarvakṣayātmakam |te surāpisamā nityaṃ pakṣibhirmūḍhabuddhibhiḥ

yeṣāmevasthitā buddhiramatā dharmagocare |te devāḥ sammattā loke na pramādavicāriṇaḥ

ye janmahetuprabhavaṃ duḥkhaṃ budhyanti śobhanam |te devā na tu ye saktāḥ kāmeṣu hitakāriṣu

viyujyamānā bahuśo bhṛtyasvajanabāndhavaiḥ |ye nodvijanti saṃsāre te devāḥ pakṣibhiḥ samāḥ

madyapānādhikaḥ pramādaḥmadyapānādhiko dṛṣṭaḥ pramādastattvadarśibhiḥ |jīryate madyapānaṃ hi
pramādo naiva jīryate

pramādamatto gatipañcake bhramatepramādopahatoloko bhramate gatipañcake |tasmāt pramādamatto
hi sarvopāyairviśiṣyate

pramādaḥ madyādapi hīnataraḥekāhaṃ paramaṃ madyaṃ pramādayati dehinaḥ |pramādaḥ kalpakoṭibhirbhramato'pi
na jīryate

ye pramādairvirahitāste gatāḥ padamavyayam |ye tu pramādavaśagāste bhavanti khagā narāḥ

hitārthinā manuṣyeṇa pramādovarjya eva hi |yasmāt pramādavaśagāḥ kleśā buddhena deśitāḥ

devānāṃ kṛte'pi pramādo heya evakhagā yadi pramādena kurvanti laghucetasaḥ |kasmāddevāḥ pramādaṃ(taṃ
) na jahaṃti viśeṣataḥ

yastu dūrāt pramādena bhayaṃ nāvaiti durmatiḥ |so'vaśyaṃ vyasane prāpte paścāttāpena dahyate

patanaṃ devalokāt te dānai rakṣanti dāruṇam |tad vicintya pramādaste na saṃsevyaḥ kathañcana

ye pramādaratā nityaṃ na te saukhyasya bhāginaḥ |pramādo duḥkhamūlaśca mūlamekaṃ sudāruṇam

padmakoṭisahastrāṇi niyutānyarbudāni ca |asaṃkhyāni ca devānāṃ pramādena vitanvitāḥ

apramādaḥ paraṃ mitraṃ pramādaśca śatruḥapramādaḥ paraṃ(mitraṃ) nityaṃ hitakaraṃ nṛṇām |pramādastu
paraṃ śatrustasyanmitraparo bhavet

śubhasyāntakaro hayeṣa viṣavad dāruṇaṃ param |durgatīnāṃ paraṃ mārgaḥ pramāda iti kathyate

pramādena pramattā ye viṣayaiścāpi rañjitāḥ |narāste mūḍhamanaso nityaṃ duḥkhasya bhoginaḥ

jñānaśūnyāḥ bhayaduḥkhamavigaṇya paśubhiḥ samāḥ bhavantiyeṣāṃ bhayaṃ na duḥkhaṃ ( ca) na ca
jñānāvalokanam |paśubhiste samāviṣṭā na purā sukhakāṃkṣiṇaḥ

āhāramaithunaratiḥ paśūnāṃ hṛdi vartate |sā ratiryadi devānāṃ te gatāḥ paśubhiḥ samāḥ

pramādaḥ mṛtyoḥ paryāya evakrīḍantyatiśayaṃ hyete mṛtyorgamye puraḥsthitā |samprāpte mṛtyusamaye
phalaṃ dāsyanti (dāruṇam )

viṣavat pariheyo('yaṃ) sarvairapi (suduḥsahaḥ) |mṛtyoḥ paryāyanāmaiṣa pramādo hṛdi dehinām

pramādena hatān pūrvaṃ paścānmṛtyuḥ pramardati |dharmajīvitasaukhyānāṃ tamekāṃśaṃ prakathyate

apramāda eva svargamārgaḥapramāda iti khyātaḥ svargamārga pradeśakaḥ |arthānathau samāvetau
paścānmokṣastathaiva (ca)

apramādaḥ guṇaḥ pramāda eva doṣaḥapramādaḥ pramādaśca guṇadoṣasamāvibhau |tatraiva mūḍhamanaso
vijānanti ca dehinaḥ |116||śatruṇā saha rakṣanti jñāne pariharanti ca |pramādaviṣavṛkṣasya
śākhāstistraḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ

jarā vyādhiśca mṛtyuñca nityaṃ tasyoparisthitāḥ |jarādayo na bādhyante puruṣaṃ satkriyānvitam

apramādī sadā bhayanirmuktaḥ san sukhītiṣṭhatisaṃsāre tiṣṭhate dhīmānapramādarataḥ sadā |nikṛntanti
sadā doṣā na pramādaṃ pariṣvajet

sadā bhayavinirmuktaḥ sukhaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam |(a) pramādācca yat saukhyaṃ śāśvataṃ sa bhayaṃ
hi tat

yattu tasmād vinirmuktaṃ tat saukhyaṃ dhruvamacyutam |śataśo manujā (hayatra) pramādena vimohitāḥ

tathāpi nāma vaśagāste pramāde pratiṣṭhitāḥ |catvāro hi viparyāsāḥ pramādasyoparisthitāḥ

pramādavirahāttepi naśyanti lokaśatravaḥ |yadanekavikalpo'yamanekabhayasaṅkaṭaḥ

pramādarahitāḥ devāḥ santo'cyutaṃ sukhamaśnutesaṃsāro bhramate duḥkhe tat pramādasya ceṣṭitam
|ekaḥ pramādavirahāt prāpyate sukhamacyutam

pramādena vinaśyanti sarvadharmā hatāstravāḥ |devānāṃ ca pramādo'yamuparyupari vartate

kathaṃ pramādasammūḍhāḥ devā yāsyanti nirvṛtim |tade(ta)t saumyamanasā cintayitvā vikalpayet

tathā me hita māstheyaṃ yathā yat syāt (sukhāvaham ) |ye devā yacca tatsaukhyaṃ paśyan yadapikiñcana

saṃskṛtasyaiva dhruvapadaprāptirbhavatitat sarva (hi) dhruvaṃ gatvā saṃskṛtasyaiṣa sambhavaḥ
|avaśyaṃ te vinaśyanti ye bhāvāḥ saṃskṛtāścalāḥ

pramādavaśād devā api duḥkhabhāginaḥ bhavantipramādaniratā (devā)nityaṃ duḥkhasya bhāginaḥ |pramādāpahatajano
yasteṣāṃ kurute matiḥ

saṃviyoge samutpanne duḥkhena paritapyate |viṣayeṣu sakāmeṣu tṛṣṇāvanagateṣu ca

tena te vañcitā devāḥ pramādavaśavartinaḥ |mūlametadanarthānāṃ yatpramādānusevanam

pramādavarjanaṃ kṣemakaramtasyaitad varjanaṃ dhanyaṃ sarvakṣemakaraṃ mahat |satvā naivāpannasukhā
duḥkhaiścāpi samanvitāḥ

tathā buddhiranuṣṭheyā yathā matsyā jalānugāḥ |svarge pramādavaśagā strīvidheyāśca te surāḥ

pramādino duḥkhabhāgino bhavantite strīvahnivinirdagdhā nityaṃ duḥkhasya bhāginaḥ |tasmāt prayatnaśo
devairaṅganā parivarjanam

karttavyaṃ kāmalolasya manasā dhṛtivardhanam |kāryākāryevimūḍhasya dharmādharme tathaiva ca

puruṣasyātmabhaṅgasya nirvāṇaṃ dūrameva tat |gurutābhāvatattvajño nipuṇo dharmagocaraḥ

dharmiṇa eva sukhamāpnuvantidharmākāṃkṣī phalākāṃkṣī tādṛśaṃ labhate sukham |nidhautamaghakalmāṣāḥ
nityaṃ dharmānuvartinaḥ

pramādavimohitā eva duḥkhamāpnuvantisukhinaste sadā dṛṣṭā na pramādavihāriṇaḥ |pramādāpahataḥ
pūrva pramādena vimohitaḥ

madyena devo'pi pramādametidevo vā puruṣaścāpi na sukhasyāntike hi saḥ |mṛtaḥ sa puruṣo nityaṃ
yo madyena pramādyati

madyadoṣādhṛtāḥ sarve bhavanti narake narāḥ |asaṃsargacaro doṣo madyamityabhidhīyate

madyena mohitā nityaṃ devāḥ narakagāminaḥ |asambhūteṣu rakṣante na sambhūte kathañcanaḥ

madātsvākāramalinā devāḥ kāmairvimohitāḥ |kāmena mohitā devā madyenāpi tathaiva ca

pramādī tattvaṃ na paśyatina tattvamatra paśyanti jātyandhā iva satpatham |pramādākulitaṃ cittaṃ
na tattvamanupaśyati

pramādaścāgnivattasmāt parivarjyaḥ samantataḥ |pramādena vinaśyanti kuśalā dharmayonayaḥ

pramādaviṣasevanaṃ nāśāyaiva jāyatemārga ca viṣasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ sarvathā naiva paśyati |daśadharmā
vinaśyanti pramādaviṣasevinām

dhyānāni caiva catvāri praṇaśyanti pramādinaḥ |apramādaṃ praśaṃsanti buddhāḥ kāmavivarjitāḥ

apramattā jarāmuktā bhavantipramādañca jugupsanti jarāmaraṇapañjaram |apramattā jarāmuktā pramattā
duḥkhabhoginaḥ

pramāda eva bandhanam |pramādo bandhanaṃ hayetad duḥkhitaṃ mandabuddhinām |apramādena kuśalā
devānāṃ samitiṅgatāḥ

tasmāt te patitā bhūyo ye pramādānusevinaḥ |pramattapuruṣaḥ sarva saṃsārānnaiva mucyate

pramādapāśapāśena yena baddhā hi dehinaḥ |akārya kāryasadṛśaṃ kārya kurvanti sarvadā

apramādinaḥ kalyāṇaparamparāmāpnuvantiapramādānnaraḥ sarva viparītaṃ (hi) paśyati |na laukikeṣu
kāryeṣu kuto na śraiyaseṣu ca

pramādaṃ na praśaṃsanti paṇḍitā buddhipāragāḥ |yathā śubhaṃ parikṣīṇaṃ bhaviṣyati divaukasām

pramādaphalaṃ hānikaramtadā pramādasya phalaṃ jñāsyanti kaṭukaṃ hi tat |kāmasaṃsaktamanasāṃ
tasyānte sukhasevinām

bhaviṣyati sukhaṃ tasmād vinipātodayo mahān |viṣayābhimukhepsūnāṃ nityamāśāgatātmanām

strīdarśanasumattānāṃ vinipāto bhaviṣyati |(nityaṃ kāmān) niṣevante pramādarāgasevinaḥ

tāḥ sarvā mṛtyusamaye parityakṣyanti yoṣitaḥ |cyavamānaṃ suraṃ sarve na kaścidanugacchati

mokṣābhilāṣiṇaḥ duṣkṛtaṃ tyajantikarmaṇā pṛṣṭhataḥ sarva gacchantamanuyāti ca |yuktaṃ ca (hi)
sadā sevyaṃ varjanīyaṃ ca duṣkṛtam

pramādaṃ ca madaṃ jahayāt pramāda (vi)rato bhavet |pramādo bhavamūlo'yaṃ pramādastu na śāntaye

pramādāpramādau vicintya dhīraḥ sukhamedhateapramādapramādābhyāmidamuktaṃ svalakṣaṇam |tad vicintya
sadā dhīraḥ sukhaṃ sucaritaṃ caret

dharmacārī kadāpi duḥkhaṃ nāpnotina dharmacārī puruṣaḥ kadācid duḥkhamṛcchaiti |saṃsarantyatha
saṃsāre prāṇinaḥ svena karmaṇā

pramādo vinipātāyakimarthamihaloko'yaṃ pramādena vihanyate |pramādaḥ śreyasāṃ nāśaḥ pramādo
bandhanaṃ param

pramādo vinipātāya pramādo narakāya ca |duḥkhasya heturevaikaḥ pramādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ

apramādarato nirvāṇamadhigacchatitasmāt sukhārthī puruṣaḥ pramādaṃ parivarjayet |yaiḥ pramādaḥ
parityaktaḥ prāptaṃ taiḥ padamacyutam

apramādarato yo hi nirvāṇasyaiva so'ntike |apramādapadaṃ hayetannirvāṇasyāgrataḥ padam

pramattaḥ sadaiva duḥkhito bhavatipramādo vinipātāya hetureṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |pramattaḥ puruṣaḥ
sarvaḥ sonmāda iva lakṣyate

laghutvaṃ yāti loke'smin pratyavāyeṣu pacyate |pramattaḥ puruṣaḥ śakto viparīteṣu vartate

hetau karmavipāke ca mṛtyūtpattau tathaiva ca |pramādāgniśca yaṃ tīkṣṇo narakānupakarṣati |tasmānnarakamokṣārtha
pramādaṃ vinivarjayet

ye pramādaṃ vinirjitya nityaṃ jñānaratā narāḥ |te kleśabandhanaṃ chitvā padaṃ yātāḥ sukhodayam

karmasūtrairnibaddhāśca cittadolāṃ samāśritāḥ |bhramanti vibhave sattvā mā pramādeṣu rakṣathaḥ

sukhī bhavati duḥkhī vai duḥkhitaścāpi sukhitaḥ |bhartāpi tṛpto bhavati mā pramādeṣu rakṣathaḥ

mātā pitā vā bhavati bhāryā mātṛtvameva ca |parivarto mahāneṣu mā pramādeṣu rakṣathaḥ

pramādājjāyate rāgo rāgād dveṣaḥ prapadyate |sa doṣapathamāpanno narakānupadhāvati

prajñāruḍhaḥ pramādaśūnyaḥ san śivaṃ panthānamāpnotiprajñā-prāsādamāroha yogakṣemamanuttamam
|eṣa panthāḥ śivaḥ śreṣṭho yaḥ pramādavivarjitaḥ |tena mārgeṇa satataṃ nirvāṇaṃ yānti paṇḍitāḥ

virodho mārgasampattau cittasantānadūṣakaḥ |āchettā dharmasetūnāṃ pramādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ

pramādopahatāḥ nāśaṃ yāntismṛti sandūṣakaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mokṣāya vṛttināśakaḥ |durgatīnāṃ paraṃ netā
pramādaḥ sampravartate

aneka puruṣaḥ kṣipto nātmano vindate hitam |nāvācyaṃ na ca kāryāṇāṃ vindate'mṛtakopamaḥ

ta ete paśubhistulyā devavigrahadhāriṇaḥ |pramādopahatā mūḍhā nṛtyanti ca hasanti ca

utpannāvicyutāḥ mārgāt kṣāntiṃ ye nāśayanti ca |nṛbhavārṇavabhūtā ye te pramādānudhāvinaḥ

janakaḥ sarvadoṣāṇāṃ bandhanaṃ pāpakarmaṇām |pramoṣaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ pramādāriḥ pravartate

sarveṣāṃ śubhakarmāṇāṃ pramādaḥ śatrurevanādhyātmikāni karmāṇi na bāhayāni kathañcana |pramādopahato
janturjānīte naṣṭamāsanaḥ

krīḍāyāṃ vyagramanaso nṛtyagāndharvalālasāḥ |atṛṣṇārviṣayairdivyairnakṣyanti vibudhālayāḥ

bhayasthāne hasantyete pramādena vimohitāḥ |mārgāmārga na vindanti jātyandhena surāḥ samāḥ

kāmadhātāveva pramattāḥ bhramantikāmadhātau bhramanyete cakravadgati pañcake |dhyānebhyo yaddhi
patanaṃ tat pramādasya ceṣṭitam

pramādaceṣṭitaṃ karmapatanāya jāyateārūpyebhyaśca yatsthānaṃ caturthaṃ prāpyalaukikāt |bhramanti
bhramadolāyāṃ tat pramādasya ceṣṭitam

pramādabandhanairbaddhaṃ tṛṣṇāpāśaiśca yantritam |traidhātukamidaṃ kṛtsnaṃ na ca buddhayantya
cetasaḥ

yat prayānti dharmasthānaṃ tṛṣṇābhayadarśitāḥ |na bhūyaḥ khedamāyāti tat pramādasya ceṣṭitam

pramādavaśāt duḥkhamevāpnoti janaḥpriyaviśleṣajaṃ nṛṇāṃ yadduḥkhaṃ hṛdi jāyate |sevanād yat
pramādasya kathayanti tathāgatā

anarthā hi trayo loke yairidaṃ naśyate jagat |vyādhirjarā ca mṛtyuśca pramādālasya sambhavāḥ

prarohanti yathā bhūmau savauṣadhitṛṇādayaḥ |tathā pramādināṃ kleṣāḥ pravartante pṛthagvidhāḥ

pramādasya viṣāṅkuraḥstrīśleṣo madyapānaṃ ca krīḍā ca viṣayaiḥ saha |cāpalyamayakausīdyaṃ pramādasya
viṣāṅkuraḥ

pramādāpramādayorlakṣaṇampramādaḥ paramaṃ duḥkhapramādaḥ paraṃ sukham |samāsāllakṣaṇaṃ proktamapramādapramādayoḥ

ataḥ pramādo na sevyaḥtasmāt pramādo na nareṇa sevyaḥ,sa durgatīnāṃ prathamāgrameva |vihāya
taṃ duḥkhasahastrayoniṃ,prayānti buddhā bhavapāragrayam

||iti apramādavargaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ||

## 7 kāmajugupsāvargaḥ

(7) kāmajugupsāvargaḥkāmasya narakahetutvamna kāmeṣu ratiṃ kuryāt kāmāḥ paramavañcakāḥ |saṃsārabandhanā
ghorāḥ sarve narakahetavaḥ

yaḥ saṃrakṣati kāmeṣu tasya duḥkhamanantakam |na kāmoragadaṣṭasya sukhamasti kathañcana

rāgādivaśagānāṃ ceṣṭitamvaraṃ niśitadhāreṇa kṣureṇa svayamātmanaḥ |saṃkṣoditā bhavejjihvā na
coktaṃ kāmagaṃva(caḥ)

rāgeṇa vañcitāḥ sattvā dveṣeṇa paripīḍitāḥ |mohasya vaśamāpannā bhāṣante'madhuraṃ vacaḥ

rāgavaśagaḥ sadā duḥkhatamamanubhavatialparāgānniruddho yaḥ kurute duṣkṛtaṃ bahu |sa rāgavaśago
mūḍho duḥkhād duḥkhamavāpnuyāt

kāmapariṇatimāhaatṛptirasmāt kāmāste na sukhā nāpi śāśvatāḥ |pariṇāme mahātīvrāstasmāt tān parivarjayet

rāgābhibhūtāḥ puruṣā narakālayavartinaḥ |na rāgavaśagā ye tu na teṣāṃ narakād bhayam

kāmaviṣaṃ parityājyamcaṇḍācaṇḍisamutthāśca caṇḍāśca pariṇāmataḥ |kāmā viṣāgnipratimāḥ parivarjyāḥ
prayatnataḥ

parivarjitakāmasya nityaṃ mokṣaratasya ca |naśyantyakuśalā dharmāstamaḥ sūryodaye tathā

kāmānalaḥ narake pātayatiindriyāṇīndriyārthajño mohayitvā pṛthagvidhāḥ |narake pātayantyete
kāmāḥ bālamanoharāḥ

rāgāgneḥ mahimāpañcendriyaprasaktasya viṣayaiḥ pañcabhistathā |muhūrtamapi rāgāgnirviṣayairnaiva
tṛpyati

saṃśleṣājjāyate vahnirviśleṣānnaiva jāyate |saṃśleṣādapi viśleṣo (rāgā) gnirjīryate nṛṇām

dūrānna dāhako vahnirviṣayastasya nāsti saḥ |dūrāntikasamo ghoro rāgāgniratidāhakaḥ

saṅkalpakāṣṭhaprabhavaḥ spṛhākuṭilavegavān |tṛṣṇā ghṛtaprasakto'yaṃ rāgāgnirapi dāhakaḥ

dagdhvā śarīrametaddhi jvalanaḥ sampraśāmyate |nāmarūpavinirmukto rāgāgnirnaiva śāmyate

udvejayati bhūtāni vahniḥ paramadāhakaḥ |rāgāgniratitīkṣṇo'pi nodvegaṃ kurute nṛṇām

pañcendriyasamuttho'yaṃ viṣayaiḥ pañcabhirvṛtaḥ |tṛṣṇāsamīraṇabalād rāgāgni(rdahati) prajāḥ

vitarkāraṇisambhūto viṣayaiḥ parivardhitaḥ |kāṣṭhavad dahyate tena na ca dṛśyo janasya saḥ

yathā yathā hi prabalo rāgāgnistapyate mahān |tathā tathā (ca) rāgāndhaiḥ svasukhaṃ parisevyate

agniḥ prakāśako bhavati rāgāgnistamasāvṛtaḥ |tasmācchiśiravad dhīmān rāgāgniṃ parivarjayet

viṣayapariṇāmamāhaviṣayābhirato janturna sukhī syāt kathañcana |viṣayāḥ viṣayairdaṣṭāḥ pariṇāme
ca dāruṇāḥ

nādye nānte na madhye ca nāsmiṃlloke na cāpare |sukhadā viṣayā kāmaṃ bhavantīha kathañcana

bāliśasya hi sarvasya tṛṣṇākrīḍanasaṃbhavaiḥ |na tṛpto viṣayairagnirjvalanasya yathendhanaiḥ

atṛptoḥ viṣayaiḥ sarvo jano'yaṃ parivañcitaḥ |mṛtyunā'bhyāsanirata etairdoṣairvidahyate

kāmamohāndhānāṃ sthitimāhasukhānāṃ kāmacārāṇāṃ mohasya khalu ceṣṭitam |tadeva yadi devānāṃ khagaiḥ
syuste samāḥ surāḥ

kalpānteṣvabhisantaptaḥ śasyate saritāṃ patiḥ |na dṛṣṭistṛpyate rūpaiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi

syāt samudrasya caryāptiḥ salilairvaśagādibhiḥ |na tu dṛṣṭiḥ samudrasya rūpārthaistṛptirasti
hi

avitṛptasya kāmebhyaḥ kiṃ sukhaṃ parikalpyate |tṛptiryāsti vitṛṣṇasya gataśokasya dehinaḥ

mādyante bahumohāndhā na ca buddhayanti bāliśāḥ |mandena kṣaṇikā yadvat malayendhanapādapāḥ

kāmā asārāḥ vañcakāśca bhavantisvapnapramoṣadharmāṇo gandharvanagaropamāḥ |riktāstucchā asārāśca
kāmāḥ paramavañcakāḥ

(kā) mā mohena ( ca) samāḥ kiṃ pākasadṛśopamāḥ |kāmā lokahitakarā vahnivat parikīrtitāḥ

kāmasyādīnavaṃ jñātvā yena tatphalamohitāḥ |tena taddarśakāḥ prītā gatakāṃkṣasya (dehinaḥ)

(a)nyathā cintyamānānāṃ yathā prāptā punastathā |sarvataḥ pāpakartāraḥ kāmā loke viṣopamāḥ

kāmairatṛptamanasaste te devāścyutāḥ punaḥ |patanti narake mūḍhāḥ kāmena parivañcitāḥ

nārīṃ nindayatinadītaraṅgacapalā vidyullekhasamāśca te |mīnāvartabalā nāryaḥ kāmalokaviṣāspadāḥ

vicintitā vivardhante varddhitā vahnisannibhāḥ |ādau cānte tathā kāmāstasmāt kāmān vivarjayet

kāmasevanaphalamyathā yathā hi sevyante vivardhante tathā tathā |vahnijvālāsamāsṛṣṭāḥ kāmāḥ
kaṭuvipākinaḥ

kāmavarjanaphalam āhaevaṃ doṣaṃ sadā jñātvā dhīraḥ kāmān vivarjayet |parivartitakāmasya sukhaṃ
bhavati naiṣṭhikam

kāmāgniḥ devānnapi pātayatiasaṅkhyāni sahasrāṇi devānāṃ niyutāni ca |patanti kāmadahanaṃ narakaṃ
vahnibhairavam

yathāgniviṣaśastrāṇi varjayanti sukhārthinaḥ |tathā kāmāḥ sadā varjyā hetavo narakasya te

na dṛṣṭo na śrutaḥ kaścid yaḥ kāmavaśagaḥ pumān |na kāmairvipralabdhaḥ syānna ca duḥkhena pīḍitaḥ

tasmādalamalaṃ kāmairmā caiteṣu manaḥ kṛthāḥ |sarve sarvāsvavasthāsu kāmā vahniviṣopamāḥ

anādirmati saṃsāre śatravaścittasambhavāḥ |ahantvāttu bhavet prītirna sā kāmaiḥ kathañcana

sakalmaṣā kaṭuphalā duḥkhāt kāmodbhavā ratiḥ |yā tu kāmavinirmuktā sā ratiḥ pari(bhāṣitā)

yoginaḥ paramā gatiḥtāṃ samāśritya gacchanti yoginaḥ paramāṃ gatim |na tu kāmakṛtā prītirnayate
padamacyutam

kāmajaratiṃ nindatiāpātamadhurā ramyā vipāke jvalanopamā |ratirbhavati kāmāgnijanyā narakagāminī

āpātaramyā madhurā madhye ramyā ca sarvadā |śāntamante ca vimalaṃ nayate padamacyutam

ādyantamadhyakalyāṇī nityaṃ māteva śobhanā |tāṃ vyudasya kathaṃ bālā rakṣante (kāmajāṃ ratim
)

kāmakṛtā ratiḥ nirataṃ tapatimadhyādinidhane duḥkhā nityaṃ doṣādibhirvṛtā |kathaṃ sā sevyate
bālairyā na dṛṣṭā sukhāvahā

viṣamañjarivad ramyā sparśe jvalanasambhavā |tathā kāmakṛtā prītiḥ pariṇāme viṣopamā

hūyamāno yathā vahnirjvalanena praśāmyati |dāhena ca prarohaḥ syāt tadvat kāmakṛtā ratiḥ

pataṅgaḥ paśyati hyagni dāhadoṣaṃ na paśyati |tathā kāmakṛtāṃ prītiṃ paśyantyakṛtabuddhayaḥ

yastu rāgakṛto dāhaḥ pacyate kāmināṃ sadā |pataṅgasadṛśaṃ dāhaṃ sarvathā nai(va) paśyati

kāmapramādāt patanaṃ dhruvamevatasmāt kāma(viṣaṃ) tyaktvā nityaṃ jīvatha he surāḥ |bhavantu
mā vṛthā janmapramādena prapātanam

saṃkṣīṇaśubhakarmāṇo nityaṃ kāmairvimohitāḥ |taṃ hitvā narakaṃ yānti kāmamohena vañcitāḥ

kāmamohitaḥ viṣavṛkṣamayaṃ puṣpaṃ pibativiṣavṛkṣamayaṃ puṣpaṃ pīyate bhramarairyathā |tathā
viṣātmakamidaṃ sukhaṃ bhuṅkte hi mohitaḥ

jīveyuḥ pāmarāḥ kecit viṣaṃ pītvā'pi durbhagāḥ |na kāmaviṣapītasya jīvitaṃ durlabhaṃ bhavet

yathā hi narake vahnirjvalayatya vicāriṇam |tathā kāmamayo vahnirdahatīha divaukasaḥ

kāmāgniḥ hiṃsaka evaśrutipāśamayo vahniḥ pretāneṣa dahatyati |dahane hiṃsako vahnirnṛṇāṃ paryeṣaṇātmakaḥ

evamagnisamaṃ tāvat parihārya samantataḥ |sarvalokamaśeṣeṇa dahyate kāmamohitaḥ

viṣayāsaktaṃ manaḥ vyasane pātayativiṣayeṣu sadācittaṃ dhāvantaṃ cañcalaṃ mahat |na tyājyaṃ
vyasane mūḍha yad paśyasi bhaviṣyati

kāmeṣu rakṣa te cittaṃ vyasanenāvabudhyate |vyasanaughe samutpanne tat paścāt paritapyate

kāmamohitāḥ mahadbhayamapi na paśyantivṛthā kāmamadairmattā devāḥ prakṛtidurbalāḥ |bhramanti
bhramitāḥ kāmairna paśyanti mahad bhayam

viśvasanti hi ye devāḥ kāmeṣvahitakāriṣu |vyatirekeṣu te paścāt pratibudhyantyamedhasaḥ

kāmāḥ paramavañcakā bhavantina ca paśyanti (vi)budhāścittena parivañcitāḥ |kṣaṇikā madhurā jātāḥ
kāmāḥ paramavañcakāḥ

kāma prati na viśvasetśataśaśca sahastraiśca koṭiśaḥ padmaśastathā |labdhā naṣṭā punaḥ kāmā
na teṣāṃ viścaset pumān

kāmairmohitaḥ pataṅgasamo narakāgninā dahyateviṣayād bandhanaṃ tīvraṃ sarve narakahetavaḥ |tasmādatyantatastyaktvā
śreyase kriyatāṃ manaḥ

rāgeṇa rañjitāḥ pūrvaṃ dveṣeṇa ca tiraskṛtāḥ |mohena mohitāścaiva te'śrutā'kṛtakāriṇaḥ

kāmārthaireṣyate bālaḥ punaḥ kāmairvimohitaḥ |sa pataṅgasamo mūḍhaḥ dahyate narakāgninā

kāmavaśagāḥ suralokādapi patantiavaśyambhāvi patanaṃ suralokāt samantataḥ |na jñātvā kāmavaśago
syādiha kathañcana

kāmena vañcitāḥ sattvāḥ kāmena ca vimohitāḥ |kāmapāśāpakṛṣṭāste patanti narake sadā

kāmaṃ tyaktva svahite manaḥ karttavyamtadetad vyasanaṃ sattvāḥ svahite kriyatāṃ manaḥ |manasāpi
svadāntena nānutapyanti dehinaḥ

manoviṣeṇa ye daṣṭāḥ kāmavegena sarvadā |te mūḍhā mṛtyuvaśagāḥ kāmānalahatā narāḥ

na tṛptirasti kāmānāṃ tṛṣṇayā hitakāriṇām |tṛṣṇāpi tṛptijanikā mano naiva hi tṛpyati

jñānadīpenaijendriyāṇāṃ viṣayebhyastṛptirjāyatena jātu viṣayaistṛptirindriyāṇāṃ bhaviṣyati |yadi
na jñānadīpena kṣapayiṣyanti (te narāḥ)

nārīsevanena nāśaḥ suniścitaḥyoṣitaḥ sevyamānā hi vitarkaśatamālikāḥ |pravardhati yathā vahnirvāyunā
samudīritaḥ

kāmāgnidīptāḥ kadāpi śāntiṃ nāpnuvantitaṃ matvā vegarabhasā nityaṃ kāmo'gnidīpitaḥ |kāmān hāpayati
jñānī buddhatattvavicintakaḥ

ye nityaṃ bhrāntamanaso nityaṃ viṣayatatparāḥ |ramante vibudhāḥ sarve tat sarvaṃ mohaceṣṭitam

sevyamāno hi vibudhairviṣayāgnirvivardhate |pāśenānena saṃyukto vahnirvāyusamīritaḥ

rāgavivaśā amarā api patantiamarāḥ rāgavivaśā nityaṃ viṣayatatparāḥ |devalokāt patantyete moharāgeṇa
vañcitāḥ

ramante viṣayairete tatra tallīnamānasāḥ |na ca vindanti yad duḥkhaṃ viprayogo bhaviṣyati

viyogajaṃ duḥkhaṃ kaṣṭapradamevayadetat sukhamevādau divyaṃ pañcaguṇānvitam |viyogajasya duḥkhasya
kalāṃ nārhatiṣoḍaśīm

kāmānāṃ tadvighāto hi janenaivopayujyate |satṛṣṇasya tathā tṛptiḥ kāmebhyo naiva jāyate

kāmānusevinaḥ duḥkhaṃ kadāpi śāntiṃ nādhigacchatianapekṣitacittasya nityaṃ kāmānusevinaḥ |dīrgharātrānuśayikaṃ
duḥkhaṃ naiva praśāmyate

tvaritaṃ rakṣyate mūḍho vyasanenaiva budhyate |paścāttu vyasane prāpte jānīte yasya tat phalam

āsvādabhadrakā hyete kāmāḥ paramadāruṇāḥ |dūtakān narakasyaitān carantyahitakāriṇaḥ

viṣayāgnidagdhāḥ narakaṃ yāntiyasteṣāṃ viśvaset martyo jñānacakṣurvigarhitaḥ |viṣayāgnirabhratulyaḥ
sa yāti narakaṃ naraḥ

alpāsvādālpahṛdayā nityaṃ puruṣavañcakāḥ |gandharvanagaraprakhyāḥ kāmā āsvādabhadrakāḥ

viṣayamohitāḥ devā api durgati yānti(yathā) dīpaprabhā(bhasma) sañchanneva punaḥ punaḥ |na ca
bindatyamanaso devā viṣayamohitāḥ

viṣayeṇātirabhasā nityaṃ kāmavaśānugāḥ |na vidanti mahad duḥkhaṃ yadavaśyaṃ bhaviṣyati

ramaṇīyāni kāmāni yasyaivaṃ jāyate matiḥ |sa paścād vyasane prāpte mūḍho'sau vipralapyate

na kāmena madāndhasya viṣayairmohitasya ca |naityikaṃ bhavati śarmma yatsukhānāmanuttamam

ādhyātmikasukhāpekṣayā kāmasukhaṃ hīnatamamyacca kāmasukhaṃ loke yacca tṛṣṇodbhavaṃ sukham |ekasyādhyātmikasyedaṃ
kalāṃ nārhatiṣoḍaśīm

kāmī kadāpi sukhaṃ nāśnutena sukhī vartate tāvadya yasya kāmo hṛdi sthitaḥ |sa sarvaduḥkhabhāgārho
narakānupadhāvati

na tṛptirvidyate kāmairapi (bhogaḥ) śataṃ nṛṇām |yatra saukhyaṃ na tatrāsti tṛptirviṣayasevinām

sevyamānāḥ sadā kāmā vardhante ca muhurmuhuḥ |te vardhitā viṣasamā bhavanti vinipātinaḥ

vipattikuśalā ghorā nityaṃ patanahetavaḥ |na ca teṣāṃ parityāgaṃ kurvanti viṣayotsavāḥ

yasya dṛṣṭisamudrasya rūpaistṛptirna vidyate |tathā śarmamanojñaiśca rasatṛptirna vidyate

gandhairapi sadā ghrāṇaṃ na tṛptimadhigacchati |sparśāḥ sammukhasaṃsparśāḥ sevyante naiva tṛpyate

śabdaiḥ kāntaiḥ sumadhuraiḥ śrotrameti na tarpaṇam |mano'pi tṛptiviśvastaṃ vardhamānaṃ na tṛpyati

indriyāṇi kāmabhūmiṣu pātayantiṣaḍendriyāṇi capalānyādhṛtāni ratau punaḥ |bhramanti muṣitā nityaṃ
kāmabhūmiṣvanekaśaḥ

na tṛptirasti devānāṃ gajānāṃ vai tṛṇairyathā |analasya svabhāvo'yaṃ tasya tṛptirna vidyate

ṣaḍete vahnayastīvrā vitarkānilamūrchitāḥ |bhramanti muṣitā nityaṃ kāmabhūmiṣvanekaśaḥ

indriyadagdhā narakaṃ yāntitairayaṃ dahyate loko na ca vindatyabuddhimān |āsvādabhadrakā hayete
narakasya ca hetavaḥ

kāmamohitaḥ sukhavañcito bhavatikāmā viṣayalobhāste lelihānā yathoragāḥ |vilocanavaro hayeṣa
na ca kāmairvimohitaḥ

kāmavaśīnaraḥ netrahīnā mūḍhataraḥsukhānusāriṇaḥ kāmo narakānupadhāvati |na netrahīno narake
pātyate satkriyānvitaḥ

tasmād varaṃ vicakṣuḥ syānna tu kāmavaśo naraḥ |aśakyavañcitā mūḍhāḥ kāmairahitakāribhiḥ

kāmacāriṇāṃ jñānādikaṃ durlabhamanirvidyanti kāmebhyo mohitāḥ svena cetasā |na jñānaṃ nāpi vijñānaṃ
vidyate kāmacāriṇām

kāmaḥ mṛtyubhavanaṃ nayatiyad duḥkhakāmajaṃ hatvā punaḥ kāmavaśānugāḥ |mitrarūpā ca ye kāmāḥ
kimpākaphalasannibhāḥ |nayanti mṛtyubhavanaṃ durgatīśca punaḥ punaḥ

anivarttya yathā toyamāpagānāmanekaśaḥ |tathā saukhyagataṃ nṛṇāṃ sarvathā na vivartate

vanopavanabhogeṣu sukhaprāpteṣvanekaśaḥ |yo na tṛpyati kāmeṣu sa naro'dhamagāmikaḥ

pramādo pahato jantuḥ kāmāsvādeṣu tatparaḥ |rūpeṣu rakṣyate nityaṃ pariṇāme ca vidyate

ghanacchāyāsvarūpāṇi karmamiśrāṇi yāni vai |tāni caiva kathaṃ devāḥ śakṣyante kāmagocare

kāmāḥ kadāpi na sevyāḥyadi nityā bhaveyurna kāmāste syurviṣopamāḥ

kāmatṛṣṇāduḥkhakarītathāpi kāmatṛṣṇā yā na sā jāti vilakṣaṇā |prāgeva nityaṃ duḥkhāya śūnyamātmānameva
ca

teṣu duḥkhavipākeṣu kathaṃ rakṣantyabuddhayaḥ |kāmāsvādeṣu rakṣyante bāliśāḥ mandabuddhayaḥ
|ādīnavaṃ na buddhayante kimpākaphalasannibham

rūpaśabdādibaddho'yaṃ tṛṣṇāviprakṛto janaḥ |dīpyate vivaśo nityaṃ, kukarmaphalamohitam

āsvādayitvā puruṣāḥ kāmān viṣaphalopamān |tṛṣṇayā tṛptamanasaḥ patanti narake punaḥ

yathābhivarṣate toyaṃ vardhante sarito yathā |tathā kāmābhivarṣeṇa devānāṃ vardhate'nalaḥ

kāmasukhasya nānto vidyatejalasambhavamīno'pi dṛśyate tṛṣṇayā''turaḥ |evaṃ sukhābhivṛddhā ca
na vitṛpyati devatā

ākāśasya yathā nānto vidyate nāpi saṃśayaḥ |evaṃ kāmeṣu nāstyantaḥ kāmināṃ nāsti saṃkṣayaḥ

salilādibhiratṛptasya sāgarasyormimālinaḥ |na tu kāmairatṛptasya tṛptirasti kathañcana

aprāptairviṣayairdevā na vitṛpyanti bāliśāḥ |viṣayairnaiva tṛpyanti sukhalālasatatparāḥ

kāmastāpāya, na tu śāntyaisaṃprāpte vyasane tīvre cyavamānepyanekaśaḥ |tapyate viṣaye tasmāt
paraṃ kāmo na śakyate

vilokya puruṣānete kāmaviśvāsaghātinaḥ |tyajanti vyasane prāptā vipadyante hi te narāḥ

atṛptasya sukhaṃ nāsti viṣayaiścāpi tṛpyate |ye vā tṛiptikarā dṛṣṭāstān buddhvā (tu) vivarjayet

viṣayānparityajya śāntiḥ sevyāsukhamūlā sadā śāntirasukhā viṣayā matāḥ |tasmācchāntisukhā devā
varjayitvā viṣayoragān

badhabandhanarogādi viṣayebhyo mahadbhayam |sambhrāntiriha saṃsāre viṣayaireva jāyate

saṃyogā viprayogāntāḥ śataśo'tha sahastraśaḥ |jātau jātau sadā dṛṣṭāḥ saugataistattvadarśibhiḥ

aneka sukhasaṃsāraḥ viṣayātmaka evaanekasukhasaṃsāro viṣayeṣu hi vidyate |viṣayaiśca bhavet
sarva jātau jātau prajāyate

kaṣṭairyairanakāmairna śakyate'mūḍhacetasā |punastāneva mohāndhāḥ sevante'kṛtabuddhayaḥ

viṣayaśatrusevinaḥ bāliśāḥ bhavantivarjate hi sadā śatruvañcanāśaṅkayā naraḥ |viṣayāḥ śatrubhūtā
hi na vaśyante kathañcana

kāmabaddhāḥ sadā mohabāliśā vā bhavanti te |na tyajanti kathaṃ mūḍhā mohitāḥ svena karmaṇā

yathā bahnibhayāt kaścid vahnimevopasevate |tathā viṣayasaṃmūḍho viṣayānupasevate

snāyusaṅgrathitaḥ pāśo dṛṣṭiramyo yathā bhavet |tathā viṣayaramyo'yaṃ pāśaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ

kāmasukhaṃ nāśāya bhavatikiṃpākasya yathā''svādo madhurāgro mahodayaḥ |paścād bhavati nāśāya
sukhaṃ tadvadidaṃ nṛṇām

pradīpasya śikhāṃ yāvat pataṅgo mohamūrchitaḥ |patate dahyate caiva tathedaṃ sukhamiṣyate

ajñātvā hi tathā bālā ramyadehasukhecchayā |spṛśanti jvalanaṃ tadvat sukhametad bhaviṣyati

tṛṣṇāvighāta eva sukhāya jāyateyathā ramyo vanamṛgastṛṣṇārta upadhāvati |na ca tṛṣṇāvighāto'sya
tadidaṃ sukhamiṣyate

na tṛptā na ca tṛpyanti na ca tṛptirbhaviṣyati |viṣayaiḥ sarvadevānāṃ tasmāt kāmo na śāntaye

viṣayaiḥ pramattāḥ duḥkhamanubhavantipratyutpannasukhāḥ kāmā nāntakalyāṇakārakāḥ |rañjitā viṣayairdevā
vikṣiptamanasaḥ sadā

nānāsaukhyapramattasya viṣayairvañcitasya ca |samprāpte mṛtyukāle ca na śamāyāsya vidyate

anukrameṇa maraṇamabhyeti tacca vidyate |viṣayāpahatairdevaiḥ kāmavyāsaktabuddhibhiḥ

kāmā ahitakāriṇo bhavantijānīyādadya me devā yad duḥkhaṃ viprayogajam |muhūrtamapi kāmeṣu na
kuryāt tatra saṃsthitim

rāgāgninā dagdho vimohito jāyateanityātmabhayāḥ kāmā nityaṃ cāhitakāriṇaḥ |tathā vimohitān kālaḥ
punastānanusevate

rāgāgninā pradahayante nityaṃ devāḥ pramohitāḥ |dahyamānāḥ punastattvaṃ praśaṃsanti punaḥ punaḥ

rathacakravatsadā bhrāntā viṣayavimohitāḥ bhavantiviṣayeṣu na rakṣyante teṣāṃ duḥkhamiva sthitam
|traidhātukamidaṃ sarva bhrāmyate rathacakravat

gandharvanagarasadṛśāḥ kāmāḥ bhavantisattvā avidyayā mugdhā nityaṃ duḥkhasya bhoginaḥ |vidyudālātacakreṇa
samāḥ kāmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |svapne gandharvanagarasadṛśā vipralambhinaḥ

pañcaskandhamatirduḥkhitastiṣṭhatianityaduḥkhaśūnyeṣu na kuryānmatimātmavān |pañcaskandhāsuraiḥ
proktaḥ śubhai riktaḥ svabhāvataḥ

anyathā viṣavad vijñaḥ kāmeṣu ca prasahyate |sa budhaḥ pāragaḥ śāntaḥ sattvānāmanukampakaḥ

nirvāṇonmukha eva doṣācchāntimadhigacchatihitvā kleśamayaṃ pāśaṃ nirvāṇasyāntike sthitaḥ

kāmavibhrāntasya śāntikathā vṛthāprabhavenna ca doṣeṣu kāmacaryāratasya ca |vibhrāntamanasastasya
kutaḥ śāntirbhaviṣyati ?

vibhrāntaṃ paśyatu mano viṣayeṣu pradhāvati |saddharmapathavibhrānto narakeṣūpapadyate

aśaktaprāptavibhraṣṭe kiṃ kāmairvidyutopamaiḥ |kiṃpākaviṣaśastrāgnisannibhairduḥkhahetubhiḥ?

kāmāgniḥ viṣayasevanena vardhateyathā yathā hi sevyante vardhante te tathā tathā |avitṛptikarā
hyete vahnidāhasya hetavaḥ

kāmān varjayitvaiva sukhamaśnuvate janāḥdāhadoṣeṇa sambhrāntāḥ ye surāḥ sukhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ |varjayitvā'śivān
kāmāstataḥ saukhyaṃ bhaviṣyati

kāmāḥ vidyud guṇopamāḥ cañcalāḥvañcayitvā janaṃ mūḍhaṃ śāṭī kṛtyeva bandhanam |pratyayo netracapalaḥ
kāmā vidyud guṇopamāḥ

uparyupari kāmā yaiḥ sevyante kāmatṛṣṇayā |te rāgavahninā dagdhā dāhād dāhamavāpnuyāt

atimūḍhatamā hyete ye surāḥ kāmamohitāḥ |athavā ye na gacchanti yatsukhāt sukhamuttamam

nirvāṇagāmino nāsti vinā muktyā kutaḥ sukham? |tasmāt kāmānna seveta kaṣṭaḥ kāmasamāgamaḥ

indriyāṇi na tṛpyante viṣayai rāgasevinaḥ |atṛptau ca kutaḥ śarma sarvathā sampraveśate

tasyāgramubhayād vetti saṃsārād duḥkhasāgarāt |kāmān tṛṣṇāviṣayagān parityajati pāpakān

kāmodayavyayau samyagavadheyauetāni girikūṭāni ramyāṇi vividhairdrumaiḥ |dhyāyante tāni saṃśritya
kāmānāmudayavyayau

śīlena śraddhayā ca iṣṭasādhanaṃ karttavyamna kāmabandhanestṛptāḥ kāmajaṃ nidhanaṃ hi tat |na
śīlaśraddhe yeneṣṭe labhyate'śivakāraṇam |kāmajaṃ nidhanaṃ hyetat saṃsārāṭavideśakam

pāpāni parityajya sukhāvahaḥ śāntimārgaḥ sevanīyaḥyatra kāmavisaṃyuktaṃ bandhanaṃ vanamucyate
|yatra bhāsayate pāpād yatra śāntiḥ sukhāvahā |tat kevalaṃ mahājñānaṃ kathyate nidhanaṃ
dhanam

kāmatṛṣṇābhyāṃ vimuktaḥ śivamāpnotiye prasaktā na kāmeṣu tṛṣṇayā na pralobhitāḥ |te śivaṃ sthānamāpannā
na kāmāgnipraveśakāḥ

avitṛptāḥ narāḥ naśyantyevatṛptirnāsti sadā kāmairna kāmāḥ śāntaye smṛtāḥ |tṛṣṇāsahāyasaṃyuktā
jvalanti jvalanopamāḥ

avitṛptā vinaśyanti narā devāstathoragāḥ |te kevalaṃ praṇaṣṭā hi narakāgni pradarśakāḥ

tṛṣṇāmohitasya mṛtyuradhipatiḥvitarkāpahatasyāsya viṣayairvañcitasya ca |tṛṣṇayā mohitasyaiṣa
mṛtyū rājā bhaviṣyati

kāmāsaktāḥ pathyāpathyaṃ na vijānantisaṃsaktakāmabhogeṣu pathyāpathyaṃ na vidyate |janā vimohitāḥ
sarvaiviṣayaiḥ kāmasaṃjñakaiḥ

kāmavaśānugāḥ viṣayaireva badhyanteviṣayaireva kṛṣyante ye surā mūḍhacetasaḥ |ye (tu) buddhaguṇairyuktā
na te kāmavaśānugāḥ

'dhīra' paribhāṣāpratyutpanneṣu kāmeṣu sadoṣeṣu viśeṣataḥ |yo na muhyati saukhyeṣu sa 'dhīra'
iti kathyate

kāmapatitānāṃ surāṇāṃ sthitimāhasvapnakāyavicitreṣu jvālāmālopameṣu ca |gandharvapuratulyeṣu
kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

tṛṣṇājanakabhūteṣu vināśāntakareṣu ca |kāntāreṣu viśāleṣu kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

ittvareṣu trichidreṣu nadīvegopameṣu ca |cañcaleṣvatidurgeṣu (kāmeṣu) patitāḥ surāḥ

pavanoddhṛtavegormijalacandre caleṣu ca |atātacakralokeṣu kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

samaṃ vidyullatā-cakra-mṛgatṛṣṇopameṣu ca |phenavaccāpyasāreṣu kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

kadalīgarbhatulyeṣu gajakarṇopameṣu ca |nadītaraṅgavegeṣu kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

kiṃpākaphalatulyeṣu vahnisannibhajātiṣu |māyāraśminibheṣveṣu kāmeṣu patitāḥ surāḥ

jñānāṅkuśena kāmo varjanīyaḥjñānāṅkuśena vāryante viṣayāstattvadarśibhiḥ |ye'muktāścapalāstīvrāḥ
sarvānarthakarā matāḥ

viṣayā eva narakapātahetavaḥviṣayāśā ca mūḍhānāṃ saṅkalpahṛtacetasām |mṛtyupāśo'yamabhyeti jīvitāśā(vi)bandhakaḥ

pacanti niraye kāmāḥ prāṇinaṃ laghucetasam |na ca vindanti mūḍhā ye mohena parivañcitāḥ

viṣayāścapalāḥ sarve gandharvanagaropamāḥ |duḥkhasaṃvartakā hyete narakāḥ pañcahetavaḥ

saṅkalpajo rāgaḥ narakaṃ pātayatisaṅkalpājjāyate rāgaḥ rāgāt krodhaḥ pravartate |krodhābhibhūtaḥ
puruṣo narakānupasevate

kāmaparityāgapūrvakaṃ nirvāṇaprāptaye yatetatasmāt kāmaṃ parityajya krodhaṃ nirvāsya paṇḍitaḥ
|mohaṃ cāpi parityajya nirvāṇābhimukho bhavet

nirvāṇapathikānāṃ kṛte śatruvadeva kāmaḥ parivarjanīyaḥśatruvad viṣayā jñeyā nirvāṇaṃ caiva
mitravat |pumān viśrāntaviṣayo nirvāṇamadhigacchati

kāmamalairalipta eva vimalaprakāśamāpnotialolupaḥ kāmamalairaliptaḥ prahīṇadoṣo hatavāṇatṛṣṇaḥ
|saṃkṣīṇadoṣo vimalaprakāśaḥ prayāti śāntiṃ svaphalopabhogī

sukhārthī kāmaṃ parityajyaiva śāntimadhigacchatiyaḥ kāmapaṅkoddhṛtavānadoṣaḥsarveṣu sattveṣu
sadā sukhārthī |sa nirmalo'śāntamanovimuktaḥprāpnoti nirvāṇasukhaṃ prasahya

iti kāmajugupsāvargaḥ saptamaḥ |

## 8 tṛṣṇāvargaḥ

(8) tṛṣṇāvargaḥtṛṣṇāgnireva narakabhūtaḥbhavotārāya tṛṣṇāgnirjvalanaḥ śīta ucyate |narake nārake
yo'gnistṛṣṇāgnistriṣu dhātuṣu

kalpabhūto hyayaṃ vahniḥ yo'yaṃ narakasambhavaḥ |bahujvālākulo vahniḥ tṛṣṇāhetusamudbhavaḥ

karmakṣayād vimucyante narakāt pāpakāriṇaḥ |triṣu dhātuṣu dahyante narāstṛṣṇāvaśānugāḥ

anādimati saṃsāre tṛṣṇāgniratibandhakaḥ |tṛṣṇāgnirnarakaṃ tasmānnāgnirnarakasambhavaḥ

tṛṣṇāgniḥ śarīraṃ manaśca dahatigātradāhaṃ paraṃ kuryānnārakeyo hutāśanaḥ |śārīraṃ mānasaṃ dāhaṃ
tṛṣṇāgniḥ kurute nṛṇām

tṛṣṇāgniḥ sāmānyāgninā viśiṣyatetasmād viśiṣyate vahniḥ tṛṣṇāhetusamudbhavaḥ |nārakeyo'samaścaiva
tṛṣṇāgnirnitarāṃ smṛtaḥ

ayamagniḥ trikālasambhavaḥtristhānagaḥ trihetuśca trikarmaparidīpakaḥ |trikālasambhavo jñeyastṛṣṇā'gniparakastathā

tṛṣṇāgniḥ sadaiva sarvatra dāhakaḥrāgāgnirdahyate svarge dveṣāgnistiryage tathā |mohāgnirdahyate
pāpe tṛṣṇāgniḥ sarvadā sthitaḥ

tṛṣṇāgneḥ svarūpa-nirūpaṇammānerṣyādhūmaviśikhaḥ saṅkalpe dhanasambhavaḥ |lobhāgnirdahate lokaṃ
nāgniḥ kāmasamudbhavaḥ

tṛṣṇāgnirviṣayasevanenaiva vardhatelobhāśīviṣadaṣṭā ye teṣāṃ śarma na vidyate |sevito bhāvito
lobho bhūya evābhivardhate

lobhasamo ripurbhuvi nāstiyathā yathendhanaṃ prāpyānalo vardhatyanekaśaḥ |śakyaḥ pālayituṃ(vahni)
rlobhavahnirna(śakyate)

cakravad bhramate loko lobhena parivañcitaḥ |anādinidhane loke nāsti lobhasamo ripuḥ

viśanti sāgarajale lobhena parivañcitāḥ |śastrasaṅghātagahanaṃ yuddhaṃ saṃpraviśanti ca

lobhahetorhi bhūpālā nāśayanti parasparam |śastrāsibhairavaprotā yuddhayante dhanatṛṣṇayā

tṛṣṇāviṣavinirmuktā lobhāṅgāravivarjitā |samaloṣṭakāñcanā ye nirvāṇasyāntike hi te

lobhāgniḥ sarvāpekṣayā viṣamaḥatīvānupapannasya dhanalobhena dahyate |na vahnirviṣamastatra
lobhāgniryatra vartate

lobhaśāntiṃ vinā nirvāṇakathā vṛthālobhādhāraprayatnena hanyāt tān rabhasā budhaḥ |anirvāpitalobhasya
nirvāṇaṃ dūrataḥ sthitam

bhavasukhaparityāgī bhavajaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāpnotibhavābhilāṣiṇīṃ nāndīṃ nābhinandanti ye narāḥ |na
teṣāṃ bhavajaṃ duḥkhaṃ svapne samupavidyate

tṛṣṇābaddhān janān mṛtyuḥ śambūka iva karṣatimatsyān yathā jālabaddhān śambūkaḥ parikarṣati
|tṛṣṇābaddhāṃstathā sattvānmṛtyuḥ samupakarṣati ||19tṛṣṇāviṣaṃ kutrāpi na muñcatisaviṣaiḥ
sāyakairviddho mṛgo yatra (pra)dhāvati |tatra tatra viṣaṃ yāti tathā tṛṣṇāviṣaṃ nṛṇām

pravāhapravahannadyā gatirgatya'nudhāvinī |yathā nirdahate bālān śuṣkendhanamivānalaḥ

āpātaramyā(viṣayā) vipākajvalanopamāḥ |tasmāt tṛṣṇā vimoktavyā yadi saukhyaṃ hi rocate

yathā hi vaḍiśagrastā mīnā mṛtyuvaśānugāḥ |tathā prataptā viṣayān paridhāvanti duḥkhitāḥ

tṛṣṇāgniḥ narakādapi mahānnarakaṃ nārakeyaṃ ca tṛṣṇānihitaṃ mahat |patanti nidhanā martyāḥ paradāropajīvinaḥ

tṛṣṇāprerito munirapi duḥkhamanubhavatina ceṣṭitamanopāpatṛṣṇayā prerito muniḥ |ṣaḍindriyasamudbhūto
viṣayendhanadāhakaḥ

devānapi tṛṣṇāgnirdahatyevatṛṣṇāgnirdahate devaṃ kāyāgnirna kathañcana |sukhāvṛtāḥ sukharatāḥ
sukhe(na) parivañcitāḥ

tṛṣṇāparivañcitaḥ patanamapi nāvagacchatipatanaṃ nāvagacchanti tṛṣṇayā parivañcitāḥ |jvālāmālākulaḥ
sarvaḥ saṃsārastṛṣṇayā''vṛtaḥ

tṛṣṇāgninā vaśībhūtā janā gacchanti durgatim |tṛṣṇāgnibhiḥ parivṛtaḥ suralokaḥ samantataḥ

tṛṣṇāgniḥ sadaiva vardhatedahyate vivaśo raktaḥ kāmabhogavaśīkṛtaḥ |yathā yathendhanaṃ prāpya
jvalanaṃ saṃpravardhate

tathā tathā sukhaṃ prāpya tṛṣṇāgnirvardhate nṛṇām |parivartayate puṃsaḥ kāṣṭhāgnirdāhadīpakaḥ

tṛṣṇāgnirdahate lokaṃ parihātuṃ na śakyate|ye viśālāṃ nadīṃ tīrṇā saṅkalpakṛtabhairavīm

tṛṣṇāpāśaśūnyaḥ paramāṃ śāntimāpnotite gatāḥ paramāṃ śāntiṃ yān hi tṛṣṇā na bādhate |tṛṣṇāpāśavimuktā
ye saṅgadoṣavivarjitāḥ

nirmuktapāpakalmāṣā vītaśokā hi te budhāḥ |kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi tṛṣṇayā vañcitā narāḥ

tṛṣṇayā vañcito lokastṛṣṇāmevopāste(na)te tyajanti(viṣayān) māyāmohavaśaṃgatāḥ |tṛṣṇayā vañcito
lokastṛṣṇāmevopasevate

lavaṇodaṃ tṛṣātoyaṃ yathā pibati nārikaḥ |na tena (tuṣyati) janturmuhuśca pariśuṣyati

duḥkhakarīṃ tṛṣṇāṃ na sevetaviṣayeṣveva tṛṣṇārtaśceṣṭate puruṣo'dhamaḥ |tasmāt tṛṣṇāṃ na seveta
sā hi tṛṣṇā durāsadā

tṛṣṇāvaśo naiva pramucyatetṛṣṇāvaśo hi puruṣaḥ saṃsārānnaiva mucyate ||anuttamāni saukhyāni
bhuktvā deveṣu jantavaḥ

tṛṣṇāpāśavikṛṣṭāḥ narakaṃ patantitṛṣṇāpāśavikṛṣṭāste patanti narakaṃ punaḥ |asvatantrādikalyāṇaṃ
nityaduḥkhamayaṃ kaṭu

tṛṣṇayā kadāpi tuṣṭirna bhavatitṛṣṇāyāḥ sevanānmuktaḥ sanmārgamadhigacchati |satṛṣṇasya kutastuṣṭirviṣayeṣu
bhaviṣyati

sā tṛptiryā vitṛṣṇasya vītaśokasya dehinaḥ |evaṃ vitarkavihitāḥ pramādena ca vañcitāḥ

tṛṣṇayā devānapi narakaṃ yāntitṛṣṇayāḥ toṣitā devāḥ patanti narakaṃ punaḥ |viḍambaneyaṃ paramā
yatsurā narakaṃ gatāḥ

krīḍakāḥ paramā bhūtvā kāmasya vaśamāgatāḥ |na codvijanti saṃsārān prāṇinaścittamohitāḥ

tṛṣṇayā duḥkhataraṃ padamāpnotiduḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ yānti tṛṣṇayā parivañcitāḥ |yathā yathā sukhasyāptirvardhate
jālinī tathā

jālinīvahnidagdhasya narakānupakarṣati |satṛṣṇasya vitarkā ye teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate

viṣayagāminaḥ tṛṣṇayā mṛtyumukhameva praviśantiavitarkavitarkantu mṛtyurājo vikarṣati |vitarkakāmavaśagāstṛṣṇāviṣayagāminaḥ

kāmāsvādapramattāḥ viṣayiṇo duḥkhinastiṣṭhantisukhasya bhoginaṃ dṛṣṭvā na vidvadbhistatheṣyate
|kāmāsvādapramattānāṃ prāṇināṃ viṣayārthinām

dehinaḥ tṛṣṇayā dahayantejālinī bādhate nityaṃ yathā badhnanti dehinaḥ |pañcālambanametattu
tṛṣṇayā naiva dahayate

tṛṣṇāvimuktā vimalā bhavantitṛṣṇāvimuktavimalā na pāpapuragāminaḥ |saṅkalpadoṣā kuṭilā tridoṣarajasodbhavāḥ

tṛṣṇayā saṃsārasāgare parivarte patanti janāḥpramādajalagambhīrāḥ strīrāgakṛtasevanāḥ |gītatūryasvarāḥ
śīghraṃ surāpānācca cañcalāḥ

sañchannaviṣayā sarve manaḥ kṣiptataraṅgiṇaḥ |tṛṣṇānadīṣu viṣame vahanti na ca gocare

gāhante te ca sammūḍhāḥ surā rāgeṇa vañcitāḥ |tridoṣakāṣṭhasaṃbhūtāḥ pramādānilavegataḥ

tṛṣṇānalaḥ suragaṇān dahate na ca te viduḥ |na kṣaṇo nāpi hi lavo na muhūrta kathañcana

yā na tṛṣṇāvaśagataiḥ suraiḥ samupabhujyate |tṛṣṇābhūmiriyaṃ kāṣṭhā vitarkajalasambhṛtā

tṛṣṇāsarpadagdhaḥ kālavaśīkṛto bhavatiyasmin krīḍanti vivaśāḥ devāḥ kāmavaśānugāḥ |cittādinā
pracaṇḍena tṛṣṇāviṣavisarpiṇā

daṣṭānupañcaśīrṣeṇa kiṃ vṛthā vilapasyatha |tṛṣṇānadī viśāleyaṃ pañcatīrthasamudbhavā

tṛṣṇāmohendrajālena viprakīrṇena sarvadā |tathā prapañcitā devā yathā na śubhabhāginaḥ

tṛṣṇāviṣayaghṛtasikto vardhata evanendriyāṇi sadā kāmaistṛpyanti hi kathañcana |saṃvardhate
tathā tṛṣṇā ghṛtasikto yathā'nalaḥ

tṛṣṇayā vividhāsu yoniṣu janāḥ bhramantinānāvidhaiḥ sukhaireṣā jālinī lokanāśinī |narakapretatiryakṣu
bhrāmayantī narān sadā

vītatṛṣṇaḥ nirmuktabandhanaḥ paramāṃ gatimāpnotimṛtyūpapattidolāyāṃ śliṣyante bāliśāḥ janāḥ
|suśīlāvītatṛṣṇāśca gatāste paramāṃ gatim

nirmuktabandhanā dhīrā gataśokā gatavyathāḥ |sukhaṃ prāpnuvanti nityaṃ ye na tṛṣṇāvaśānugāḥ
|janmaduḥkhamayaiḥ pāśairna te vidhyanti sūrayaḥ

tṛṣṇāvisṛṣṭiḥ jñānāya pravartayatiyeṣāṃ sarvāsvavasthāsu jñāneṣu vihitaṃ manaḥ |animitte mano
yeṣāṃ visṛṣṭā ye ca tṛṣṇayā |te vītamalakāntārāḥ pāraṃ prāptāḥ sukhodayam

tṛṣṇāmohapramattāḥ bhave bhave bhramantitṛṣṇāmohapramattā ye ratisaukhyāstathaiva ca |mohitāste
devagaṇā bhramiṣyanti bhave bhave

aharniśaṃ tṛṣṇā tāpayatikuṭṭanavyavahārā ye paricittāpahāriṇaḥ |avidyābahulā ye vā nityaṃ dāhābhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ
|na rātrau na divā teṣāṃ hṛdayaṃ suprasīdati

lobhābhibhūtāḥ tuṣāgnikalpā bhavantilobhābhibhūtamanasāṃ parivittābhikāṅkṣiṇām |teṣāṃ tuṣāgnikalpānāṃ
viśvasenna svabhāvataḥ

viṣayendhana sarpād bhayamevocitambibheti hi naraḥ sarvaḥ sarpādiva viṣendhanāt |lobhena viṣayeṇaivā'bhibhūtāste
narā bhṛśam

tṛṣṇāvaśagāḥ vividhāṃ yoniṃ labhantete mṛtā narakaṃ yānti pretayoniṃ tathaiva ca |tasmādapi
vinirmuktā narakād vahnisammukhāt

ākāṅkṣiṇaḥ nityaṃ duḥkhabhāgino bhavantipañcajanmaśatānyete bhavanti parikāṅkṣiṇaḥ |vivarṇā
dīnavadanā nityaṃ duḥkhasya bhāginaḥ |bhavanti manujāḥ sarve lobhopahatacetasaḥ

vivekasampannāḥ paramāṃ gatiṃ labhanteprahīṇalobhā ye santi nityaṃ jñānābhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ |buddhimantaḥ
sadā santaḥ te gatāḥ paramāṃ gatim |nirvāṇahṛdayā vītalobhamohāḥ sadā narāḥ

lobhākṛṣṭasya vinipātaḥlobhāśīviṣadaṣṭasya vinipāto dhruvaṃ sthitaḥ |kriyamāṇo dhruvaṃ loko
vardhate sa muhurmuhuḥ

śuṣkendhanaṃ samādāya yathā vahniḥ pravardhate |dhanatṛṣṇāratāḥ sattvāḥ dhanopārjanatatparāḥ

mṛtyukāle samutpanne tyajanti vivaśā dhanam |yacca tatsambhavaṃ yogāt tat sarva na vinaśyati

lobhātmā ghoraṃ narakaṃ yātitena vittena lobhātmā nīyate narakaṃ bhṛśam |anyaistadbhujyate vittaṃ
sa tu pāpena lipyate

prayānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ paścāttāpena dahyate |anartho hyartharūpeṇa sukharūpeṇa vā sukham

lobhatyāgaṃ prājñaḥ kuryātamitraṃ mitrarūpeṇa lobho'yaṃ hṛdi vartate |na lobhaṃ saṃśrayet prājño
lobhāgnirdahyate sadā

lobhadagdhāḥ narakagāmino bhavantitena dagdhā bhṛśaṃ sattvāḥ paścānnarakagāminaḥ |lokasādhāraṇā
hyete vibhavāḥ sukhavarjitāḥ

tṛṣṇābhayavimuktireva śreyaskarīteṣāmarthe kathaṃ pāpa kriyate mandabuddhibhiḥ |tṛṣṇābhayavimuktasya
nirāśasya hi sarvataḥ

vītakāṅkṣasya dhīrasya nityaṃ padamavasthitam |sampattau dhābate loko vipattau nāvabudhyate

sampattiḥ kṣayāntāvipadantā hi sampattiḥ kṣayāntaṃ divasaṃ yathā |yathā tiṣṭhati sampattiḥ vipattiḥ
pāpikā tathā

kāmabhogairdevā api narakaṃ yāntiavitṛptasya kāmebhyastṛṣṇayā paridahyate |yasyeṣṭāḥ sampado
nityaṃ sukhaṃ cābhimataṃ sadā

jani nāśayate tāsāṃ tṛṣṇā naṣṭā sukhāvahā |te devā narakaṃ yānti kāmabhogaistathārpitāḥ

analāceṣṭitaṃ sarva tad vadanti tathāgatāḥ |manuṣyā yacca narakaṃ prayā(nti) śataśastathā

vimugdhāḥ yati jīvanaṃ nāvagacchanticeṣṭitaṃ tad viśālāyāḥ yoṣikāyā vidurbudhāḥ |vimohitā na
vindanti tvaritaṃ svalpajīvitam |82||tṛṣṇayā sukṛtāni vinaśyantisukṛtāni ca naśyanti tṛṣṇā
naiva vinaśyati |bhave bhave gatā satvāḥ na vindanti śubhāśubham

śubhasya phalameveṣṭaṃ yat surāḥ paribhuñjate |aśubhasya tathā dṛṣṭamasukhaṃ vinipātajam

śubhāśubhaprahīṇā eva jarāmaraṇarahitā bhavantiśubhāśubhaprahīṇā ye saṅgadoṣavivarjitāḥ |te
gatāḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitam

tṛṣṇānadīparikṣiptaḥ janaḥ kimapi nāvagacchatipañcāraṃ bhavacakraṃ tat tṛṣṇānābhipuraḥsaram
|nadīrāgaparikṣiptaṃ na ca loko'vabudhyate

doṣāvartatarā jñeyā saṅkalpamakarākulāḥ |tṛṣṇānadī viśāleyaṃ na ca loko'vabudhyate

tṛṣṇā triṣu kāleṣu vañcikātrikāle vañcanī tṛṣṇā nityamajñānakāriṇī |na tasyāṃ viśvased dhīmān
saṃsārabandhanā hi sā

tṛṣṇā lokabandhanabhūtāmitravad dṛśyate kāle śatruvacca nikṛntati |na tasyāṃ viśvaset prājñaḥ
sā hi lokasya bandhanam

śakyaṃ hi bandhanaṃ chettumāyasaṃ dāruvattathā |na tṛṣṇābandhanaṃ chettuṃ nityaṃ kāmagaveṣibhiḥ

yasyeṣṭo bandhabhedo'yaṃ yasyeṣṭaṃ sukhamavyayam |sa tṛṣṇayā vimuktaḥ (syāt)prajñāśo (dhanakṛd)
bhavet

jñānena tṛṣṇāvṛkṣasya chedanaṃ karttavyamjñānālokaḥ sukhāloko duḥkhaṃ tṛṣṇātamaḥsmṛtam |tasmādālokamāsthāya
tamo nudati paṇḍitaḥ

jñānakhaḍgena tīkṣṇena tṛṣṇāvṛkṣaṃ nikṛntati |nikṛttavṛkṣaḥ (sa) naraḥ sukhaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam

doṣeṇa bahulā hyeṣā nadī prasravaṇākulā |doṣā netānahitvā ( na) bhavānmuñcati paṇḍitaḥ

prajñānāvamāśritya tṛṣṇānadyāḥ pāraṃ gacchatitṛṣṇānadīṃ tripathagāṃ pramādāvartadustarām |prajñānāvaṃ
samāśritya pāraṃ gacchantyanāmayam

mahecchatāmahacchatraṃ yena cāyānti bāliśāḥ |tasmānmahecchatā badhyā tvayeyaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā

mahecchaiva hṛdayavraṇabhūtāmahecchatāvraṇastīvro hṛdaye yasya jāyate |na rātrau na divā tasya
sukhaṃ bhavati lobhinaḥ

saṅkalpe tu na sambhūtaḥ tṛṣṇāvāyusamīritaḥ |mahecchatāmayo vahnirdahate hṛdayaṃ nṛṇām

lobhenātmano'hitaṃ kurvanti janāḥlobhenāveṣṭitamanāḥ puruṣo laghucetasā |jīvitānyapi sārāṇi
jahāti dhanatṛṣṇayā

pāpāni hi ca karmāṇi kurvanti puruṣāḥ kṣitau |dhanalābhena tat sarva pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ

ye sāhasaṃ na kurvanti viśanti jvalanaṃ ca yat |tat sarva lobhadoṣeṇa kurvantyahitamātmanaḥ

śastrānilāni duḥkhāni vividhāni ca (sarvataḥ) |viśanti (vai) narā mūḍhā lobhastatra hi kāraṇam

hṛdayastho manovahnirnityaṃ bahvicchatāṃ nṛṇām |alpecchatayā (hi) hṛcchāntirbhavatilābhinaḥ

yathāgnirindhanenaiva praśāntimadhigacchati |tathā vahvicchatāṃ nṛṇāṃ dhanairvṛddhiḥ prajāyate

alpecchataiva sukhambhūpālā (hi) dhanaistṛptāḥ koṭiśo nidhanaṃ gatāḥ |yāsyanti cānye nidhanaṃ
tasmādalpecchatā sukham

tṛṣṇāvihīna eva sukhamāpnotiduḥkhaṃ vahvicchatā nṛṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ |(heyā sarvaprayatnena
) idamuktaṃ parīkṣakaiḥ |eṣa panthāḥ śivaḥ śreṣṭho yena tṛṣṇā vaśīkṛtā

||iti tṛṣṇāvargo'ṣṭamaḥ ||

## 9 strījugupsāvargaḥ

(9) strījugupsāvargaḥstriya evānarthamūlamstriyo mūlaṃ (hi) pāpasya dhananāśasya sarvathā |svahite
ye na niratāḥ kutasteṣāṃ bhavet sukham

anarthakarmaratayaḥ śāṭhyerṣyābahulāstriyaḥ |lokadvayavināśāya puruṣāṇāmavasthitāḥ

nityaṃ sarāgakuśalā nityaṃ tadvacanāḥ parāḥ |anyacca hṛdaye tāsāṃ kathayantyanyadeva vā

āpātamadhurāḥ sūkṣmāḥ vipāke vajracetasaḥ |nopakāreṇa satkāraṃ smaranti laghucetasaḥ

striyo vidyutsvabhāvahṛdayā aticañcalāḥnāśayitvā priyaśataṃ smarantyekaṃ hi vipriyam |vidyutsvabhāvahṛdayāḥ
striyaḥ pāpasya bhūmayaḥ

strīṇāṃ nāśasādhakatvapradarśanamstrīhetunāśamicchanti puruṣā vakracetasaḥ |strīvināśo vināśo
drāgiha loke paratra ca

strīdarśanamevāgnivaddahatievaṃ tu sarvaviṣayāḥ strīdarśanamihaikajam |abhibhūya sarvaviṣayān
nāryagnijvalanaṃ mahat

strīdoṣapradarśanapūrvakaṃ nārījugupsāsamīkṣaṇamsaṃśleṣādapi viśleṣaḥ smaraṇāt kathanādapi |strīṇāṃ
dāhasamuttho'yaṃ vahnirantarjahāsakaḥ

rāgeṇa saha jāyante nityaṃ vai dāruṇāḥ striyaḥ |dānena saha jāyante yathā loke hutāśanāḥ

na bhavet tādṛśo dāho yo'yaṃ vahnisamudbhavaḥ |yādṛśaḥ strīmado hyasti dehināṃ hṛdayodbhavaḥ

sarvalokavināśāya sarvadharmakṣayāya ca |hetavo narakasyaitāḥ striyaḥ proktā maharṣibhiḥ

mukhato madhurāmarṣā hṛdayena viṣopamāḥ |anavasthitasauhārdā nāsāṃ kaścit priyo naraḥ

muhūrtena priyastāsāṃ muhūrtena tathā'priyaḥ |anavasthitasauhārdāścañcalā kṣaṇikopamāḥ

vañcanāhetukuśalā nityaṃ kāryaparāyaṇāḥ |nityaṃ saṃyogamanaso nityaṃ mānaparāyaṇāḥ

devānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ piśācoragarākṣasāḥ |na bandhabhūtā yādṛśyaḥ striyaḥ kālaviṣopamāḥ

nopakāraṃ smarantyetā na kuśalaṃ nāpi vikramam |anavasthitacittāśca vāyuvegasamāḥ striyaḥ

bhavanti sampado yatra rakṣyante yatra yoṣitaḥ |vyasaneṣu viraktāstu tyajanti puruṣaṃ dhruvam

yaṃ yaṃ gacchanti puruṣaṃ rakṣyante tatra yoṣitaḥ |śīghraṃ śīghraṃ naraṃ hyetāstyajanti puruṣaṃ
sthitam

yathā hi bhramarī puṣpaṃ śuṣkaṃ tyajati sarvadā |tathā vittena rahitaṃ puruṣaṃ tyajati priyā

nistriṃśahṛdayāḥ krūrāścañcalāstamasāvṛtāḥ |striyaḥ puruṣanāśāya jātāḥ kenāpi hetunā

devānāṃ bandhanaṃ nāma yathā strībandhanaṃ matam |strībandhananibaddhāstu patanti narakaṃ punaḥ

etadagraṃ hi rāgāṇāṃ yo rāgaḥ strīsamudbhavaḥ |strīrāgadagdhamanasaṃ paścād dahati pāvakaḥ

pratyakṣāṇyapi karmāṇi rāgaiścāpahataḥ pumān |na vetti mūḍhahṛdayaḥ strīrāgeṇa vimohitaḥ

viśvāsya viṣaye puruṣaṃ bad dhvā priyamanekaśaḥ |tyajanti vittanāśena tvacaṃ yadvadbhujaṅgamāḥ

sarvopāyabhṛtā nāryaḥ sarvaśaḥ paripālitāḥ |na śakyāḥ svavaśīkarttuṃ striyaḥ paramadāruṇāḥ

āsāṃ sarvasvabhāvānāṃ nārīṇāṃ calacetasām |na yāyājjātu viśvāsaṃ pumān dhīreṇa cetasā

strīvidheyāstu ye martyā nityaṃ kāmagaveṣiṇaḥ |paścimadarśanaṃ teṣāṃ suraloke bhaviṣyati

pañcāṅgikena tūryeṇa vipralubdhāḥ samantataḥ |vindanti vyasane duḥkhaṃ yadavaśyaṃ bhaviṣyati

imāstāścañcalā nāryo yāsāṃ rāgaḥ kṛtastvayā |tā bhavantaṃ parityajya punaranyaṃ tato gatāḥ

dvidhā hi prakṛtiryāsāṃ (yoṣitāṃ) sahacāriṇī |bhūyo'bhiyanti puruṣaṃ vyasaneṣu tyajanti ca

mṛgavanmohayantyetāḥ puruṣaṃ rāgamohitam |paścāt (tu) vyasane prāpte tyajanti laghucetasaḥ

nopakāraṃ na satkāraṃ na priyāṇi na santatim |smaranti yoṣitastīvrā vyasane samupasthite

mlānaṃ puṣpaṃ yathā tyaktvā bhramaro'nyatra dhāvati |tathā hi vyasane prāpte tyajanti khalu
yoṣitaḥ

anapekṣitasauhārdāścañcalāśca raṇapriyāḥ |bhavanti yoṣitaḥ sarvā viṣamiśraṃ yathā madhu

mohayanti narān kāmairvākyaiścāpi viśeṣataḥ |na teṣāṃ viśvaseddhīmān puruṣo dhīramānasaḥ

ābhirvimohitāḥ kiṃ vā raṃjitāḥ puruṣāḥ svataḥ |na kurvanti hitaṃ vākyaṃ yathā muṣṇikagāmikam

devāsuranarān yakṣān piśācoragarākṣasān |indrajālamayā nāryo vañcayanti viśeṣataḥ

etadagrañca pāśānāṃ yadidaṃ strīmayaṃ dṛḍham |anena baddhāḥ puruṣā bhramanti bhavacārake

na kaścit pāśapāśo'yaṃ hṛtpāśo yoṣitaḥ param |hṛtpāśabandhanairbaddhāḥ puruṣā duḥkhamohitāḥ

dahayate chidyate pāśasstrīmayastu na dahayate |naraka -preta-tiryakṣu gacchantamanugacchati

mūrtimān badhyate kāyaḥ pāśena mahatā tathā |amūrtigaṃ cittamidaṃ strīpāśena tu badhyate

(na) dṛśyate pāśamanyaṃ yena badhnanti yoṣitaḥ |abhijñeyamapramāṇaṃ strīmayaṃ bandhanaṃ mahat

anenāpātaramyeṇa (duḥkha) mokṣeṇa sarvadā |pāśena baddhāḥ puruṣā na mucyante bhavārṇavāt

ṣaḍindriyāṇi badhnāti pāśo yaḥ strīmayo mahān |pāśastu kāyamevaikaṃ kaścit badhnāti vā na vā

bandhanaṃ na dṛḍhaṃ hīdaṃ mokṣavārya ṣaḍāyasam |saṃraktacittabhoge hi mandabuddhernarasya ca

snāyuyantreṇa baddhāsu vistṛtakarmabhūmiṣu |tīkṣṇaraktāsinā yukto maraṇārthamihāgataḥ

strīṇāṃ daurguṇyamvañcanācchalakūṭāsu rabhasā nu viśeṣataḥ |cañcalo bhrāntacittāsu bhrāntastvaṅgakṣaṇāriṣu

vibhrānto bhrāntakathitairbhūṣaṇānāṃ tathā svanaiḥ |haranti puruṣaṃ kṣipraṃ vañcanākuśalāḥ striyaḥ

nānāvidheṣu puṣpeṣu yathā carati ṣaṭ padaḥ |nānāvidheṣu martyeṣu tathemāścañcalāḥ striyaḥ

yathā madhurikā pītvā puṣpamanyatra gacchati |tathā puruṣamāpīyaḥ prayānti rabhasaṃ striyaḥ

arthādāneṣu kuśalāḥ kruddhā nityaṃ durāsadāḥ |vañcayitvā naraṃ śīghramanyatra saṃprayānti tāḥ

sādhyamāyāpraharaṇāḥ kālakūṭaviṣopamāḥ |striyaḥ puruṣaghātinyaścāturyeṣu vyavasthitāḥ

vāyvākāśānalā yadvan na śaktā grathituṃ naraiḥ |tathopāyaśatairnāryo (na) śaktā rakṣituṃ naraiḥ

anarthavyādhimṛtyūnāṃ duṣkṛtānāñca karmaṇām |hetubhūtāḥ paraṃ nāryo mokṣacaryābadhasya ca

yadanekaprakāreṣu śaṭheṣu pāpakarmasu |(patanti ) manujā loke tatra hetuḥ parastriyaḥ

na bālayauvanenaiva vārdhakyenaiva śāmyati |cāpalyaṃ sahajaṃ strīṇāṃ bhāskarasya yathā prabhā

anityaṃ sauhṛdaṃ tāsāṃ dīptānāmarciṣāṃ kaṇaiḥ |vairañca śāśvataṃ tāsāmaśmanāṃ ca yathā vraṇaiḥ

dhanahīne virajyante dhanayuktā bhavanti ca |yāvadartha striyastāvadarthahīne kutaḥ striyaḥ?

na sevābhiścadānena nopakāraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ |svīkarttuṃ na striyaḥ śakyāḥ jvalanopamacetasaḥ

yathā naro'nukūlaśca chandakarttā yathā yathā |tathā tathā striyastasya vañcanākuśalāḥ param

puṣpacchatro yathā sarpo bhasmacchatro yathā nalaḥ |rūpacchannaṃ tathā cittamāsāṃ bhavati yoṣitām

viṣavṛkṣe yathā puṣpaṃ dṛṣṭiramyaṃ na śāntaye |viṣavṛkṣa (samā) nāryaḥ parivarjyāḥ samantataḥ

nārīṇāṃ darśanākāṃkṣī viṣayesu ca tatparaḥ |naro na sukhabhogāya (haya) smilloke na cāpare

nāgninā na ca śastreṇa na balena na jantubhiḥ |strīmayaṃ bandhanaṃ hetuḥ śakyate na durāsadam

doṣajālamidaṃ (sūtraṃ)strīmayaṃ carate bhuvi |yastrīvivarjito dhīmān asmilloke mahīyate

yathā bhūtairmanuṣyāṇāṃ svakarmotthāpane na ca |tathā striyo hi rakṣanti viṣamārtha tyajanti
ca

gatārthavibhavaṃ bhūyo varjitaṃ svena karmaṇā |devaṃ samīkṣya capalāḥ striyo naiva prabhāvikāḥ

strīsukhaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅguramkṣaṇabhaṅgamidaṃ saukhyamadhigatamavasthitam |catvāryetāni duḥkhāni
sevitāni nṛbhiḥ sadā

strī viṣāgnisadṛśātasmāt tāni vivarjyāni viṣāgnisadṛśāni hi |yaḥ kaściccapalo janturyaśca doṣodbhavaḥ
sadā

tasmāt parisamāviṣṭāḥ striyaḥ kāraṇavatsalāḥ |na sūryastamaso heturnāgniḥ śītasya kāraṇam

na strīṇāṃ sauhṛdaṃ citte svalpamapyavatiṣṭhate |yathā sthirā bhūmiriyaṃ yathā vāyuḥ sadā calaḥ

striyaḥ doṣameva smarantitathā strīṇāṃ kṛtaṃ nāsti doṣamayyaḥ sadā smṛtāḥ |ciraprayatnāḥ puruṣaiḥ
lobhitā bahuśaḥ striyaḥ

tyajanti vyasane prāpte śuṣkaṃ sara ivāśugāḥ |nordhvagāḥ sarito dṛṣṭā nāśmano gatiriṣyate

girīṇāṃ gamanaṃ nāsti strīṇāṃ naivāsti sauhṛdam |vapukarti sadā nṛṇāṃ dharmārthayaśasāṃ tathā

yoniścānarthajālasya doṣāṇāmudbhavasya ca |bhaved vajramayī mṛdvī tyajed (rātrau yathā) ravim

na strī tyajedasādhyāni janmāpekṣā viśeṣataḥ |nopacārakriyā dānapriyavākyāpalāpanaiḥ

svīkartuṃ na striyaḥ śakyā jvalanopamacetasaḥ |sukheṣu samatāṃ yānti vyasaneṣu tyajanti ca

upakārāṃśca vismṛtya doṣamekaṃ smaranti tāḥ |vanopavanaśaileṣu bhuktvā sukhamanekaśaḥ

strī lokasya bandhanamsaṃprāpte vyasane tīvre striya paribhavanti hi |lokasya bandhanaṃ nāryo
vañcanākṛtivardhikāḥ

adhamāḥ vinipātānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ cāspadaṃ (bahu) |sarvalokavināśāya vaśīkurvanti tṛṣṇayā

yathā strībandhanamidaṃ durviṣahyaṃ kṛtaṃ mahat ||yadidaṃ bandhanaṃ loke kāmarāgamayaṃ mahat

strīvarjanameva śreyaṣkaramtathā cānyaprayatnena vicārya upalabhyate |vaśīkurvanti tā (nāryaḥ)
kāmavāṇairanekaśaḥ

svalpaiḥ parājitaṃ kṛtvā kāmasya vaśagāḥ striyaḥ |rāgerṣyāśāṭhyabhūmīnāṃ vidyuccañcalacetasām

lobhāhaṅkārayonīnāṃ na viśvāsyāḥ kathañcana |śastrāgnisadṛśāstīkṣṇāḥ kāmapāśaparātmanaḥ

svabhraprapātaviṣamaṃ gaṃbhīrasamacetasām |parābhidrohamāyendrajālatadgatamānasān

akālamṛtyu-vajrāgni- kālakūṭasamātmanām |anekadoṣasambhārananditānāmanekaśaḥ

yadi śīlalavaḥ kaścit strīṇāṃ manasi vartate |cireṇa dahate vahniḥ sa spṛṣṭāḥ pavaneritaḥ

strīdarśanasamucchreyamātraṃ dahati pāvakaḥ |tasmāt striyo vivarjyāḥ syuḥ yadicchet sukhamuttamam

etat sarva paraṃ loke nārīṇāṃ varjanaṃ sadā |yadṛcchājālinīhetuṃ yaḥ icched bhūtimātmanaḥ

ya icchati nivṛttiṃ tu sa naraḥ strīṃ vivarjayet |sukhāsaktastathārtho ( ca) kusīdaścañcalaḥ
śaṭhaḥ

pāpasevī sumṛṣṭāśī naro bhadraṃ na paśyati |udyukto vīryavān dhīro dhārmikaḥ strīṃ vivarjayet
|dakṣo hetuphalaśraddho naraḥ kalyāṇavān bhavet

||iti strījugupsāvargo navamaḥ ||
